### `integrations/magpie/test_magpie_with_aiv.py`

Component G: proof against Magpie's real classifier. 101 lines, sha256 `5e1b825b4e38fc85`.

```python
# Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Proof that the AIV config rows work with Apache Magpie's real PR triage classifier.

Runs Magpie's own `pr_management` code (config loader + triage classifier) from a local
Magpie checkout against the snippet files in `magpie-config/`. Nothing is mocked on the
Magpie side. Skipped when no checkout is found; point MAGPIE_ROOT at one to run it:

    MAGPIE_ROOT=/path/to/apache-magpie python -m pytest integrations/magpie
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CONFIG_DIR = HERE / "magpie-config"
DEFAULT_ROOT = HERE.parents[2] / "magpie-review"
MAGPIE_ROOT = Path(os.environ.get("MAGPIE_ROOT", DEFAULT_ROOT))
PR_TOOL = MAGPIE_ROOT / "tools" / "pr-management"

pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(
    not (PR_TOOL / "src" / "pr_management").is_dir(),
    reason=f"no Apache Magpie checkout at {MAGPIE_ROOT} (set MAGPIE_ROOT)",
)

AIV = "AIV Integrity Gate"


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def magpie():
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PR_TOOL / "src"))
    from pr_management import config, model, people  # noqa: PLC0415
    from pr_management.triage import classify  # noqa: PLC0415

    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("magpie_test_helpers", PR_TOOL / "tests" / "helpers.py")
    helpers = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(helpers)
    cfg = config.load(MAGPIE_ROOT, CONFIG_DIR)

    def decide(**pr_kwargs):
        prs = [model.from_node(helpers.pr(**pr_kwargs))]
        prs[0].commits_behind = 0
        (d,) = classify.classify(
            prs, cfg, classify.Options(viewer="triager", now=helpers.NOW, authors="default", sweeps=False),
            people.Maintainers(), {}, set(),
        )
        return d

    return {"cfg": cfg, "decide": decide, "check": helpers.check, "pr": helpers.pr}


def test_snippet_rows_are_read_by_magpie(magpie):
    cfg = magpie["cfg"]
    assert "^AIV Integrity Gate$" in cfg.real_ci_patterns
    assert AIV in cfg.static_check_patterns
    rows = [c for c in cfg.check_map if c.pattern == AIV]
    assert rows and rows[0].url and rows[0].url.startswith("https://")


def test_aiv_failure_is_a_static_check_failure_not_a_rerun(magpie):
    check = magpie["check"]
    d = magpie["decide"](rollup="FAILURE", checks=[check("Tests (unit)"), check(AIV, "FAILURE")])
    assert d.row == "12", (d.row, d.reason)
    assert d.action == "comment"
    assert AIV in d.reason


def test_aiv_failure_alongside_a_test_failure_still_routes_to_a_code_fix(magpie):
    check = magpie["check"]
    d = magpie["decide"](rollup="FAILURE", checks=[check(AIV, "FAILURE"), check("Tests (unit)", "FAILURE")])
    assert d.row == "12b", (d.row, d.reason)
    assert d.action == "comment"


def test_green_aiv_counts_as_real_ci(magpie):
    check = magpie["check"]
    d = magpie["decide"](checks=[check("Mergeable"), check(AIV)])
    assert (d.row, d.classification, d.action) == ("20", "passing", "mark-ready")


def test_without_aiv_or_tests_a_green_rollup_is_not_passing(magpie):
    check = magpie["check"]
    d = magpie["decide"](checks=[check("Mergeable"), check("boring-cyborg")])
    assert d.classification != "passing"


def test_short_fragment_would_overmatch(magpie):
    """Documents why the snippet uses the full job name, using Magpie's own matcher."""
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PR_TOOL / "src"))
    from pr_management import ci, config  # noqa: PLC0415

    loose = config.Config(static_check_patterns=["aiv"])
    assert ci.is_static("naive-tests", loose)
    assert not ci.is_static("naive-tests", magpie["cfg"])
    assert ci.is_static(AIV, magpie["cfg"])
```

### `integrations/review-ledger/aiv_review_ledger.py`

Component H: ledger tool. 336 lines, sha256 `e90697ed2cc857be`.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""AIV review ledger: evidence for promoting or demoting deterministic rules.

Records, per pull request, what AIV found on each head commit and what AI reviewers said, then
reports which rules authors act on and where AI reviewers find things AIV does not. The report
only proposes; a maintainer decides whether to change `.aiv/config.yaml`.

Signals are proxies, stated as such in the report:
  * an AIV finding "acted on" = present on an earlier head of a merged PR, gone on its final head
  * an AIV finding "merged with" = still present on the final head of a merged PR
  * an AI comment "outdated" = GitHub marks it outdated (the commented line changed afterwards)
  * an AI comment "downvoted" = it carries a thumbs-down reaction

Standard library only. Subcommands:

  record-aiv   --ledger L --pr N --sha S --aiv-report aiv-report.json
  record-final --ledger L --pr N --sha S --merged true|false
  record-ai    --ledger L --pr N --comments review-comments.json [--ai-reviewers a,b]
  collect-ai   --ledger L --pr N --repo owner/name   (GitHub REST, token from GITHUB_TOKEN)
  report       --ledger L [--ledger L2 ...] [--format md|json] [--min-prs 10] [--promote-at 0.75]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import json
import math
import os
import re
import sys
import urllib.request
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable

SCHEMA = 1
DEFAULT_AI_REVIEWERS = (
    "copilot-pull-request-reviewer[bot]",
    "copilot",
    "coderabbitai[bot]",
    "gemini-code-assist[bot]",
    "claude[bot]",
)
_UNSAFE = re.compile("[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x1f\x7f\u200b-\u200f\u202a-\u202e\u2060-\u2064\u2066-\u2069\ufeff\U000e0000-\U000e007f]")


def _now() -> str:
    return dt.datetime.now(dt.UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")


def clean(text: Any, limit: int = 160) -> str:
    """One line of untrusted PR text: no control or invisible characters, no pipes, bounded."""
    s = _UNSAFE.sub("", str(text or "")).replace("\r", " ").replace("\n", " ").replace("|", "/").strip()
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s)
    return s if len(s) <= limit else s[: limit - 3] + "..."


def append(ledger: Path, row: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    ledger.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with ledger.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        fh.write(json.dumps({"schema": SCHEMA, "recorded_at": _now(), **row}, sort_keys=True) + "\n")


def read_rows(paths: Iterable[Path]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for p in paths:
        files = sorted(p.glob("*.jsonl")) if p.is_dir() else [p]
        for f in files:
            for n, line in enumerate(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), 1):
                if not line.strip():
                    continue
                try:
                    row = json.loads(line)
                except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                    raise SystemExit(f"{f}:{n}: not JSON ({e.msg})") from e
                if row.get("schema") != SCHEMA:
                    raise SystemExit(f"{f}:{n}: unsupported schema {row.get('schema')!r}")
                rows.append(row)
    return rows


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ recording


def aiv_findings(report: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    """Blocking and advisory findings from an AIV schema v3 JSON report. Passed-gate notes are skipped."""
    if report.get("schema_version") != 3:
        raise SystemExit(f"AIV report schema_version must be 3, got {report.get('schema_version')!r}")
    out = []
    for g in report.get("gates", []):
        part = g.get("partition")
        if part not in ("blocking", "advisory"):
            continue
        findings = g.get("findings") or [{"rule_id": f"{g.get('id')}.gate", "file": "(run)"}]
        for f in findings:
            out.append({"rule": str(f.get("rule_id")), "partition": part, "file": str(f.get("file", ""))})
    return out


def ai_comments(comments: list[dict[str, Any]], reviewers: Iterable[str]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """AI reviewer comments from the GitHub `pulls/{n}/comments` REST payload."""
    wanted = {r.lower() for r in reviewers}
    out = []
    for c in comments:
        login = ((c.get("user") or {}).get("login") or "").lower()
        if login not in wanted:
            continue
        reactions = c.get("reactions") or {}
        out.append({
            "id": c.get("id"),
            "reviewer": login,
            "path": str(c.get("path") or ""),
            "outdated": c.get("position") is None and c.get("line") is None,
            "downvoted": int(reactions.get("-1") or 0) > 0,
            "upvoted": int(reactions.get("+1") or 0) > 0,
            "head": clean((c.get("body") or "").strip().split("\n", 1)[0]),
        })
    return out


def collect_comments(repo: str, pr: int, token: str, api: str = "https://api.github.com") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[\w.-]+/[\w.-]+", repo):
        raise SystemExit("--repo must be owner/name")
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    page = 1
    while page <= 30:
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            f"{api}/repos/{repo}/pulls/{int(pr)}/comments?per_page=100&page={page}",
            headers={"Accept": "application/vnd.github+json", "Authorization": f"Bearer {token}",
                     "X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28"},
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:  # noqa: S310 - fixed https API base
            batch = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
        out.extend(batch)
        if len(batch) < 100:
            break
        page += 1
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ reporting


def wilson(successes: int, n: int, z: float = 1.959964) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """95% Wilson score interval for a proportion; (0, 1) when n is 0."""
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, 1.0
    p = successes / n
    denom = 1 + z * z / n
    centre = (p + z * z / (2 * n)) / denom
    half = z * math.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / n + z * z / (4 * n * n)) / denom
    return max(0.0, centre - half), min(1.0, centre + half)


def analyse(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], min_prs: int = 10, promote_at: float = 0.75,
            demote_below: float = 0.2) -> dict[str, Any]:
    runs: dict[int, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    finals: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    comments: dict[int, dict[Any, dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(dict)
    for r in rows:
        pr = int(r["pr"])
        if r["kind"] == "aiv-run":
            runs[pr].append(r)
        elif r["kind"] == "final":
            finals[pr] = r
        elif r["kind"] == "ai-comments":
            for c in r["comments"]:
                comments[pr][c["id"]] = c  # newest record of a comment wins
    rules: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = defaultdict(lambda: {"partition": set(), "seen": 0, "acted_on": 0, "merged_with": 0})
    unresolved_finals = 0
    for pr, final in finals.items():
        if not final.get("merged"):
            continue
        pr_runs = sorted(runs.get(pr, []), key=lambda x: x["recorded_at"])
        final_runs = [x for x in pr_runs if x["sha"] == final["sha"]]
        if not final_runs:
            unresolved_finals += 1
            continue
        last = {f["rule"] for f in final_runs[-1]["findings"]}
        ever: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        for x in pr_runs:
            for f in x["findings"]:
                ever[f["rule"]].add(f["partition"])
        for rule, parts in ever.items():
            s = rules[rule]
            s["partition"] |= parts
            s["seen"] += 1
            s["acted_on" if rule not in last else "merged_with"] += 1
    rule_rows = []
    for rule, s in sorted(rules.items()):
        n = s["acted_on"] + s["merged_with"]
        lo, hi = wilson(s["acted_on"], n)
        parts = sorted(s["partition"])
        proposal = ""
        if "blocking" in parts and s["merged_with"]:
            proposal = "investigate: merged while still blocking (bypass, override or rule turned off)"
        elif parts == ["advisory"] and n >= min_prs and lo >= promote_at:
            proposal = "candidate: promote to fail (authors almost always fix it)"
        elif parts == ["advisory"] and n >= min_prs and hi < demote_below:
            proposal = "candidate: turn off or narrow (authors rarely act on it)"
        rule_rows.append({"rule": rule, "partitions": parts, "merged_prs": n, "acted_on": s["acted_on"],
                          "merged_with": s["merged_with"], "acted_on_low95": round(lo, 3),
                          "acted_on_high95": round(hi, 3), "proposal": proposal})
    reviewers: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(lambda: {"comments": 0, "outdated": 0, "downvoted": 0, "upvoted": 0})
    beyond_aiv = []
    for pr, by_id in comments.items():
        aiv_files = {f["file"] for x in runs.get(pr, []) for f in x["findings"]}
        for c in by_id.values():
            s = reviewers[c["reviewer"]]
            s["comments"] += 1
            s["outdated"] += bool(c["outdated"])
            s["downvoted"] += bool(c["downvoted"])
            s["upvoted"] += bool(c["upvoted"])
            if c["outdated"] and not c["downvoted"] and c["path"] not in aiv_files:
                beyond_aiv.append({"pr": pr, "reviewer": c["reviewer"], "path": clean(c["path"], 120),
                                   "head": clean(c["head"])})
    rev_rows = []
    for name, s in sorted(reviewers.items()):
        lo, hi = wilson(s["outdated"], s["comments"])
        rev_rows.append({"reviewer": name, **s, "outdated_low95": round(lo, 3), "outdated_high95": round(hi, 3)})
    return {
        "schema": SCHEMA,
        "prs_with_runs": len(runs),
        "merged_prs": sum(1 for f in finals.values() if f.get("merged")),
        "merged_prs_without_final_run": unresolved_finals,
        "rules": rule_rows,
        "ai_reviewers": rev_rows,
        "ai_findings_beyond_aiv": beyond_aiv[:50],
        "ai_findings_beyond_aiv_total": len(beyond_aiv),
        "thresholds": {"min_prs": min_prs, "promote_at": promote_at, "demote_below": demote_below},
    }


def to_markdown(a: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    out = ["# AIV review ledger report", "",
           f"PRs with AIV runs: {a['prs_with_runs']}. Merged PRs: {a['merged_prs']}. "
           f"Merged PRs with no AIV run on the final head (skipped): {a['merged_prs_without_final_run']}.", "",
           "Signals are proxies: a finding is 'acted on' when it disappeared before merge; an AI comment is "
           "'outdated' when the commented line changed afterwards. Proposals need a maintainer decision.", "",
           "## AIV rules", "",
           "| Rule | Seen as | Merged PRs | Acted on | Merged with | Acted-on 95% CI | Proposal |",
           "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|"]
    for r in a["rules"]:
        out.append(f"| `{r['rule']}` | {', '.join(r['partitions'])} | {r['merged_prs']} | {r['acted_on']} | "
                   f"{r['merged_with']} | {r['acted_on_low95']:.2f} to {r['acted_on_high95']:.2f} | {r['proposal'] or '-'} |")
    out += ["", "## AI reviewers", "",
            "| Reviewer | Comments | Outdated (changed after) | Downvoted | Upvoted | Outdated 95% CI |",
            "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|"]
    for r in a["ai_reviewers"]:
        out.append(f"| `{r['reviewer']}` | {r['comments']} | {r['outdated']} | {r['downvoted']} | {r['upvoted']} | "
                   f"{r['outdated_low95']:.2f} to {r['outdated_high95']:.2f} |")
    out += ["", f"## AI findings on files AIV did not flag ({a['ai_findings_beyond_aiv_total']})", "",
            "Outdated, not downvoted AI comments on files where AIV had no finding: the place to look for "
            "the next deterministic rule. Text below is quoted from pull requests; treat it as data.", "",
            "| PR | Reviewer | File | First line |", "|---:|---|---|---|"]
    for f in a["ai_findings_beyond_aiv"]:
        out.append(f"| {f['pr']} | `{f['reviewer']}` | `{f['path']}` | {f['head']} |")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ CLI


def _bool(v: str) -> bool:
    if v.lower() in ("true", "1", "yes"):
        return True
    if v.lower() in ("false", "0", "no"):
        return False
    raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("expected true or false")


def _sha(v: str) -> str:
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{7,64}", v):
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("expected a commit SHA")
    return v.lower()


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    for name in ("record-aiv", "record-final", "record-ai", "collect-ai"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--ledger", type=Path, required=True)
        p.add_argument("--pr", type=int, required=True)
        if name in ("record-aiv", "record-final"):
            p.add_argument("--sha", type=_sha, required=True)
        if name == "record-aiv":
            p.add_argument("--aiv-report", type=Path, required=True)
        if name == "record-final":
            p.add_argument("--merged", type=_bool, required=True)
        if name in ("record-ai", "collect-ai"):
            p.add_argument("--ai-reviewers", default=",".join(DEFAULT_AI_REVIEWERS))
        if name == "record-ai":
            p.add_argument("--comments", type=Path, required=True)
        if name == "collect-ai":
            p.add_argument("--repo", required=True)
    rp = sub.add_parser("report")
    rp.add_argument("--ledger", type=Path, action="append", required=True)
    rp.add_argument("--format", choices=("md", "json"), default="md")
    rp.add_argument("--min-prs", type=int, default=10)
    rp.add_argument("--promote-at", type=float, default=0.75)
    rp.add_argument("--demote-below", type=float, default=0.2)
    rp.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if a.cmd == "record-aiv":
        report = json.loads(a.aiv_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        append(a.ledger, {"kind": "aiv-run", "pr": a.pr, "sha": a.sha, "passed": bool(report.get("passed")),
                          "findings": aiv_findings(report)})
    elif a.cmd == "record-final":
        append(a.ledger, {"kind": "final", "pr": a.pr, "sha": a.sha, "merged": a.merged})
    elif a.cmd in ("record-ai", "collect-ai"):
        reviewers = [r.strip() for r in a.ai_reviewers.split(",") if r.strip()]
        if a.cmd == "record-ai":
            raw = json.loads(a.comments.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        else:
            token = os.environ.get("GITHUB_TOKEN", "")
            if not token:
                raise SystemExit("GITHUB_TOKEN is not set")
            raw = collect_comments(a.repo, a.pr, token)
        append(a.ledger, {"kind": "ai-comments", "pr": a.pr, "comments": ai_comments(raw, reviewers)})
    else:
        result = analyse(read_rows(a.ledger), a.min_prs, a.promote_at, a.demote_below)
        text = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n" if a.format == "json" else to_markdown(result)
        if a.output:
            a.output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        else:
            sys.stdout.write(text)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

### `integrations/review-ledger/test_aiv_review_ledger.py`

Component H: ledger tests. 195 lines, sha256 `75058393014d1bc8`.

```python
# Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("aiv_review_ledger", Path(__file__).with_name("aiv_review_ledger.py"))
L = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(L)

A, B, C = "a" * 40, "b" * 40, "c" * 40


def report(*findings: tuple[str, str, str], passed: bool = True) -> dict:
    gates = {}
    for rule, part, file in findings:
        gid = rule.split(".")[0]
        g = gates.setdefault((gid, part), {"id": gid, "partition": part, "findings": []})
        g["findings"].append({"rule_id": rule, "file": file})
    return {"schema_version": 3, "passed": passed,
            "gates": [{"id": "review-routing", "partition": "pass", "findings": [{"rule_id": "review-routing.tier"}]},
                      *gates.values()]}


def write(tmp: Path, name: str, obj) -> Path:
    p = tmp / name
    p.write_text(json.dumps(obj), encoding="utf-8")
    return p


def test_findings_extraction_skips_passed_gates_and_requires_schema_3():
    r = report(("invariant.placeholder", "advisory", "a.py"), ("syntax.parse", "blocking", "b.py"))
    r["gates"].append({"id": "design", "partition": "advisory", "findings": []})
    got = L.aiv_findings(r)
    assert {"rule": "invariant.placeholder", "partition": "advisory", "file": "a.py"} in got
    assert {"rule": "syntax.parse", "partition": "blocking", "file": "b.py"} in got
    assert {"rule": "design.gate", "partition": "advisory", "file": "(run)"} in got
    assert all(f["rule"] != "review-routing.tier" for f in got)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        L.aiv_findings({"schema_version": 2})


def test_ai_comment_extraction_and_sanitising():
    raw = [
        {"id": 1, "user": {"login": "copilot-pull-request-reviewer[bot]"}, "path": "a.py", "position": None,
         "line": None, "reactions": {"-1": 0, "+1": 2}, "body": "Possible None deref\nmore"},
        {"id": 2, "user": {"login": "CodeRabbitAI[bot]"}, "path": "b.py", "position": 3, "line": 10,
         "reactions": {"-1": 1}, "body": "x |y\u200b" + "z" * 300},
        {"id": 3, "user": {"login": "maintainer"}, "path": "c.py", "body": "human"},
        {"id": 4, "user": None, "body": "ghost"},
    ]
    got = L.ai_comments(raw, L.DEFAULT_AI_REVIEWERS)
    assert [c["id"] for c in got] == [1, 2]
    assert got[0]["outdated"] and got[0]["upvoted"] and not got[0]["downvoted"]
    assert got[0]["head"] == "Possible None deref"
    assert not got[1]["outdated"] and got[1]["downvoted"]
    assert "|" not in got[1]["head"] and "\u200b" not in got[1]["head"] and got[1]["head"].endswith("...")


def test_wilson_bounds():
    assert L.wilson(0, 0) == (0.0, 1.0)
    lo, hi = L.wilson(10, 10)
    assert 0.69 < lo < 0.73 and hi == pytest.approx(1.0)
    lo, hi = L.wilson(0, 20)
    assert lo == 0.0 and 0.15 < hi < 0.17


def _ledger(tmp: Path) -> Path:
    ledger = tmp / "ledger.jsonl"
    # 12 merged PRs where an advisory placeholder appeared then was fixed: promotion candidate.
    for pr in range(1, 13):
        L.main(["record-aiv", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", str(pr), "--sha", A,
                "--aiv-report", str(write(tmp, f"r{pr}a.json", report(("invariant.placeholder", "advisory", "a.py"))))])
        L.main(["record-aiv", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", str(pr), "--sha", B,
                "--aiv-report", str(write(tmp, f"r{pr}b.json", report()))])
        L.main(["record-final", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", str(pr), "--sha", B, "--merged", "true"])
    # 20 merged PRs that kept an advisory density note: demotion candidate. (With 0 of 12 the 95% upper
    # bound is still about 0.24, above the 0.2 threshold, so 12 would honestly not be enough evidence.)
    for pr in range(100, 120):
        L.main(["record-aiv", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", str(pr), "--sha", C,
                "--aiv-report", str(write(tmp, f"d{pr}.json", report(("density.low", "advisory", "d.py"))))])
        L.main(["record-final", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", str(pr), "--sha", C, "--merged", "yes"])
    # A PR merged while a blocking rule was present, one closed unmerged, one merged with no final run.
    L.main(["record-aiv", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "40", "--sha", A,
            "--aiv-report", str(write(tmp, "x.json", report(("invariant.merge-conflict", "blocking", "x.py"), passed=False)))])
    L.main(["record-final", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "40", "--sha", A, "--merged", "1"])
    L.main(["record-aiv", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "41", "--sha", A,
            "--aiv-report", str(write(tmp, "y.json", report(("syntax.parse", "blocking", "y.py"), passed=False)))])
    L.main(["record-final", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "41", "--sha", A, "--merged", "false"])
    L.main(["record-final", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "42", "--sha", C, "--merged", "true"])
    # AI comments on PR 1: one outdated on a file AIV flagged, one outdated elsewhere, one downvoted.
    comments = [
        {"id": 7, "user": {"login": "copilot-pull-request-reviewer[bot]"}, "path": "a.py", "position": None, "line": None},
        {"id": 8, "user": {"login": "copilot-pull-request-reviewer[bot]"}, "path": "z.py", "position": None,
         "line": None, "body": "Off-by-one in the loop bound"},
        {"id": 9, "user": {"login": "copilot-pull-request-reviewer[bot]"}, "path": "q.py", "position": None,
         "line": None, "reactions": {"-1": 1}, "body": "wrong"},
    ]
    L.main(["record-ai", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "1", "--comments", str(write(tmp, "c.json", comments))])
    L.main(["record-ai", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "1", "--comments", str(write(tmp, "c.json", comments))])
    return ledger


def test_report_proposes_promotion_demotion_and_flags_bypass(tmp_path, capsys):
    ledger = _ledger(tmp_path)
    a = L.analyse(L.read_rows([ledger]))
    by = {r["rule"]: r for r in a["rules"]}
    assert by["invariant.placeholder"]["acted_on"] == 12
    assert by["invariant.placeholder"]["proposal"].startswith("candidate: promote")
    assert by["density.low"]["merged_with"] == 20
    assert by["density.low"]["proposal"].startswith("candidate: turn off")
    twelve = L.analyse([r for r in L.read_rows([ledger]) if not (r["pr"] >= 112 and r["pr"] < 120)])
    assert {r["rule"]: r for r in twelve["rules"]}["density.low"]["proposal"] == "", "0/12 is not enough evidence"
    assert by["invariant.merge-conflict"]["proposal"].startswith("investigate")
    assert "syntax.parse" not in by, "closed unmerged PRs are not evidence"
    assert a["merged_prs"] == 34 and a["merged_prs_without_final_run"] == 1
    (rev,) = a["ai_reviewers"]
    assert rev["comments"] == 3, "re-recorded comments are de-duplicated by id"
    assert (rev["outdated"], rev["downvoted"]) == (3, 1)
    assert a["ai_findings_beyond_aiv_total"] == 1
    assert a["ai_findings_beyond_aiv"][0]["path"] == "z.py"

    small = L.analyse(L.read_rows([ledger]), min_prs=50)
    assert all(r["proposal"] == "" or r["proposal"].startswith("investigate") for r in small["rules"])

    assert L.main(["report", "--ledger", str(ledger)]) == 0
    md = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert md.startswith("# AIV review ledger report")
    assert "| `invariant.placeholder` | advisory | 12 | 12 | 0 |" in md
    assert "Off-by-one in the loop bound" in md
    assert "\u2014" not in md and "\u2013" not in md
    out = tmp_path / "r.json"
    L.main(["report", "--ledger", str(tmp_path), "--format", "json", "--output", str(out)])
    assert json.loads(out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["merged_prs"] == 34


def test_bad_inputs_fail_loudly(tmp_path):
    bad = tmp_path / "bad.jsonl"
    bad.write_text('{"schema": 1, "kind": "final"}\nnot json\n', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        L.read_rows([bad])
    bad.write_text('\n{"schema": 9}\n', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        L.read_rows([bad])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        L.main(["record-final", "--ledger", str(bad), "--pr", "1", "--sha", "not-a-sha", "--merged", "true"])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        L.main(["record-final", "--ledger", str(bad), "--pr", "1", "--sha", A, "--merged", "maybe"])


def test_collect_ai_uses_github_rest_with_pagination(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    pages = {1: [{"id": i, "user": {"login": "coderabbitai[bot]"}, "path": "a.py", "position": 1, "line": 1}
                 for i in range(100)],
             2: [{"id": 500, "user": {"login": "coderabbitai[bot]"}, "path": "b.py", "position": None, "line": None}]}
    seen = []

    class Resp:
        def __init__(self, body):
            self.body = body

        def read(self):
            return self.body

        def __enter__(self):
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *a):
            return False

    def fake_urlopen(req, timeout):
        seen.append((req.full_url, req.headers.get("Authorization")))
        page = int(req.full_url.rsplit("page=", 1)[1])
        return Resp(json.dumps(pages[page]).encode())

    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", fake_urlopen)
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_TOKEN", "tok")
    ledger = tmp_path / "l.jsonl"
    L.main(["collect-ai", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "5", "--repo", "apache/airflow"])
    row = json.loads(ledger.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert len(row["comments"]) == 101
    assert seen[0] == ("https://api.github.com/repos/apache/airflow/pulls/5/comments?per_page=100&page=1", "Bearer tok")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        L.main(["collect-ai", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "5", "--repo", "bad repo"])
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_TOKEN")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        L.main(["collect-ai", "--ledger", str(ledger), "--pr", "5", "--repo", "apache/airflow"])


def test_clean_handles_none():
    assert L.clean(None) == ""
```

### `integrations/review-ledger/README.md`

Component H: ledger guide. 58 lines, sha256 `20bf3ddb21dfcba2`.

````markdown
# AIV review ledger

A small, standard-library Python tool that turns real review outcomes into evidence for changing
AIV policy. It records what AIV found on each PR head and what AI reviewers commented, then reports:

- **Rules authors act on.** An advisory rule whose findings are almost always fixed before merge is a
  candidate to promote to `fail`. One whose findings are almost never acted on is a candidate to turn
  off or narrow.
- **Rules that did not hold.** A blocking rule still present on the final head of a merged PR means a
  bypass, an override or a rule that was switched off. Investigate.
- **What AI reviewers find that AIV does not.** AI comments that were acted on (GitHub marks them
  outdated because the line changed) on files where AIV had no finding. This is where to look for the
  next deterministic rule. A human writes that rule and its tests.

Proposals use 95% Wilson intervals and a minimum sample (`--min-prs`, default 10), so a handful of PRs
never triggers a policy change. The report proposes; a maintainer decides and edits `.aiv/config.yaml`.

## Signals, and what they are not

| Signal | Meaning | Limitation |
|---|---|---|
| acted on | finding present on an earlier head of a merged PR, absent on the final head | the author may have removed the code for another reason |
| merged with | finding still present on the final head of a merged PR | needs an AIV run recorded on the final head; PRs without one are counted and skipped |
| outdated | GitHub marks the AI comment outdated (the line changed later) | a line can change without addressing the comment |
| downvoted / upvoted | thumbs reactions on the AI comment | most comments get no reaction |

Closed, unmerged PRs are not used as evidence.

## Usage

```bash
L=ledger/aiv-review-ledger.jsonl
# after each AIV run on a PR head
python aiv_review_ledger.py record-aiv   --ledger $L --pr 123 --sha $HEAD_SHA --aiv-report aiv-report.json
# when the PR closes
python aiv_review_ledger.py collect-ai   --ledger $L --pr 123 --repo owner/name   # needs GITHUB_TOKEN
python aiv_review_ledger.py record-final --ledger $L --pr 123 --sha $HEAD_SHA --merged true
# any time
python aiv_review_ledger.py report --ledger ledger/ --format md > ledger-report.md
```

`--ledger` for `report` accepts files or directories of `*.jsonl` and can repeat, so per-PR artifacts
can be downloaded into one folder and reported together. `record-ai` reads a saved
`pulls/{n}/comments` payload instead of calling GitHub. AI reviewer logins default to Copilot,
CodeRabbit, Gemini Code Assist and Claude; override with `--ai-reviewers`.

The example workflow `review-ledger-example.yml` records one row file per event and uploads it as an
artifact. Where to keep the ledger long term (an artifact store, a data branch, object storage) is the
adopter's choice; the tool only appends lines.

Text quoted from pull requests is sanitised (control, zero-width, bidi and tag characters removed,
pipes replaced, length bounded) and labelled as data in the report.

## Tests

```bash
python -m pytest integrations/review-ledger
```
````

### `integrations/review-ledger/review-ledger-example.yml`

Component H: example workflow (inactive). 79 lines, sha256 `d83629198d4b130f`.

```yaml
# Example only: copy to .github/workflows/ and adapt. Not active in this repository.
#
# Records AIV review-ledger rows as artifacts:
#   * on every AIV Gate completion for a PR: what AIV found on that head (needs the AIV job to
#     upload its JSON report as artifact "aiv-report", see the step below)
#   * when the PR closes: the AI reviewer comments and the final head / merged state
# Download the "aiv-ledger-*" artifacts into one folder and run
#   python integrations/review-ledger/aiv_review_ledger.py report --ledger <folder>
#
# Add this step to the AIV job so the report is available:
#   - uses: actions/upload-artifact@ea165f8d65b6e75b540449e92b4886f43607fa02 # v4
#     if: always()
#     with: { name: aiv-report, path: aiv-report.json, if-no-files-found: ignore }
#
# Security: runs on workflow_run / pull_request_target with read-only permissions and never checks
# out PR code. The ledger tool comes from the default branch.
name: AIV review ledger

on:
  workflow_run:
    workflows: ["AIV Gate"]
    types: [completed]
  pull_request_target:
    types: [closed]

permissions:
  contents: read
  pull-requests: read
  actions: read

jobs:
  record:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 5
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@34e114876b0b11c390a56381ad16ebd13914f8d5
        with:
          ref: ${{ github.event.repository.default_branch }}
          sparse-checkout: integrations/review-ledger
          persist-credentials: false

      - name: Fetch the AIV report of this run
        if: github.event_name == 'workflow_run' && github.event.workflow_run.event == 'pull_request'
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4 # pin to a commit SHA in your repository
        with:
          name: aiv-report
          run-id: ${{ github.event.workflow_run.id }}
          github-token: ${{ github.token }}
        continue-on-error: true

      - name: Record AIV findings for the PR head
        if: github.event_name == 'workflow_run' && github.event.workflow_run.event == 'pull_request'
        env:
          PR: ${{ github.event.workflow_run.pull_requests[0].number }}
          SHA: ${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_sha }}
        run: |
          if [ -z "$PR" ] || [ ! -f aiv-report.json ]; then echo "no PR number or report; nothing to record"; exit 0; fi
          python integrations/review-ledger/aiv_review_ledger.py record-aiv \
            --ledger "ledger/aiv-$PR-$SHA.jsonl" --pr "$PR" --sha "$SHA" --aiv-report aiv-report.json

      - name: Record AI comments and the final state
        if: github.event_name == 'pull_request_target'
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          PR: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
          SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}
          MERGED: ${{ github.event.pull_request.merged }}
        run: |
          python integrations/review-ledger/aiv_review_ledger.py collect-ai \
            --ledger "ledger/final-$PR.jsonl" --pr "$PR" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY"
          python integrations/review-ledger/aiv_review_ledger.py record-final \
            --ledger "ledger/final-$PR.jsonl" --pr "$PR" --sha "$SHA" --merged "$MERGED"

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@ea165f8d65b6e75b540449e92b4886f43607fa02 # v4
        with:
          name: aiv-ledger-${{ github.run_id }}
          path: ledger/
          if-no-files-found: ignore
          retention-days: 90
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/Glob.java`

Component J: strict globs. 61 lines, sha256 `f9c8c6dfd88bd539`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Strict path globs for file classification. {@code **&#47;} matches zero or more directories,
 * {@code **} matches anything, {@code *} and {@code ?} stay inside one path segment. A pattern without
 * a slash matches the file name in any directory. Unlike {@code PathFilter}, there is no substring
 * fallback, so {@code docs/**} never matches {@code src/docs_util.py}.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
final class Glob {

    private Glob() {
    }

    static Pattern compile(String glob) {
        String g = glob.trim().replace('\\', '/');
        StringBuilder re = new StringBuilder(g.contains("/") ? "" : "(?:.*/)?");
        for (int i = 0; i < g.length(); i++) {
            char c = g.charAt(i);
            if (c == '*' && i + 1 < g.length() && g.charAt(i + 1) == '*') {
                boolean slash = i + 2 < g.length() && g.charAt(i + 2) == '/';
                re.append(slash ? "(?:.*/)?" : ".*");
                i += slash ? 2 : 1;
            } else if (c == '*') {
                re.append("[^/]*");
            } else if (c == '?') {
                re.append("[^/]");
            } else {
                re.append(Pattern.quote(String.valueOf(c)));
            }
        }
        return Pattern.compile(re.toString());
    }

    static List<Pattern> compileAll(List<String> globs) {
        List<Pattern> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String g : globs) {
            out.add(compile(g));
        }
        return out;
    }

    static boolean any(String path, List<Pattern> patterns) {
        for (Pattern p : patterns) {
            if (p.matcher(path).matches()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/DiffStats.java`

Component J: per-file added and removed lines. 62 lines, sha256 `c4f7cac75d04ef21`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Splits a unified {@code git diff} into added and removed lines per file. Only lines inside hunks
 * count, so {@code ---}/{@code +++} headers are never mistaken for content.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
final class DiffStats {

    record FileDiff(List<String> added, List<String> removed) {
        static final FileDiff EMPTY = new FileDiff(List.of(), List.of());

        int changed() {
            return added.size() + removed.size();
        }

        /** True when the file changed but every change disappears once whitespace is ignored. */
        boolean whitespaceOnly() {
            return changed() > 0 && squash(added).equals(squash(removed));
        }

        private static String squash(List<String> lines) {
            return String.join("", lines).replaceAll("\\s+", "");
        }
    }

    private DiffStats() {
    }

    static Map<String, FileDiff> parse(String raw) {
        Map<String, FileDiff> out = new HashMap<>();
        FileDiff current = null;
        boolean inHunk = false;
        for (String line : raw.split("\n", -1)) {
            String l = line.endsWith("\r") ? line.substring(0, line.length() - 1) : line;
            if (l.startsWith("diff --git ")) {
                int b = l.lastIndexOf(" b/");
                current = new FileDiff(new ArrayList<>(), new ArrayList<>());
                out.put(b < 0 ? l.substring(11) : l.substring(b + 3), current);
                inHunk = false;
            } else if (current != null && l.startsWith("@@")) {
                inHunk = true;
            } else if (inHunk && l.startsWith("+")) {
                current.added().add(l.substring(1));
            } else if (inHunk && l.startsWith("-")) {
                current.removed().add(l.substring(1));
            }
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/Classifier.java`

Component J: file categories and areas. 102 lines, sha256 `cd30c74624053858`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Assigns each changed path one category and one area. Project globs for a category are checked
 * before the built-in globs; categories are tried in {@link #CATEGORIES} order and the first match
 * wins. Areas come from the configured map in order, then the first {@code areaDepth} directories.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
final class Classifier {

    static final String SOURCE = "source";

    /** Category order matters: lockfiles before config, tests before docs, docs before config. */
    static final Map<String, List<String>> DEFAULTS = defaults();
    static final Set<String> CATEGORIES = categories();
    static final List<String> DEFAULT_NON_REVIEWABLE = List.of("lockfile", "generated", "vendored", "binary");

    private final Map<String, List<Pattern>> categories = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, List<Pattern>> areas = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final int areaDepth;

    Classifier(Map<String, List<String>> projectCategories, Map<String, List<String>> areaGlobs, int areaDepth) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, List<String>> e : DEFAULTS.entrySet()) {
            List<String> globs = new ArrayList<>(projectCategories.getOrDefault(e.getKey(), List.of()));
            globs.addAll(e.getValue());
            categories.put(e.getKey(), Glob.compileAll(globs));
        }
        areaGlobs.forEach((name, globs) -> areas.put(name, Glob.compileAll(globs)));
        this.areaDepth = areaDepth;
    }

    String category(String path) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, List<Pattern>> e : categories.entrySet()) {
            if (Glob.any(path, e.getValue())) {
                return e.getKey();
            }
        }
        return SOURCE;
    }

    String area(String path) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, List<Pattern>> e : areas.entrySet()) {
            if (Glob.any(path, e.getValue())) {
                return e.getKey();
            }
        }
        String[] parts = path.split("/");
        if (parts.length == 1) {
            return "(root)";
        }
        return String.join("/", java.util.Arrays.copyOfRange(parts, 0, Math.min(areaDepth, parts.length - 1)));
    }

    private static Map<String, List<String>> defaults() {
        Map<String, List<String>> m = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        m.put("lockfile", List.of("package-lock.json", "yarn.lock", "pnpm-lock.yaml", "uv.lock", "poetry.lock",
                "Cargo.lock", "go.sum", "Gemfile.lock", "composer.lock", "*.lock"));
        m.put("generated", List.of("**/generated/**", "*_pb2.py", "*_pb2_grpc.py", "*.min.js", "*.min.css",
                "*.snap", "**/dist/**"));
        m.put("vendored", List.of("vendor/**", "**/vendor/**", "third_party/**", "**/third_party/**",
                "thirdparty/**"));
        m.put("binary", List.of("*.png", "*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.gif", "*.ico", "*.pdf", "*.jar", "*.zip", "*.gz",
                "*.woff", "*.woff2", "*.ttf", "*.so", "*.dll", "*.exe"));
        m.put("ci", List.of(".github/workflows/**", ".github/actions/**", ".gitlab-ci.yml", ".circleci/**",
                "Jenkinsfile", "azure-pipelines.yml", ".buildkite/**"));
        m.put("build-infra", List.of());
        m.put("ai-instruction", List.of("AGENTS.md", "CLAUDE.md", "GEMINI.md", ".github/copilot-instructions.md",
                ".github/instructions/**", ".cursor/**", ".cursorrules", ".kiro/**", ".github/skills/**"));
        m.put("migration", List.of("**/migrations/**", "**/alembic/**"));
        m.put("dependency-manifest", List.of("requirements*.txt", "pyproject.toml", "setup.py", "setup.cfg",
                "package.json", "pom.xml", "build.gradle", "build.gradle.kts", "Cargo.toml", "go.mod", "Gemfile",
                "*.csproj"));
        m.put("test", List.of("**/test/**", "**/tests/**", "**/__tests__/**", "**/testing/**", "**/testdata/**",
                "test_*.py", "*_test.py", "conftest.py", "*_test.go", "*Test.java", "*Tests.java", "*IT.java",
                "*Test.scala", "*Suite.scala", "*Spec.scala", "*Test.kt", "*_spec.rb", "*_test.rb",
                "*.test.*", "*.spec.*"));
        m.put("docs", List.of("*.md", "*.rst", "*.adoc", "*.txt", "docs/**", "**/docs/**", "**/newsfragments/**",
                "CHANGELOG*", "NEWS*"));
        m.put("config", List.of("*.yaml", "*.yml", "*.json", "*.toml", "*.ini", "*.cfg", "*.properties",
                "*.conf", "*.xml"));
        return java.util.Collections.unmodifiableMap(m);
    }

    private static Set<String> categories() {
        Set<String> s = new java.util.LinkedHashSet<>(DEFAULTS.keySet());
        s.add(SOURCE);
        return java.util.Collections.unmodifiableSet(s);
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/MetaText.java`

Component J: title and body facts. 171 lines, sha256 `ab556922313e95e5`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Deterministic facts from the PR title and body: intent class, linked issue, template sections,
 * description claims about tests, and AI-disclosure consistency. Everything here is pattern based and
 * documented; nothing interprets meaning.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
final class MetaText {

    static final Map<String, Pattern> INTENTS = intents();
    private static final Pattern COMMENT = Pattern.compile("(?s)<!--.*?-->");
    private static final Pattern WORD = Pattern.compile("[\\p{L}\\p{N}][\\p{L}\\p{N}'_-]*");
    private static final Pattern ISSUE = Pattern.compile("(?<![\\w&])#\\d+\\b|github\\.com/[\\w.-]+/[\\w.-]+/issues/\\d+");
    private static final Pattern NEXT_HEADING = Pattern.compile("(?m)^\\s{0,3}(#{1,6}\\s|\\*\\*[^*\\n]+\\*\\*\\s*$)");
    /*
     * Only claims about tests ADDED count ("added 22 test cases", "5 new tests", "12 tests added").
     * Calibration showed that counts of tests RUN ("127 tests passing", "1111 passed") are common in
     * good PRs and say nothing about the diff.
     */
    private static final Pattern CLAIM_COUNT = Pattern.compile(
            "(?i)\\b(?:added|adds|add|wrote|writes|introduced?|introduces|includes?)\\s+(\\d{1,4})\\s+"
                    + "(?:new\\s+|unit\\s+|additional\\s+|integration\\s+)*(?:test cases|tests|test)\\b"
                    + "|(?<![#\\w.])(\\d{1,4})\\s+(?:new|additional)\\s+(?:unit\\s+|integration\\s+)?(?:test cases|tests)\\b"
                    + "|(?<![#\\w.])(\\d{1,4})\\s+(?:unit\\s+|integration\\s+)?(?:test cases|tests)\\s+(?:were\\s+)?added\\b");
    private static final Pattern CLAIM_ADDED = Pattern.compile(
            "(?i)\\b(added|adds|add|wrote|written|new|includes?|included|updated?)\\b[^.\\n]{0,30}?\\b(?:unit |integration )?tests?\\b");
    /** Negations and offers ("happy to add tests", "will add tests") are not claims that tests were added. */
    private static final Pattern NEGATION = Pattern.compile(
            "(?i)\\b(no|not|without|none|nothing|never|will|would|could|can|should|if|happy to|todo|follow-?up)\\b|n't");
    private static final Pattern UNCHECKED_ITEM = Pattern.compile("^\\s*[-*]\\s*\\[\\s\\]");
    /** A filled Generated-by trailer; {@code [Tool Name]} is the unfilled template placeholder. */
    private static final Pattern TRAILER = Pattern.compile("(?im)^\\s*generated-by:\\s*(?!\\[tool name)\\S");
    private static final Pattern YES_UNCHECKED = Pattern.compile("(?im)^\\s*[-*]\\s*\\[\\s\\]\\s*yes\\b");
    private static final Pattern YES_CHECKED = Pattern.compile("(?im)^\\s*[-*]\\s*\\[[xX]\\]\\s*yes\\b");
    private static final Pattern TEST_DEF = Pattern.compile(
            "^\\s*(?:async\\s+)?def\\s+test\\w*\\s*\\(|^\\s*@(?:Test|ParameterizedTest)\\b|^\\s*func\\s+Test\\w*\\s*\\("
                    + "|^\\s*(?:it|test)(?:\\.each\\([^)]*\\))?\\s*\\(\\s*['\"`]");

    private MetaText() {
    }

    static String intent(String title) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, Pattern> e : INTENTS.entrySet()) {
            if (e.getValue().matcher(title).find()) {
                return e.getKey();
            }
        }
        return "unknown";
    }

    static boolean linkedIssue(String text, List<Pattern> extra) {
        if (ISSUE.matcher(text).find()) {
            return true;
        }
        for (Pattern p : extra) {
            if (p.matcher(text).find()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** Body without HTML comments and without lines matching any ignore pattern (template boilerplate). */
    static String clean(String body, List<Pattern> ignore) {
        List<String> kept = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String line : COMMENT.matcher(body).replaceAll("").split("\n")) {
            boolean drop = false;
            for (Pattern p : ignore) {
                drop |= p.matcher(line).find();
            }
            if (!drop) {
                kept.add(line);
            }
        }
        return String.join("\n", kept);
    }

    static int words(String text) {
        Matcher m = WORD.matcher(text);
        int n = 0;
        while (m.find()) {
            n++;
        }
        return n;
    }

    static List<String> missingSections(String body, List<String> required) {
        List<String> missing = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String name : required) {
            Matcher h = Pattern.compile("(?im)^\\s{0,3}(?:#{1,6}\\s*|\\*\\*\\s*)" + Pattern.quote(name) + ".*$").matcher(body);
            if (!h.find()) {
                missing.add(name);
                continue;
            }
            String rest = body.substring(h.end());
            Matcher next = NEXT_HEADING.matcher(rest);
            if (words(next.find() ? rest.substring(0, next.start()) : rest) == 0) {
                missing.add(name);
            }
        }
        return missing;
    }

    static int testsClaimCount(String body) {
        int max = 0;
        Matcher m = CLAIM_COUNT.matcher(body);
        while (m.find()) {
            String n = m.group(1) != null ? m.group(1) : m.group(2) != null ? m.group(2) : m.group(3);
            max = Math.max(max, Integer.parseInt(n));
        }
        return max;
    }

    static boolean testsAddedClaim(String body) {
        for (String line : body.split("\n")) {
            if (UNCHECKED_ITEM.matcher(line).find()) {
                continue;
            }
            Matcher m = CLAIM_ADDED.matcher(line);
            while (m.find()) {
                String around = line.substring(Math.max(0, m.start() - 25), m.end());
                if (!NEGATION.matcher(around).find()) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    static boolean disclosureMismatch(String body) {
        // Only "tool named, box left unchecked" is a contradiction. A ticked box with the tool named after
        // "Yes:" instead of in the trailer is a normal way to fill the template (calibration finding).
        return TRAILER.matcher(body).find() && YES_UNCHECKED.matcher(body).find() && !YES_CHECKED.matcher(body).find();
    }

    static int testFunctions(List<String> addedLines) {
        int n = 0;
        for (String l : addedLines) {
            if (TEST_DEF.matcher(l).find()) {
                n++;
            }
        }
        return n;
    }

    private static Map<String, Pattern> intents() {
        Map<String, Pattern> m = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        m.put("typo", Pattern.compile("(?i)\\b(typos?|spelling|misspell\\w*|grammar)\\b"));
        m.put("docs", Pattern.compile("(?i)\\b(docs?|documentation|docstrings?|readme)\\b"));
        m.put("bump", Pattern.compile("(?i)\\b(bump|bumps|bumped|upgrade|upgrades)\\b"));
        m.put("test", Pattern.compile("(?i)^\\s*(\\[?tests?\\]?:|\\[tests?\\])|\\b(add|improve|fix)\\s+(unit\\s+)?tests?\\b"));
        m.put("refactor", Pattern.compile("(?i)\\b(refactor\\w*|cleanup|clean up|rename\\w*)\\b"));
        m.put("fix", Pattern.compile("(?i)\\b(fix(es|ed|ing)?|bug|minor|small|tweak|nit|correct)\\b"));
        m.put("feature", Pattern.compile("(?i)\\b(add|adds|added|feat|feature|support|implement\\w*|introduce\\w*|new)\\b"));
        return java.util.Collections.unmodifiableMap(m);
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/GitHistory.java`

Component J: read-only git history. 52 lines, sha256 `24def88b8bcf0e07`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/**
 * Runs read-only {@code git} queries for history facts. Any failure (no repository, shallow clone,
 * unknown ref, timeout) yields an empty result, which makes the fact unknown rather than failing the run.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
@FunctionalInterface
interface GitHistory {

    Optional<String> run(Path workspace, List<String> args);

    static GitHistory process(long timeoutMs) {
        return (workspace, args) -> git(workspace, args, timeoutMs);
    }

    static Optional<String> git(Path workspace, List<String> args, long timeoutMs) {
        Path out = null;
        try {
            out = Files.createTempFile("aiv-change-policy", ".out");
            List<String> cmd = new ArrayList<>(List.of("git", "-c", "core.quotepath=off"));
            cmd.addAll(args);
            Process p = new ProcessBuilder(cmd).directory(workspace.toFile())
                    .redirectOutput(out.toFile()).redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.DISCARD).start();
            if (!p.waitFor(timeoutMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) {
                p.destroyForcibly().waitFor(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            return p.exitValue() == 0 ? Optional.of(Files.readString(out, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) : Optional.empty();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        } finally {
            if (out != null) {
                out.toFile().delete();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Fact names the policy language accepts, and rendering helpers. A fact missing from the map is
 * unknown in this run (for example PR metadata in a local run), except per-category and per-area
 * counts, which are zero when absent.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
final class Facts {

    static final String BY_CATEGORY = "files.by_category.";
    static final String BY_AREA = "files.by_area.";

    static final Set<String> NAMES = Set.of(
            "files.changed", "files.reviewable", "files.added", "files.deleted", "files.renamed",
            "lines.changed", "lines.added", "lines.deleted", "lines.source_changed", "lines.test_changed",
            "lines.docs_changed", "lines.non_test_changed", "lines.total_changed",
            "code.large_new_files", "code.max_function_lines", "code.stub_functions",
            "scope.areas", "scope.area_list", "churn.whitespace_only_files",
            "tests.files_changed", "tests.functions_added", "tests.source_areas_without_tests",
            "budget.exceeded", "budget.detail",
            "intent.class", "meta.labels", "meta.author_association", "meta.author_tier", "meta.is_draft",
            "meta.linked_issue", "meta.body_words", "template.sections_missing", "template.missing_list",
            "claims.tests_count", "claims.tests_overclaim", "claims.tests_added", "claims.ai_disclosure_mismatch",
            "git.commits", "git.behind_base", "git.commits_without_body", "git.conventional_prefix_commits");

    private Facts() {
    }

    static boolean isKnown(String name) {
        if (name.startsWith(BY_CATEGORY)) {
            return Classifier.CATEGORIES.contains(name.substring(BY_CATEGORY.length()));
        }
        return NAMES.contains(name) || (name.startsWith(BY_AREA) && name.length() > BY_AREA.length());
    }

    static Object lookup(Map<String, Object> facts, String name) {
        Object v = facts.get(name);
        if (v == null && (name.startsWith(BY_CATEGORY) || name.startsWith(BY_AREA))) {
            return 0;
        }
        return v;
    }

    /** Replaces {@code {name}} with a fact or param value; unknown names render as {@code ?}. */
    static String render(String template, Map<String, Object> facts, Map<String, Object> params) {
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
        int i = 0;
        while (i < template.length()) {
            int open = template.indexOf('{', i);
            int close = open < 0 ? -1 : template.indexOf('}', open);
            if (close < 0) {
                out.append(template, i, template.length());
                break;
            }
            out.append(template, i, open);
            String key = template.substring(open + 1, close);
            Object v = Facts.isKnown(key) ? lookup(facts, key) : params.get(key);
            out.append(v == null ? "?" : sanitize(v));
            i = close + 1;
        }
        return out.toString();
    }

    static String sanitize(Object v) {
        String s = v instanceof List<?> l ? String.join(", ", l.stream().map(Object::toString).toList()) : v.toString();
        s = s.replaceAll("[\\p{Cntrl}]+", " ").trim();
        return s.length() > 200 ? s.substring(0, 200) + "..." : s;
    }

    /** Compact JSON object for the {@code change-policy.facts} note and calibration tools. */
    static String toJson(Map<String, Object> facts) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("{");
        for (Map.Entry<String, Object> e : facts.entrySet()) {
            if (sb.length() > 1) {
                sb.append(',');
            }
            sb.append(quote(e.getKey())).append(':').append(value(e.getValue()));
        }
        return sb.append('}').toString();
    }

    private static String value(Object v) {
        if (v instanceof Number || v instanceof Boolean) {
            return v.toString();
        }
        if (v instanceof List<?> l) {
            return "[" + String.join(",", l.stream().map(o -> quote(o.toString())).toList()) + "]";
        }
        return quote(v.toString());
    }

    static String quote(String s) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("\"");
        for (char c : s.toCharArray()) {
            if (c == '"' || c == '\\') {
                sb.append('\\').append(c);
            } else if (c < 0x20) {
                sb.append(String.format("\\u%04x", (int) c));
            } else {
                sb.append(c);
            }
        }
        return sb.append('"').toString();
    }
}
```
