Component J: policy condition language. 175 lines, sha256 `f773ee25efceb551`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Structured policy condition: {@code all}, {@code any}, {@code not}, or a leaf
 * {@code {fact: <name>, <op>: <value>}}. Parsing validates fact names, operators and
 * {@code ${param}} references, so configuration errors surface even for conditions that never run.
 * Evaluation is three-valued: a missing fact makes its leaf UNKNOWN, and UNKNOWN propagates the way
 * SQL NULL does through AND, OR and NOT.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
sealed interface Condition permits Condition.All, Condition.Any, Condition.Not, Condition.Leaf {

    enum Tri { TRUE, FALSE, UNKNOWN }

    Set<String> OPS = Set.of("eq", "ne", "gt", "gte", "lt", "lte", "in", "not_in", "contains");

    Tri eval(Map<String, Object> facts, Set<String> unknown);

    record All(List<Condition> parts) implements Condition {
        @Override
        public Tri eval(Map<String, Object> facts, Set<String> unknown) {
            Tri out = Tri.TRUE;
            for (Condition c : parts) {
                Tri t = c.eval(facts, unknown);
                if (t == Tri.FALSE) {
                    return Tri.FALSE;
                }
                if (t == Tri.UNKNOWN) {
                    out = Tri.UNKNOWN;
                }
            }
            return out;
        }
    }

    record Any(List<Condition> parts) implements Condition {
        @Override
        public Tri eval(Map<String, Object> facts, Set<String> unknown) {
            Tri out = Tri.FALSE;
            for (Condition c : parts) {
                Tri t = c.eval(facts, unknown);
                if (t == Tri.TRUE) {
                    return Tri.TRUE;
                }
                if (t == Tri.UNKNOWN) {
                    out = Tri.UNKNOWN;
                }
            }
            return out;
        }
    }

    record Not(Condition inner) implements Condition {
        @Override
        public Tri eval(Map<String, Object> facts, Set<String> unknown) {
            Tri t = inner.eval(facts, unknown);
            return t == Tri.UNKNOWN ? t : (t == Tri.TRUE ? Tri.FALSE : Tri.TRUE);
        }
    }

    record Leaf(String fact, String op, Object operand) implements Condition {
        @Override
        public Tri eval(Map<String, Object> facts, Set<String> unknown) {
            Object v = Facts.lookup(facts, fact);
            if (v == null) {
                unknown.add(fact);
                return Tri.UNKNOWN;
            }
            return compare(v) ? Tri.TRUE : Tri.FALSE;
        }

        private boolean compare(Object v) {
            switch (op) {
                case "eq":
                    return same(v, operand);
                case "ne":
                    return !same(v, operand);
                case "in":
                    return ((List<?>) operand).stream().anyMatch(o -> same(v, o));
                case "not_in":
                    return ((List<?>) operand).stream().noneMatch(o -> same(v, o));
                case "contains":
                    return v instanceof List<?> l ? l.stream().anyMatch(o -> same(o, operand))
                            : v.toString().contains(operand.toString());
                default:
                    if (!(v instanceof Number n)) {
                        throw new IllegalArgumentException("change-policy fact " + fact + " is not numeric, cannot use " + op);
                    }
                    int c = Double.compare(n.doubleValue(), ((Number) operand).doubleValue());
                    return switch (op) {
                        case "gt" -> c > 0;
                        case "gte" -> c >= 0;
                        case "lt" -> c < 0;
                        default -> c <= 0;
                    };
            }
        }

        private static boolean same(Object a, Object b) {
            if (a instanceof Number x && b instanceof Number y) {
                return Double.compare(x.doubleValue(), y.doubleValue()) == 0;
            }
            return a.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(b.toString());
        }
    }

    static Condition parse(Object node, Map<String, Object> params, String where) {
        if (!(node instanceof Map<?, ?> m) || m.isEmpty()) {
            throw err(where, "must be a non-empty map");
        }
        if (m.containsKey("all") || m.containsKey("any")) {
            String key = m.containsKey("all") ? "all" : "any";
            if (m.size() != 1 || !(m.get(key) instanceof List<?> l) || l.isEmpty()) {
                throw err(where, "'" + key + "' must be the only key and hold a non-empty list");
            }
            List<Condition> parts = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < l.size(); i++) {
                parts.add(parse(l.get(i), params, where + "." + key + "[" + i + "]"));
            }
            return key.equals("all") ? new All(parts) : new Any(parts);
        }
        if (m.containsKey("not")) {
            if (m.size() != 1) {
                throw err(where, "'not' must be the only key");
            }
            return new Not(parse(m.get("not"), params, where + ".not"));
        }
        Object fact = m.get("fact");
        if (fact == null || m.size() != 2) {
            throw err(where, "a leaf needs 'fact' and exactly one operator (" + String.join(", ", new java.util.TreeSet<>(OPS)) + ")");
        }
        if (!Facts.isKnown(fact.toString())) {
            throw err(where, "unknown fact '" + fact + "'");
        }
        String op = m.keySet().stream().map(Object::toString).filter(k -> !k.equals("fact")).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        if (!OPS.contains(op)) {
            throw err(where, "unknown operator '" + op + "'");
        }
        Object operand = resolve(m.get(op), params, where);
        boolean list = op.equals("in") || op.equals("not_in");
        if (list != (operand instanceof List<?>) || operand == null) {
            throw err(where, "operator '" + op + "' needs " + (list ? "a list" : "a single value"));
        }
        if (Set.of("gt", "gte", "lt", "lte").contains(op) && !(operand instanceof Number)) {
            throw err(where, "operator '" + op + "' needs a number");
        }
        return new Leaf(fact.toString(), op, operand);
    }

    private static Object resolve(Object v, Map<String, Object> params, String where) {
        if (v instanceof String s && s.startsWith("${") && s.endsWith("}")) {
            String name = s.substring(2, s.length() - 1);
            if (!params.containsKey(name)) {
                throw err(where, "unknown param '" + name + "'");
            }
            return params.get(name);
        }
        return v;
    }

    private static IllegalArgumentException err(String where, String msg) {
        return new IllegalArgumentException("change-policy " + where + ": " + msg);
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/PolicyConfig.java`

Component J: pack loading, merging, validation. 254 lines, sha256 `f4d7ac977839ad17`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import org.yaml.snakeyaml.LoaderOptions;
import org.yaml.snakeyaml.Yaml;
import org.yaml.snakeyaml.constructor.SafeConstructor;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Validated change-policy configuration: a built-in pack (default {@code oss-maintainer}) overlaid with
 * the project's gate config. Maps merge by key with the project winning; classification globs and areas
 * from the project are checked before the pack's; policies merge field by field by {@code id}.
 * Unknown keys, categories, facts, operators or params are configuration errors.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
record PolicyConfig(
        Classifier classifier,
        Set<String> nonReviewable,
        Map<String, Limits> budgets,
        Map<String, String> associationTier,
        List<String> requiredSections,
        List<Pattern> templateIgnore,
        List<Pattern> linkedIssuePatterns,
        List<String> overrideLabels,
        Map<String, Object> params,
        List<Policy> policies,
        boolean emitFacts,
        boolean gitFacts,
        long gitTimeoutMs) {

    static final Set<String> KEYS = Set.of("pack", "params", "classification", "non_reviewable_categories", "areas",
            "area_depth", "budgets", "author_tiers", "template", "linked_issue_patterns", "overrides", "policies",
            "emit_facts", "git_facts", "git_timeout_ms", "rule_actions", "unsuppressible_rules");
    static final Set<String> POLICY_KEYS = Set.of("id", "when", "action", "overridable", "override_labels", "message");
    private static final Pattern ID = Pattern.compile("[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,49}");

    record Limits(Integer files, Integer lines, Integer nonTestLines) {
    }

    record Policy(String id, Condition when, String action, boolean overridable, List<String> overrideLabels,
                  String message) {
    }

    static PolicyConfig load(Map<String, Object> project) {
        for (String k : project.keySet()) {
            if (!KEYS.contains(k)) {
                throw err("unknown key '" + k + "'");
            }
        }
        String packName = String.valueOf(project.getOrDefault("pack", "oss-maintainer"));
        Map<String, Object> pack = packName.equals("none") ? Map.of() : pack(packName);
        Map<String, Object> params = merged(map(pack.get("params"), "params"), map(project.get("params"), "params"));
        Map<String, List<String>> classification = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> layer : List.of(map(project.get("classification"), "classification"),
                map(pack.get("classification"), "classification"))) {
            for (Map.Entry<String, Object> e : layer.entrySet()) {
                if (!Classifier.CATEGORIES.contains(e.getKey()) || e.getKey().equals(Classifier.SOURCE)) {
                    throw err("unknown classification category '" + e.getKey() + "'");
                }
                classification.computeIfAbsent(e.getKey(), x -> new ArrayList<>()).addAll(strings(e.getValue(), "classification"));
            }
        }
        Map<String, List<String>> areas = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> layer : List.of(map(project.get("areas"), "areas"), map(pack.get("areas"), "areas"))) {
            layer.forEach((k, v) -> areas.putIfAbsent(k, strings(v, "areas")));
        }
        Set<String> nonReviewable = new LinkedHashSet<>(strings(
                project.getOrDefault("non_reviewable_categories", Classifier.DEFAULT_NON_REVIEWABLE), "non_reviewable_categories"));
        for (String c : nonReviewable) {
            if (!Classifier.CATEGORIES.contains(c)) {
                throw err("unknown non_reviewable category '" + c + "'");
            }
        }
        Map<String, Limits> budgets = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        merged(map(pack.get("budgets"), "budgets"), map(project.get("budgets"), "budgets"))
                .forEach((k, v) -> budgets.put(k, limits(k, v)));
        Map<String, String> tiers = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        merged(map(pack.get("author_tiers"), "author_tiers"), map(project.get("author_tiers"), "author_tiers"))
                .forEach((tier, assoc) -> strings(assoc, "author_tiers").forEach(a -> tiers.put(a.toUpperCase(), tier)));
        Map<String, Object> template = map(project.get("template"), "template");
        Map<String, Object> overrides = map(project.get("overrides"), "overrides");
        Map<String, Map<String, Object>> rawPolicies = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (Object src : List.of(pack.getOrDefault("policies", List.of()), project.getOrDefault("policies", List.of()))) {
            mergePolicies(rawPolicies, src);
        }
        Map<String, Object> perPolicy = map(overrides.get("per_policy"), "overrides.per_policy");
        List<Policy> policies = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> p : rawPolicies.values()) {
            policies.add(policy(p, params, strings(perPolicy.getOrDefault(p.get("id"), List.of()), "overrides.per_policy")));
        }
        return new PolicyConfig(new Classifier(classification, areas, positiveInt(project.getOrDefault("area_depth", 1), "area_depth")),
                nonReviewable, budgets, tiers, strings(template.getOrDefault("required_sections", List.of()), "template"),
                patterns(template.getOrDefault("ignore_patterns", List.of()), "template.ignore_patterns"),
                patterns(project.getOrDefault("linked_issue_patterns", List.of()), "linked_issue_patterns"),
                strings(overrides.getOrDefault("labels", List.of()), "overrides.labels"), params, policies,
                bool(project.getOrDefault("emit_facts", false), "emit_facts"),
                bool(project.getOrDefault("git_facts", true), "git_facts"),
                positiveInt(project.getOrDefault("git_timeout_ms", 10000), "git_timeout_ms"));
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    static Map<String, Object> pack(String name) {
        if (!ID.matcher(name).matches()) {
            throw err("unknown pack '" + name + "'");
        }
        InputStream in = PolicyConfig.class.getResourceAsStream("packs/" + name + ".yaml");
        if (in == null) {
            throw err("unknown pack '" + name + "' (built-in: oss-maintainer, airflow, strict-agent, none)");
        }
        Map<String, Object> self = new Yaml(new SafeConstructor(new LoaderOptions())).load(in);
        Object parent = self.remove("extends");
        if (parent == null) {
            return self;
        }
        Map<String, Object> base = pack(parent.toString());
        Map<String, Map<String, Object>> policies = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        mergePolicies(policies, base.getOrDefault("policies", List.of()));
        mergePolicies(policies, self.getOrDefault("policies", List.of()));
        Map<String, Object> out = new LinkedHashMap<>(base);
        for (String k : List.of("params", "budgets", "author_tiers")) {
            out.put(k, merged(map(base.get(k), k), map(self.get(k), k)));
        }
        for (String k : List.of("classification", "areas")) {
            out.put(k, merged(map(self.get(k), k), map(base.get(k), k)));
        }
        out.put("policies", new ArrayList<>(policies.values()));
        return out;
    }

    private static void mergePolicies(Map<String, Map<String, Object>> into, Object src) {
        if (!(src instanceof List<?> list)) {
            throw err("policies must be a list");
        }
        for (Object o : list) {
            Map<String, Object> p = map(o, "policies[]");
            Object id = p.get("id");
            if (id == null || !ID.matcher(id.toString()).matches()) {
                throw err("policy id must be lower-case letters, digits or hyphens, got '" + id + "'");
            }
            for (String k : p.keySet()) {
                if (!POLICY_KEYS.contains(k)) {
                    throw err("policy " + id + ": unknown key '" + k + "'");
                }
            }
            into.computeIfAbsent(id.toString(), x -> new LinkedHashMap<>()).putAll(p);
        }
    }

    private static Policy policy(Map<String, Object> p, Map<String, Object> params, List<String> extraLabels) {
        String id = p.get("id").toString();
        String action = String.valueOf(p.getOrDefault("action", "warn"));
        if (!Set.of("fail", "warn", "off").contains(action)) {
            throw err("policy " + id + ": action must be fail, warn or off");
        }
        if (p.get("when") == null) {
            throw err("policy " + id + ": 'when' is required");
        }
        List<String> labels = new ArrayList<>(strings(p.getOrDefault("override_labels", List.of()), "override_labels"));
        labels.addAll(extraLabels);
        return new Policy(id, Condition.parse(p.get("when"), params, "policy " + id), action,
                bool(p.getOrDefault("overridable", false), "overridable"), labels,
                String.valueOf(p.getOrDefault("message", "Change policy " + id + " matched.")));
    }

    private static Limits limits(String key, Object v) {
        Map<String, Object> m = map(v, "budgets." + key);
        for (String k : m.keySet()) {
            if (!Set.of("files", "lines", "non_test_lines").contains(k)) {
                throw err("budgets." + key + ": unknown limit '" + k + "' (files, lines, non_test_lines)");
            }
        }
        return new Limits(optInt(m.get("files"), key), optInt(m.get("lines"), key), optInt(m.get("non_test_lines"), key));
    }

    private static Integer optInt(Object v, String key) {
        return v == null ? null : positiveInt(v, "budgets." + key);
    }

    static int positiveInt(Object v, String field) {
        if (v instanceof Integer n && n > 0) {
            return n;
        }
        throw err(field + " must be a positive integer, got '" + v + "'");
    }

    private static boolean bool(Object v, String field) {
        if (v instanceof Boolean b) {
            return b;
        }
        throw err(field + " must be true or false");
    }

    private static Map<String, Object> merged(Map<String, Object> base, Map<String, Object> top) {
        Map<String, Object> out = new LinkedHashMap<>(base);
        out.putAll(top);
        return out;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    static Map<String, Object> map(Object v, String field) {
        if (v == null) {
            return Map.of();
        }
        if (v instanceof Map<?, ?> m) {
            Map<String, Object> out = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            m.forEach((k, val) -> out.put(String.valueOf(k), val));
            return out;
        }
        throw err(field + " must be a map");
    }

    static List<String> strings(Object v, String field) {
        if (!(v instanceof List<?> l)) {
            throw err(field + " must be a list of strings");
        }
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Object o : l) {
            if (o == null || o.toString().isBlank()) {
                throw err(field + " must not contain blank entries");
            }
            out.add(o.toString().trim());
        }
        return out;
    }

    private static List<Pattern> patterns(Object v, String field) {
        List<Pattern> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String s : strings(v, field)) {
            try {
                out.add(Pattern.compile(s));
            } catch (java.util.regex.PatternSyntaxException e) {
                throw err(field + ": invalid regular expression '" + s + "'");
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    static IllegalArgumentException err(String msg) {
        return new IllegalArgumentException("change-policy: " + msg);
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/FactCollector.java`

Component J: fact snapshot. 262 lines, sha256 `66802d226a82578a`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.ChangedFile;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.Diff;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.TreeMap;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Computes the fact snapshot for one change. Pure function of the diff, the PR metadata and read-only
 * git history; facts that cannot be computed in this run are left out (unknown).
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
final class FactCollector {

    /** PR metadata supplied by CI; every field may be null in a local run. */
    record Meta(String title, String body, List<String> labels, String authorAssociation, Boolean draft) {
    }

    private static final Pattern CONVENTIONAL = Pattern.compile("^(feat|fix|chore|docs|refactor|test|tests|ci|build|perf|style)(\\([^)]*\\))?!?:");
    /** Function or class starts in added lines: Python, Go, JavaScript/TypeScript, Kotlin, Rust. */
    private static final Pattern UNIT_START = Pattern.compile(
            "^\\s*(?:(?:async\\s+)?def\\s+\\w+\\s*\\(|class\\s+\\w+|func\\s+(?:\\([^)]*\\)\\s*)?\\w+\\s*\\("
                    + "|(?:export\\s+)?(?:async\\s+)?function\\s+\\w+\\s*\\(|fun\\s+\\w+\\s*\\(|(?:pub\\s+)?fn\\s+\\w+\\s*[(<])");
    private static final Pattern FUNCTION_START = Pattern.compile(
            "^\\s*(?:(?:async\\s+)?def\\s|func\\s|(?:export\\s+)?(?:async\\s+)?function\\s|fun\\s|(?:pub\\s+)?fn\\s)");
    private static final Pattern STUB_BODY = Pattern.compile(
            "^\\s*(?:pass|\\.\\.\\.|raise\\s+NotImplementedError(?:\\(.*\\))?|throw\\s+new\\s+(?:Error|UnsupportedOperationException)\\(.*\\);?"
                    + "|todo!\\(.*\\)|unimplemented!\\(.*\\)|panic\\(\"not implemented\"\\))\\s*$");
    private static final Pattern DOC_OR_COMMENT = Pattern.compile("^\\s*(?:#|//|/\\*|\\*|\"\"\"|''')");
    static final int LARGE_NEW_FILE_LINES = 500;

    private FactCollector() {
    }

    static Map<String, Object> collect(Diff diff, List<ChangedFile> files, Meta meta, PolicyConfig cfg,
                                       GitHistory git, Path workspace) {
        Map<String, Object> f = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        Map<String, DiffStats.FileDiff> stats = DiffStats.parse(diff.getRawDiff());
        Map<String, Integer> byCategory = new TreeMap<>();
        Map<String, int[]> byArea = new TreeMap<>(); // files, lines, non-test lines
        Set<String> sourceAreas = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        Set<String> testAreas = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        int changed = 0, reviewable = 0, added = 0, deleted = 0, renamed = 0, wsOnly = 0;
        int lAdded = 0, lDeleted = 0, lSource = 0, lTest = 0, lDocs = 0, testFiles = 0, testFns = 0;
        int largeNewFiles = 0, maxFunction = 0, stubs = 0;
        for (ChangedFile cf : files) {
            String path = cf.getPath() == null ? "" : cf.getPath().replace('\\', '/');
            if (path.isBlank()) {
                continue;
            }
            changed++;
            DiffStats.FileDiff d = stats.getOrDefault(path, DiffStats.FileDiff.EMPTY);
            String cat = cfg.classifier().category(path);
            String area = cfg.classifier().area(path);
            byCategory.merge(cat, 1, Integer::sum);
            added += cf.getChangeType() == ChangedFile.ChangeType.ADDED ? 1 : 0;
            deleted += cf.getChangeType() == ChangedFile.ChangeType.DELETED ? 1 : 0;
            renamed += cf.getChangeType() == ChangedFile.ChangeType.RENAMED ? 1 : 0;
            boolean ws = d.whitespaceOnly();
            wsOnly += ws ? 1 : 0;
            if (cfg.nonReviewable().contains(cat) || ws || d.changed() == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            reviewable++;
            lAdded += d.added().size();
            lDeleted += d.removed().size();
            int[] a = byArea.computeIfAbsent(area, x -> new int[3]);
            a[0]++;
            a[1] += d.changed();
            if (cat.equals("test")) {
                testFiles++;
                lTest += d.changed();
                testFns += MetaText.testFunctions(d.added());
                testAreas.add(area);
            } else {
                a[2] += d.changed();
            }
            lSource += cat.equals(Classifier.SOURCE) ? d.changed() : 0;
            lDocs += cat.equals("docs") ? d.changed() : 0;
            if (cat.equals(Classifier.SOURCE)) {
                sourceAreas.add(area);
                largeNewFiles += cf.getChangeType() == ChangedFile.ChangeType.ADDED
                        && d.added().size() >= LARGE_NEW_FILE_LINES ? 1 : 0;
                int[] shape = codeShape(d.added());
                maxFunction = Math.max(maxFunction, shape[0]);
                stubs += shape[1];
            }
        }
        f.put("files.changed", changed);
        f.put("files.reviewable", reviewable);
        f.put("files.added", added);
        f.put("files.deleted", deleted);
        f.put("files.renamed", renamed);
        byCategory.forEach((k, v) -> f.put(Facts.BY_CATEGORY + k, v));
        byArea.forEach((k, v) -> f.put(Facts.BY_AREA + k, v[0]));
        f.put("lines.changed", lAdded + lDeleted);
        f.put("lines.added", lAdded);
        f.put("lines.deleted", lDeleted);
        f.put("lines.source_changed", lSource);
        f.put("lines.test_changed", lTest);
        f.put("lines.docs_changed", lDocs);
        f.put("lines.non_test_changed", lAdded + lDeleted - lTest);
        // numstat over every changed path, including generated and vendored files: the raw size of the diff
        f.put("lines.total_changed", Math.max(0, diff.getLinesAdded()) + Math.max(0, diff.getLinesDeleted()));
        f.put("code.large_new_files", largeNewFiles);
        f.put("code.max_function_lines", maxFunction);
        f.put("code.stub_functions", stubs);
        f.put("scope.areas", byArea.size());
        f.put("scope.area_list", String.join(", ", byArea.keySet()));
        f.put("churn.whitespace_only_files", wsOnly);
        f.put("tests.files_changed", testFiles);
        f.put("tests.functions_added", testFns);
        sourceAreas.removeAll(testAreas);
        f.put("tests.source_areas_without_tests", sourceAreas.size());
        String tier = meta.authorAssociation() == null ? null
                : cfg.associationTier().getOrDefault(meta.authorAssociation().toUpperCase(), "external");
        budget(f, cfg, tier, reviewable, lAdded + lDeleted, lAdded + lDeleted - lTest, byArea);
        metadata(f, meta, cfg, tier, testFns);
        if (cfg.gitFacts()) {
            history(f, git, workspace, diff.getBaseRef(), diff.getHeadRef());
        }
        return f;
    }

    /**
     * Shape of the added lines of one source file: the longest run of added lines that follows a function
     * start before the next function or class start, and the number of functions whose first statement is
     * a stub ({@code pass}, {@code ...}, {@code raise NotImplementedError}, {@code todo!()} and similar).
     * Added lines from separate hunks are treated as one sequence, so the length is an upper bound.
     */
    static int[] codeShape(List<String> added) {
        int longest = 0, current = -1, stubs = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < added.size(); i++) {
            String line = added.get(i);
            if (UNIT_START.matcher(line).find()) {
                longest = Math.max(longest, current);
                current = FUNCTION_START.matcher(line).find() ? 0 : -1;
                if (current == 0 && stubFollows(added, i)) {
                    stubs++;
                }
            } else if (current >= 0) {
                current++;
            }
        }
        return new int[] {Math.max(longest, current), stubs};
    }

    private static boolean stubFollows(List<String> added, int def) {
        for (int j = def + 1; j < added.size(); j++) {
            String next = added.get(j);
            if (!next.isBlank() && !DOC_OR_COMMENT.matcher(next).find()) {
                return STUB_BODY.matcher(next).matches();
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static void budget(Map<String, Object> f, PolicyConfig cfg, String tier, int files, int lines,
                               int nonTest, Map<String, int[]> byArea) {
        List<String> over = new ArrayList<>();
        String wholeKey = tier != null && cfg.budgets().containsKey(tier) ? tier : "default";
        check(over, "", wholeKey, cfg.budgets().get(wholeKey), files, lines, nonTest);
        for (Map.Entry<String, int[]> e : byArea.entrySet()) {
            String key = tier != null && cfg.budgets().containsKey(tier + "." + e.getKey()) ? tier + "." + e.getKey() : e.getKey();
            int[] a = e.getValue();
            check(over, e.getKey() + ": ", key, cfg.budgets().get(key), a[0], a[1], a[2]);
        }
        f.put("budget.exceeded", !over.isEmpty());
        f.put("budget.detail", over.isEmpty() ? "within budget" : String.join("; ", over));
    }

    private static void check(List<String> over, String prefix, String key, PolicyConfig.Limits l,
                              int files, int lines, int nonTest) {
        if (l == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (l.files() != null && files > l.files()) {
            over.add(prefix + files + " reviewable files > " + l.files() + " (" + key + ")");
        }
        if (l.lines() != null && lines > l.lines()) {
            over.add(prefix + lines + " changed lines > " + l.lines() + " (" + key + ")");
        }
        if (l.nonTestLines() != null && nonTest > l.nonTestLines()) {
            over.add(prefix + nonTest + " non-test lines > " + l.nonTestLines() + " (" + key + ")");
        }
    }

    private static void metadata(Map<String, Object> f, Meta meta, PolicyConfig cfg, String tier, int testFns) {
        if (meta.title() != null) {
            f.put("intent.class", MetaText.intent(meta.title()));
        }
        if (meta.labels() != null) {
            f.put("meta.labels", meta.labels());
        }
        if (tier != null) {
            f.put("meta.author_association", meta.authorAssociation().toUpperCase());
            f.put("meta.author_tier", tier);
        }
        if (meta.draft() != null) {
            f.put("meta.is_draft", meta.draft());
        }
        if (meta.body() == null) {
            return;
        }
        String body = MetaText.clean(meta.body(), cfg.templateIgnore());
        f.put("meta.linked_issue", MetaText.linkedIssue((meta.title() == null ? "" : meta.title()) + "\n" + body,
                cfg.linkedIssuePatterns()));
        f.put("meta.body_words", MetaText.words(body));
        List<String> missing = MetaText.missingSections(body, cfg.requiredSections());
        f.put("template.sections_missing", missing.size());
        f.put("template.missing_list", missing.isEmpty() ? "none missing" : String.join(", ", missing));
        int claims = MetaText.testsClaimCount(body);
        f.put("claims.tests_count", claims);
        f.put("claims.tests_overclaim", claims == 0 ? 0 : claims - testFns);
        f.put("claims.tests_added", MetaText.testsAddedClaim(body));
        f.put("claims.ai_disclosure_mismatch", MetaText.disclosureMismatch(body));
    }

    private static void history(Map<String, Object> f, GitHistory git, Path ws, String base, String head) {
        if (base == null || head == null || base.isBlank() || head.isBlank()) {
            return;
        }
        count(git.run(ws, List.of("rev-list", "--count", base + ".." + head))).ifPresent(n -> f.put("git.commits", n));
        count(git.run(ws, List.of("rev-list", "--count", head + ".." + base))).ifPresent(n -> f.put("git.behind_base", n));
        git.run(ws, List.of("log", "--format=%s%x1f%b%x1e", base + ".." + head)).ifPresent(log -> {
            int noBody = 0, conventional = 0;
            for (String entry : log.split("\u001e")) {
                String[] parts = entry.strip().split("\u001f", 2);
                if (parts[0].isBlank()) {
                    continue;
                }
                noBody += parts.length < 2 || parts[1].isBlank() ? 1 : 0;
                conventional += CONVENTIONAL.matcher(parts[0].strip()).find() ? 1 : 0;
            }
            f.put("git.commits_without_body", noBody);
            f.put("git.conventional_prefix_commits", conventional);
        });
    }

    private static Optional<Integer> count(Optional<String> out) {
        try {
            return out.map(s -> Integer.parseInt(s.strip()));
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/ChangePolicyGate.java`

Component J: the gate. 186 lines, sha256 `cb4bb19957e78c7d`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.AIVContext;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.ChangedFile;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.Finding;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.GateResult;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.port.QualityGate;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.TreeSet;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Evaluates declarative change policies (review budgets, untested source changes, title versus diff,
 * description claims, template and history rules) over deterministic facts about the pull request.
 *
 * <p>Default off. Every built-in policy is advisory ({@code warn}); a project promotes a measured policy
 * with {@code action: fail}. A policy whose facts are unavailable (for example PR metadata in a local run)
 * does not fire and is listed as skipped. Maintainer override labels turn a fired policy into a notice.
 *
 * <pre>
 * - id: change-policy
 *   enabled: true
 *   config:
 *     pack: oss-maintainer          # or airflow, strict-agent, none
 *     budgets: { external.core: { non_test_lines: 200 } }
 *     overrides: { labels: [large-change-approved] }
 *     policies:
 *       - { id: untested-source-change, action: fail }
 * </pre>
 *
 * See docs/AI-integration/CHANGE-POLICY-ENGINE-DESIGN.md.
 *
 * @author Vaquar Khan
 */
public final class ChangePolicyGate implements QualityGate {

    public static final String ID = "change-policy";
    public static final String FACTS_RULE = "change-policy.facts";
    public static final String OVERRIDE_RULE = "change-policy.override";
    private static final Pattern JSON_STRING = Pattern.compile("\"((?:[^\"\\\\]|\\\\.)*)\"");

    private final Function<String, String> env;
    private final GitHistory git;

    public ChangePolicyGate() {
        this(System::getenv, null);
    }

    ChangePolicyGate(Function<String, String> env, GitHistory git) {
        this.env = env;
        this.git = git;
    }

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return ID;
    }

    @Override
    public GateResult evaluate(AIVContext context) {
        PolicyConfig cfg = PolicyConfig.load(context.getConfig().findGate(ID).map(g -> g.getConfig()).orElse(Map.of()));
        FactCollector.Meta meta = meta(context);
        GitHistory history = git != null ? git : GitHistory.process(cfg.gitTimeoutMs());
        Map<String, Object> facts = FactCollector.collect(context.getDiff(), allFiles(context), meta, cfg, history,
                context.getWorkspace());
        List<Finding> findings = new ArrayList<>();
        Set<String> skipped = new TreeSet<>();
        Set<String> labels = new LinkedHashSet<>(meta.labels() == null ? List.of() : meta.labels());
        int blocking = 0, advisory = 0;
        for (PolicyConfig.Policy p : cfg.policies()) {
            String rule = "policy." + p.id();
            String action = context.getConfig().getRuleAction(ID, rule, p.action());
            if (action.equals("off")) {
                continue;
            }
            Set<String> unknown = new TreeSet<>();
            Condition.Tri t = p.when().eval(facts, unknown);
            if (t == Condition.Tri.UNKNOWN) {
                skipped.add(p.id() + " (" + String.join(", ", unknown) + ")");
                continue;
            }
            if (t == Condition.Tri.FALSE) {
                continue;
            }
            // Policy-specific labels always apply; project-wide labels only for overridable policies.
            String override = firstLabel(labels, p.overrideLabels(), p.overridable() ? cfg.overrideLabels() : List.of());
            if (override != null) {
                findings.add(Finding.atLine(OVERRIDE_RULE, "(diff)", 1,
                        "[POLICY-OVERRIDE] " + p.id() + " by label " + override));
                continue;
            }
            findings.add(Finding.atLine(rule, "(diff)", 1, "[" + action.toUpperCase() + "] "
                    + Facts.render(p.message(), facts, cfg.params())));
            if (action.equals("fail")) {
                blocking++;
            } else {
                advisory++;
            }
        }
        if (cfg.emitFacts()) {
            findings.add(Finding.atLine(FACTS_RULE, "(diff)", 1, Facts.toJson(facts)));
        }
        String msg = (blocking + advisory) + " change policies matched (" + blocking + " blocking)"
                + (skipped.isEmpty() ? "" : "; skipped, facts unavailable: " + String.join("; ", skipped));
        if (blocking > 0) {
            // Only fail-action policies block; warn policies, overrides and the facts note stay advisory.
            Set<String> advisoryRules = new LinkedHashSet<>();
            for (Finding f : findings) {
                if (!f.getMessage().startsWith("[FAIL] ")) {
                    advisoryRules.add(f.getRuleId());
                }
            }
            return new GateResult(ID, false, msg, true, findings, advisoryRules);
        }
        return advisory > 0 ? GateResult.advisory(ID, msg, findings) : new GateResult(ID, true, msg, false, findings);
    }

    private static String firstLabel(Set<String> present, List<String> perPolicy, List<String> global) {
        for (List<String> source : List.of(perPolicy, global)) {
            for (String l : source) {
                if (present.contains(l)) {
                    return l;
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static List<ChangedFile> allFiles(AIVContext context) {
        List<ChangedFile> files = new ArrayList<>(context.getDiff().getChangedFiles());
        if (context.getMetadata().get(AIVContext.EXCLUDED_CHANGED_FILES) instanceof List<?> excluded) {
            for (Object o : excluded) {
                if (o instanceof ChangedFile f) {
                    files.add(f);
                }
            }
        }
        return files;
    }

    FactCollector.Meta meta(AIVContext context) {
        String title = text(context, "pr_title", "AIV_PR_TITLE");
        String body = text(context, "pr_body", "AIV_PR_BODY");
        String assoc = text(context, "pr_author_association", "AIV_PR_AUTHOR_ASSOCIATION");
        String draft = text(context, "pr_draft", "AIV_PR_DRAFT");
        Object rawLabels = context.getMetadata().get("pr_labels");
        List<String> labels;
        if (rawLabels instanceof List<?> l) {
            labels = l.stream().map(String::valueOf).toList();
        } else {
            String json = rawLabels != null ? rawLabels.toString() : env.apply("AIV_PR_LABELS");
            labels = json == null || json.isBlank() ? null : jsonStrings(json);
        }
        // In a PR run the title is always present, so a blank body is an empty description, not unknown.
        return new FactCollector.Meta(title, body == null && title != null ? "" : body, labels, assoc == null ? null : assoc.trim(),
                draft == null ? null : Boolean.parseBoolean(draft.trim()));
    }

    /** Metadata key first (set by embedding tools), then the variable the composite action exports. */
    private String text(AIVContext context, String key, String envVar) {
        Object v = context.getMetadata().get(key);
        String s = v != null && !v.toString().isBlank() ? v.toString() : env.apply(envVar);
        return s == null || s.isBlank() ? null : s;
    }

    static List<String> jsonStrings(String json) {
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        Matcher m = JSON_STRING.matcher(json);
        while (m.find()) {
            out.add(m.group(1).replace("\\\"", "\"").replace("\\\\", "\\"));
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/resources/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/packs/oss-maintainer.yaml`

Component J: built-in pack. 113 lines, sha256 `63c0be04359cd776`.

```yaml
# Built-in pack: oss-maintainer. Every policy is advisory (warn) except mega-diff, which never flagged a
# merged PR in the calibration data and can be overridden by label. See
# docs/AI-integration/CHANGE-POLICY-ENGINE-DESIGN.md for the evidence behind each one.
budgets:
  default: { files: 50, lines: 1500 }
author_tiers:
  maintainer: [OWNER, MEMBER, COLLABORATOR]
policies:
  - id: over-budget
    when: { fact: budget.exceeded, eq: true }
    action: warn
    overridable: true
    message: "Change is over the review budget: {budget.detail}. Split it into smaller PRs, or ask a maintainer to apply an override label."
  # The only blocking pack policy. On the Airflow index (15,575 PRs) every one of the 27 PRs with
  # 1,000+ changed files was closed unmerged; most were small fixes built on the wrong base branch.
  - id: mega-diff
    when: { fact: files.changed, gte: 1000 }
    action: fail
    overridable: true
    message: "{files.changed} files changed. A diff this size is almost always a wrong base branch or an accidental merge. Rebase onto the target branch, or ask a maintainer to apply an override label for a real mass change."
  # 5,000 lines, not 1,000: on human Airflow PRs a small-change title with 1,000+ lines still merged at
  # 66.1% (baseline 75.9%); at 5,000+ lines it merged at 32.3%.
  - id: small-fix-huge-diff
    when:
      all:
        - { fact: intent.class, in: [typo, docs, fix, bump] }
        - { fact: lines.total_changed, gte: 5000 }
    action: warn
    overridable: true
    message: "Title reads as '{intent.class}' but the diff changes {lines.total_changed} lines. Check the base branch, drop unrelated changes, or retitle and explain."
  - id: large-new-code-unit
    when:
      any:
        - { fact: code.large_new_files, gte: 1 }
        - { fact: code.max_function_lines, gte: 150 }
        - { fact: code.stub_functions, gte: 2 }
    action: warn
    message: "New code is hard to review in one piece: {code.large_new_files} new source files of 500+ lines, longest new function about {code.max_function_lines} lines, {code.stub_functions} stub functions. Split it into smaller units and remove stubs."
  - id: small-intent-large-diff
    when:
      all:
        - { fact: intent.class, in: [typo, docs, fix, bump] }
        - { fact: files.reviewable, gt: 10 }
    action: warn
    overridable: true
    message: "Title reads as '{intent.class}' but {files.reviewable} reviewable files changed ({scope.area_list}). Keep a small fix small, or retitle and explain the wider change."
  - id: untested-source-change
    when:
      all:
        - { fact: lines.source_changed, gte: 200 }
        - { fact: tests.files_changed, eq: 0 }
    action: warn
    overridable: true
    message: "{lines.source_changed} source lines changed with no test changes. Add or update tests, or explain why none are needed."
  # CI definitions only. Calibration on Airflow showed build-infra (dev tooling, its tests) changes
  # routinely ride along with source changes in merged PRs, so build-infra is not part of this policy.
  - id: infra-with-source
    when:
      all:
        - { fact: files.by_category.ci, gte: 1 }
        - { fact: lines.source_changed, gt: 0 }
    action: warn
    message: "CI workflow files change together with source code. Send the CI change as its own PR."
  - id: ci-change-by-non-collaborator
    when:
      all:
        - { fact: meta.author_tier, eq: external }
        - any:
            - { fact: files.by_category.ci, gte: 1 }
            - { fact: files.by_category.build-infra, gte: 1 }
    action: warn
    message: "CI or build-infrastructure files changed by an author without write access. A maintainer must review the CI change before project CI runs it."
  - id: lockfile-change
    when:
      all:
        - { fact: files.by_category.lockfile, gte: 1 }
        - { fact: files.by_category.dependency-manifest, eq: 0 }
        - not: { fact: intent.class, eq: bump }
    action: warn
    message: "A lockfile changed without any dependency manifest change. Regenerate it in a dedicated dependency PR, or revert it."
  - id: formatting-churn
    when: { fact: churn.whitespace_only_files, gte: 3 }
    action: warn
    message: "{churn.whitespace_only_files} files change only whitespace or formatting. Revert formatting-only edits outside the change."
  - id: description-claims-mismatch
    when:
      any:
        - { fact: claims.tests_overclaim, gt: 2 }
        - all:
            - { fact: claims.tests_added, eq: true }
            - { fact: tests.files_changed, eq: 0 }
            - { fact: lines.source_changed, gt: 0 }
    action: warn
    message: "The description claims {claims.tests_count} tests or added tests, but the diff adds {tests.functions_added} test functions in {tests.files_changed} test files. Make the description match the diff."
  # Maintainers are exempt: calibration showed release, backport and CI-upgrade PRs by maintainers
  # (short template-free bodies) were most of the merged hits.
  - id: template-compliance
    when:
      all:
        - { fact: meta.author_tier, ne: maintainer }
        - any:
            - { fact: template.sections_missing, gt: 0 }
            - { fact: meta.body_words, lt: 15 }
    action: warn
    message: "The PR description is missing required content ({template.missing_list}; {meta.body_words} words). Explain why the change is needed and how it was tested."
  - id: disclosure-consistency
    when: { fact: claims.ai_disclosure_mismatch, eq: true }
    action: warn
    message: "A Generated-by trailer and the AI disclosure checkbox disagree. Fix the disclosure section."
  - id: behind-base
    when: { fact: git.behind_base, gt: 200 }
    action: warn
    message: "The branch is {git.behind_base} commits behind the base branch. Rebase before review."
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/main/resources/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/packs/airflow.yaml`

Component J: built-in pack. 21 lines, sha256 `faaae2c74be146f4`.

```yaml
# Built-in pack: airflow. Extends oss-maintainer with Apache Airflow paths and its proposed
# commit-message rule. All policies stay advisory.
extends: oss-maintainer
classification:
  build-infra: ["dev/breeze/**", "scripts/ci/**"]
  test: ["**/*.test.tsx", "**/*.test.ts", "**/tests/system/**"]
  generated: ["**/openapi-gen/**", "**/*.gen.ts", "**/_generated/**"]
areas:
  providers: ["providers/**"]
  ui: ["airflow-core/src/airflow/ui/**"]
  core: ["airflow-core/**"]
  task-sdk: ["task-sdk/**"]
  chart: ["chart/**", "helm-tests/**"]
  dev: ["dev/**", "scripts/**"]
policies:
  - id: commit-hygiene
    when:
      any:
        - { fact: git.conventional_prefix_commits, gt: 0 }
    action: warn
    message: "{git.conventional_prefix_commits} commit subjects use conventional-commit prefixes, which this project does not use. Reword the commits."
```

# Built-in pack: strict-agent. For repositories that accept agent PRs only for small tasks.
# Size and test policies block (plus mega-diff from the base pack); a maintainer override label still applies.
extends: oss-maintainer
budgets:
  default: { files: 20, lines: 600 }
policies:
  - { id: over-budget, action: fail }
  - { id: small-fix-huge-diff, action: fail }
  - { id: small-intent-large-diff, action: fail }
  - { id: untested-source-change, action: fail }

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/test/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/TextAndPathTest.java`

Component J: glob, diff, classifier and text tests. 140 lines, sha256 `ae4aad4d9a81e1b7`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class TextAndPathTest {

    private static boolean g(String glob, String path) {
        return Glob.any(path, Glob.compileAll(List.of(glob)));
    }

    @Test
    void globsAreStrict() {
        assertTrue(g("docs/**", "docs/a/b.md"));
        assertFalse(g("docs/**", "src/docs_util.py"), "no substring fallback");
        assertTrue(g("**/tests/**", "tests/a.py"));
        assertTrue(g("**/tests/**", "providers/x/tests/a.py"));
        assertFalse(g("**/tests/**", "src/pytests.py"));
        assertTrue(g("*.lock", "a/b/c.lock"), "slash-free pattern matches the file name anywhere");
        assertTrue(g("test_?.py", "x/test_a.py"));
        assertFalse(g("src/*.py", "src/a/b.py"));
        assertTrue(g("src/**", "src/a/b.py"));
        assertTrue(g("a+b(c).txt", "a+b(c).txt"), "regex metacharacters are literal");
        assertFalse(Glob.any("x", List.of()));
    }

    @Test
    void diffStatsReadsOnlyHunkLines() {
        String raw = "diff --git a/a.py b/a.py\r\n--- a/a.py\n+++ b/a.py\n@@ -1,2 +1,2 @@\n-old\n+new\n+++plus\n ctx\n"
                + "diff --git a/b.py b/b.py\nnew file mode 100644\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/b.py\n"
                + "diff --git weird\n@@\n+x\n";
        Map<String, DiffStats.FileDiff> m = DiffStats.parse(raw);
        assertEquals(List.of("new", "++plus"), m.get("a.py").added());
        assertEquals(List.of("old"), m.get("a.py").removed());
        assertEquals(0, m.get("b.py").changed(), "headers are not content");
        assertEquals(List.of("x"), m.get("weird").added());
        assertTrue(DiffStats.parse("@@\n+orphan\n").isEmpty(), "hunk without a file header is ignored");
    }

    @Test
    void whitespaceOnlyIgnoresMovedBlocks() {
        assertTrue(new DiffStats.FileDiff(List.of("a  =  1", ""), List.of("a=1")).whitespaceOnly());
        assertFalse(new DiffStats.FileDiff(List.of("b", "a"), List.of("a", "b")).whitespaceOnly(), "reordering is a change");
        assertFalse(DiffStats.FileDiff.EMPTY.whitespaceOnly());
    }

    @Test
    void classifierUsesProjectGlobsFirstAndAreaFallback() {
        Classifier c = new Classifier(Map.of("build-infra", List.of("dev/breeze/**"), "test", List.of("**/*.test.tsx")),
                new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("ui", List.of("ui/**"))), 2);
        assertEquals("lockfile", c.category("ui/package-lock.json"));
        assertEquals("build-infra", c.category("dev/breeze/x.py"));
        assertEquals("ci", c.category(".github/workflows/ci.yml"));
        assertEquals("ai-instruction", c.category("AGENTS.md"));
        assertEquals("dependency-manifest", c.category("providers/x/pyproject.toml"));
        assertEquals("test", c.category("ui/src/a.test.tsx"));
        assertEquals("test", c.category("src/test/java/FooTest.java"));
        assertEquals("docs", c.category("docs/x.rst"));
        assertEquals("config", c.category("a/b.yaml"));
        assertEquals("source", c.category("airflow/models/dag.py"));
        assertEquals("ui", c.area("ui/src/a.tsx"));
        assertEquals("airflow/models", c.area("airflow/models/dag.py"));
        assertEquals("a", c.area("a/b.py"));
        assertEquals("(root)", c.area("setup.py"));
    }

    @Test
    void intentClasses() {
        assertEquals("typo", MetaText.intent("Fix typo in docs"));
        assertEquals("docs", MetaText.intent("Update README"));
        assertEquals("bump", MetaText.intent("Bump ruff to 0.6"));
        assertEquals("test", MetaText.intent("Add unit tests for hook"));
        assertEquals("refactor", MetaText.intent("Refactor scheduler loop"));
        assertEquals("fix", MetaText.intent("Fix race in triggerer"));
        assertEquals("feature", MetaText.intent("Add support for X"));
        assertEquals("unknown", MetaText.intent("Scheduler loop"));
    }

    @Test
    void bodyFacts() {
        List<Pattern> jira = List.of(Pattern.compile("AIRFLOW-\\d+"));
        assertTrue(MetaText.linkedIssue("closes: #123", List.of()));
        assertTrue(MetaText.linkedIssue("see https://github.com/apache/airflow/issues/9", List.of()));
        assertFalse(MetaText.linkedIssue("color &#123; and a#1", List.of()));
        assertTrue(MetaText.linkedIssue("AIRFLOW-42", jira));
        assertFalse(MetaText.linkedIssue("nothing", jira));

        String body = "Intro <!-- hidden words here -->\nWas generative AI tooling used?\nreal text";
        String clean = MetaText.clean(body, List.of(Pattern.compile("^Was generative")));
        assertEquals("Intro \nreal text", clean);
        assertEquals(3, MetaText.words(clean));

        String tpl = "## Why\nBecause it breaks.\n## How was this tested\n<!-- fill -->\n**Notes**\nx";
        assertEquals(List.of("How was this tested", "Missing"),
                MetaText.missingSections(MetaText.clean(tpl, List.of()), List.of("Why", "How was this tested", "Missing")));
        assertEquals(List.of(), MetaText.missingSections("**Why**\nlast section text", List.of("Why")));
    }

    @Test
    void testClaims() {
        assertEquals(22, MetaText.testsClaimCount("Added 22 test cases, 22/22 passed (100%)"));
        assertEquals(5, MetaText.testsClaimCount("This PR has 5 new unit tests"));
        assertEquals(12, MetaText.testsClaimCount("12 tests were added"));
        assertEquals(0, MetaText.testsClaimCount("Unit tests for all operators (127 tests passing). 1111 passed"),
                "tests run are not tests added");
        assertEquals(0, MetaText.testsClaimCount("Python 3.12, fixes #12 new tests, 3 test files"));
        assertTrue(MetaText.testsAddedClaim("I added unit tests for the hook"));
        assertFalse(MetaText.testsAddedClaim("No new tests added, behaviour unchanged"));
        assertFalse(MetaText.testsAddedClaim("- [ ] Added tests\nnothing else"), "unchecked template items claim nothing");
        assertFalse(MetaText.testsAddedClaim("Refactors the loop"));
        assertFalse(MetaText.testsAddedClaim("Happy to add tests if you want"), "an offer is not a claim");
        assertEquals(4, MetaText.testFunctions(List.of("def test_a():", "    async def test_b(x):", "@Test",
                "func TestC(t *testing.T) {", "def helper():")));
        assertEquals(2, MetaText.testFunctions(List.of("it('works', () => {", "test.each([1])('x', () => {")));
    }

    @Test
    void disclosureConsistency() {
        assertTrue(MetaText.disclosureMismatch("- [ ] Yes\nGenerated-by: Claude Code"));
        assertTrue(MetaText.disclosureMismatch("- [ ] Yes\nGenerated-by: [Cursor]"), "tool named in brackets");
        assertFalse(MetaText.disclosureMismatch("- [x] Yes: Cursor IDE\nGenerated-by: [Tool Name]"),
                "ticked box with the tool named inline is a normal answer");
        assertFalse(MetaText.disclosureMismatch("- [x] Yes\nGenerated-by: Claude Code"));
        assertFalse(MetaText.disclosureMismatch("- [ ] Yes\nGenerated-by: [Tool Name]"), "template placeholder");
        assertFalse(MetaText.disclosureMismatch("- [ ] Yes\nGenerated-by: [Tool Name and Version]"), "Flink placeholder");
        assertFalse(MetaText.disclosureMismatch("Generated-by: Copilot"), "no checkbox in this template");
    }
}
```

/*

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class ConditionAndConfigTest {

    private static final Map<String, Object> FACTS = Map.of("files.reviewable", 12, "intent.class", "fix",
            "meta.labels", List.of("a", "b"), "scope.area_list", "core, ui", "budget.exceeded", true);

    private static Condition.Tri eval(Map<String, Object> node, Set<String> unknown) {
        return Condition.parse(node, Map.of("n", 10), "t").eval(FACTS, unknown);
    }

    private static Condition.Tri eval(Map<String, Object> node) {
        return eval(node, new HashSet<>());
    }

    @Test
    void leafOperators() {
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "gt", "${n}")));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "gte", 12)));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.FALSE, eval(Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "lt", 12)));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "lte", 12.0)));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "eq", 12.0)));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "intent.class", "eq", "FIX")));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "intent.class", "ne", "docs")));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "budget.exceeded", "eq", true)));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "intent.class", "in", List.of("typo", "fix"))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.FALSE, eval(Map.of("fact", "intent.class", "not_in", List.of("fix"))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "meta.labels", "contains", "b")));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "scope.area_list", "contains", "ui")));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "files.by_category.ci", "eq", 0)), "absent counts are zero");
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("fact", "files.by_area.core", "eq", 0)));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> eval(Map.of("fact", "intent.class", "gt", 1)));
    }

    @Test
    void threeValuedLogic() {
        Map<String, Object> unknownLeaf = Map.of("fact", "git.behind_base", "gt", 1);
        Map<String, Object> t = Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "gt", 1);
        Map<String, Object> f = Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "lt", 1);
        Set<String> unknown = new HashSet<>();
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.UNKNOWN, eval(unknownLeaf, unknown));
        assertEquals(Set.of("git.behind_base"), unknown);
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.FALSE, eval(Map.of("all", List.of(unknownLeaf, f))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.UNKNOWN, eval(Map.of("all", List.of(unknownLeaf, t))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("all", List.of(t, t))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("any", List.of(unknownLeaf, t))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.UNKNOWN, eval(Map.of("any", List.of(f, unknownLeaf))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.FALSE, eval(Map.of("any", List.of(f, f))));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.FALSE, eval(Map.of("not", t)));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.TRUE, eval(Map.of("not", f)));
        assertEquals(Condition.Tri.UNKNOWN, eval(Map.of("not", unknownLeaf)));
    }

    @Test
    void parseRejectsBadConditions() {
        Map<String, Object> withNull = new HashMap<>();
        withNull.put("fact", "files.reviewable");
        withNull.put("eq", null);
        List<Object> bad = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList("x", Map.of(), Map.of("all", List.of()), Map.of("any", "x"),
                Map.of("all", List.of(Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "gt", 1)), "x", 1),
                Map.of("not", Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "gt", 1), "x", 1),
                Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable"), Map.of("fact", "nope", "gt", 1),
                Map.of("fact", "files.by_category.nope", "gt", 1), Map.of("fact", "files.by_area.", "gt", 1),
                Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "between", 1), Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "in", 1),
                Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "eq", List.of(1)), Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "gt", "x"),
                Map.of("fact", "files.reviewable", "gt", "${missing}"), withNull,
                Map.of("eq", 1, "gt", 2)));
        for (Object node : bad) {
            assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Condition.parse(node, Map.of(), "t"), String.valueOf(node));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void renderAndJson() {
        Map<String, Object> facts = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        facts.put("files.reviewable", 3);
        facts.put("meta.labels", List.of("x\"y", "z"));
        facts.put("intent.class", "fix\nnow\u0001");
        facts.put("budget.exceeded", false);
        assertEquals("3 files, fix now, ?, 50, labels x\"y, z, 0, {open",
                Facts.render("{files.reviewable} files, {intent.class}, {git.commits}, {max}, labels {meta.labels}, "
                        + "{files.by_category.ci}, {open", facts, Map.of("max", 50)));
        assertEquals("a".repeat(200) + "...", Facts.sanitize("a".repeat(250)));
        assertEquals("{\"files.reviewable\":3,\"meta.labels\":[\"x\\\"y\",\"z\"],"
                + "\"intent.class\":\"fix\\u000anow\\u0001\",\"budget.exceeded\":false}", Facts.toJson(facts));
        assertEquals("\"a\\\\b\"", Facts.quote("a\\b"));
    }

    @Test
    void packsLoadAndMerge() {
        PolicyConfig oss = PolicyConfig.load(Map.of());
        assertEquals(14, oss.policies().size());
        assertEquals(List.of("mega-diff"), oss.policies().stream().filter(p -> !p.action().equals("warn"))
                .map(PolicyConfig.Policy::id).toList(), "mega-diff is the only blocking pack policy");
        assertEquals(50, oss.budgets().get("default").files());
        assertEquals("maintainer", oss.associationTier().get("COLLABORATOR"));
        assertFalse(oss.emitFacts());
        assertTrue(oss.gitFacts());

        PolicyConfig airflow = PolicyConfig.load(Map.of("pack", "airflow"));
        assertEquals(15, airflow.policies().size());
        assertEquals("build-infra", airflow.classifier().category("dev/breeze/x.py"));
        assertEquals("ui", airflow.classifier().area("airflow-core/src/airflow/ui/a.tsx"));
        assertEquals("core", airflow.classifier().area("airflow-core/src/airflow/models/dag.py"));

        PolicyConfig strict = PolicyConfig.load(Map.of("pack", "strict-agent"));
        assertEquals(5, strict.policies().stream().filter(p -> p.action().equals("fail")).count());
        assertEquals(20, strict.budgets().get("default").files());

        assertTrue(PolicyConfig.load(Map.of("pack", "none")).policies().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void projectOverridesPack() {
        Map<String, Object> cfg = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        cfg.put("params", Map.of("cap", 5));
        cfg.put("classification", Map.of("generated", List.of("gen/**")));
        cfg.put("areas", Map.of("core", List.of("core/**")));
        cfg.put("area_depth", 2);
        cfg.put("non_reviewable_categories", List.of("lockfile"));
        cfg.put("budgets", Map.of("external", Map.of("files", 10, "lines", 100), "external.core", Map.of("non_test_lines", 9)));
        cfg.put("author_tiers", Map.of("trusted", List.of("contributor")));
        cfg.put("template", Map.of("required_sections", List.of("Why"), "ignore_patterns", List.of("^Was")));
        cfg.put("linked_issue_patterns", List.of("AIRFLOW-\\d+"));
        cfg.put("overrides", Map.of("labels", List.of("big-ok"), "per_policy", Map.of("custom", List.of("custom-ok"))));
        cfg.put("policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "over-budget", "action", "fail"),
                Map.of("id", "custom", "when", Map.of("fact", "files.changed", "gt", "${cap}"), "overridable", false,
                        "override_labels", List.of("x"))));
        cfg.put("emit_facts", true);
        cfg.put("git_facts", false);
        cfg.put("git_timeout_ms", 50);
        cfg.put("rule_actions", Map.of());
        PolicyConfig p = PolicyConfig.load(cfg);
        assertEquals("generated", p.classifier().category("gen/a.py"));
        assertEquals("core", p.classifier().area("core/a/b.py"));
        assertEquals("x/y", p.classifier().area("x/y/z.py"));
        assertEquals(Set.of("lockfile"), p.nonReviewable());
        assertEquals(9, p.budgets().get("external.core").nonTestLines());
        assertEquals("trusted", p.associationTier().get("CONTRIBUTOR"));
        assertEquals(List.of("Why"), p.requiredSections());
        assertEquals(1, p.templateIgnore().size());
        assertEquals(1, p.linkedIssuePatterns().size());
        assertEquals(List.of("big-ok"), p.overrideLabels());
        PolicyConfig.Policy over = p.policies().stream().filter(x -> x.id().equals("over-budget")).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertEquals("fail", over.action());
        assertTrue(over.message().startsWith("Change is over the review budget"), "pack fields kept on partial override");
        PolicyConfig.Policy custom = p.policies().get(p.policies().size() - 1);
        assertEquals(List.of("x", "custom-ok"), custom.overrideLabels());
        assertFalse(custom.overridable());
        assertEquals("Change policy custom matched.", custom.message());
        assertTrue(p.emitFacts());
        assertFalse(p.gitFacts());
        assertEquals(50, p.gitTimeoutMs());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidConfig() {
        List<Map<String, Object>> bad = List.of(
                Map.of("nope", 1), Map.of("pack", "missing"), Map.of("pack", "../x"), Map.of("params", "x"),
                Map.of("classification", Map.of("source", List.of("a"))), Map.of("classification", Map.of("nope", List.of("a"))),
                Map.of("classification", Map.of("test", "a")), Map.of("areas", Map.of("a", List.of(" "))),
                Map.of("non_reviewable_categories", List.of("nope")), Map.of("area_depth", 0), Map.of("area_depth", "2"),
                Map.of("budgets", Map.of("default", Map.of("files", -1))), Map.of("budgets", Map.of("default", Map.of("size", 1))),
                Map.of("budgets", Map.of("default", "x")), Map.of("template", Map.of("ignore_patterns", List.of("("))),
                Map.of("policies", "x"), Map.of("policies", List.of("x")), Map.of("policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "Bad"))),
                Map.of("policies", List.of(Map.of("action", "warn"))),
                Map.of("policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "a", "colour", 1))),
                Map.of("policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "a", "action", "block", "when", Map.of("fact", "files.changed", "gt", 1)))),
                Map.of("policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "a"))),
                Map.of("policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "a", "when", Map.of("fact", "files.changed", "gt", 1), "overridable", "yes"))),
                Map.of("emit_facts", "true"), Map.of("linked_issue_patterns", "x"));
        for (Map<String, Object> cfg : bad) {
            assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> PolicyConfig.load(cfg), cfg.toString());
        }
        assertEquals(Map.of(), PolicyConfig.map(null, "x"));
    }
}
