### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/test/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/ChangePolicyGateTest.java`

Component J: gate tests. 361 lines, sha256 `8e77a1f59a365af7`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.AIVConfig;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.AIVContext;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.ChangedFile;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.Diff;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.Finding;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.GateResult;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.function.Function;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class ChangePolicyGateTest {

    /** Builds a unified diff and the matching changed-file list. */
    private static final class Change {
        final StringBuilder raw = new StringBuilder();
        final List<ChangedFile> files = new ArrayList<>();

        Change file(String path, ChangedFile.ChangeType type, List<String> plus, List<String> minus) {
            files.add(new ChangedFile(path, type, ""));
            raw.append("diff --git a/").append(path).append(" b/").append(path).append("\n--- a/").append(path)
                    .append("\n+++ b/").append(path).append("\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n");
            minus.forEach(l -> raw.append('-').append(l).append('\n'));
            plus.forEach(l -> raw.append('+').append(l).append('\n'));
            return this;
        }

        Change mod(String path, int lines) {
            List<String> plus = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < lines; i++) {
                plus.add("x" + i + " = " + i);
            }
            return file(path, ChangedFile.ChangeType.MODIFIED, plus, List.of());
        }

        Diff diff() {
            return new Diff("base", "head", files, raw.toString(), 0, 0, null, false);
        }
    }

    private static final GitHistory GIT = (ws, args) -> switch (String.join(" ", args)) {
        case "rev-list --count base..head" -> Optional.of("2\n");
        case "rev-list --count head..base" -> Optional.of("300\n");
        default -> Optional.of("feat: x\u001f\u001e\nFix thing\u001fbody text\u001e\n");
    };

    private static GateResult run(Map<String, Object> cfg, Change c, Map<String, Object> meta, GitHistory git) {
        var config = new AIVConfig(List.of(new AIVConfig.GateConfig(ChangePolicyGate.ID, true, cfg)), Map.of());
        AIVContext ctx = new AIVContext(Paths.get("."), c.diff(), config, meta);
        return new ChangePolicyGate(k -> null, git).evaluate(ctx);
    }

    private static List<String> rules(GateResult r) {
        return r.getFindings().stream().map(Finding::getRuleId).toList();
    }

    private static Map<String, Object> facts(Map<String, Object> cfg, Change c, Map<String, Object> meta, GitHistory git) {
        PolicyConfig pc = PolicyConfig.load(cfg);
        AIVContext ctx = new AIVContext(Paths.get("."), c.diff(), new AIVConfig(List.of(), Map.of()), meta);
        return FactCollector.collect(c.diff(), c.files, new ChangePolicyGate(k -> null, git).meta(ctx), pc, git, Paths.get("."));
    }

    @Test
    void smallCleanChangePassesAndReportsSkippedFacts() {
        Change c = new Change().mod("src/a.py", 5).mod("tests/test_a.py", 3);
        GateResult r = run(Map.of(), c, Map.of(), (ws, a) -> Optional.empty());
        assertEquals("change-policy", new ChangePolicyGate().getId());
        assertTrue(r.isPassed());
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().isEmpty());
        assertTrue(r.getMessage().startsWith("0 change policies matched (0 blocking); skipped, facts unavailable: "), r.getMessage());
        assertTrue(r.getMessage().contains("behind-base (git.behind_base)"), r.getMessage());
        assertTrue(r.getMessage().contains("template-compliance (meta.author_tier, meta.body_words, template.sections_missing)"),
                r.getMessage());
    }

    private static Change messyChange() {
        Change c = new Change();
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
            c.mod("src/m" + i + ".py", 20);
        }
        c.mod(".github/workflows/ci.yml", 1).mod("uv.lock", 50);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            c.file("fmt/f" + i + ".py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.MODIFIED, List.of("a = 1"), List.of("a=1"));
        }
        return c;
    }

    private static Map<String, Object> messyMeta() {
        return Map.of("pr_title", "Fix typo", "pr_body", "Added 22 test cases, all pass.\n- [ ] Yes\nGenerated-by: Claude Code",
                "pr_author_association", " contributor ", "pr_draft", "false", "pr_labels", List.of("unrelated"));
    }

    @Test
    void everyPackPolicyFiresAsAdvisoryOnAMessyChange() {
        Map<String, Object> cfg = Map.of("budgets", Map.of("default", Map.of("files", 5)),
                "template", Map.of("required_sections", List.of("Why")),
                "overrides", Map.of("labels", List.of("large-change-approved")));
        GateResult r = run(cfg, messyChange(), messyMeta(), GIT);
        assertFalse(r.isPassed());
        assertFalse(r.blocksCi(), "pack policies are advisory");
        assertEquals(List.of("policy.over-budget", "policy.small-intent-large-diff", "policy.untested-source-change",
                "policy.infra-with-source", "policy.ci-change-by-non-collaborator", "policy.lockfile-change",
                "policy.formatting-churn", "policy.description-claims-mismatch", "policy.template-compliance",
                "policy.disclosure-consistency", "policy.behind-base"), rules(r));
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().stream().allMatch(f -> f.getMessage().startsWith("[WARN] ") && f.getFilePath().equals("(diff)")));
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().get(0).getMessage().contains("13 reviewable files > 5 (default)"), r.getFindings().get(0).getMessage());
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().get(1).getMessage().contains("Title reads as 'typo' but 13 reviewable files"));
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().get(10).getMessage().contains("300 commits behind"));
        assertEquals("11 change policies matched (0 blocking)", r.getMessage());
    }

    @Test
    void failOverrideRuleActionsAndFactsNote() {
        Map<String, Object> cfg = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        cfg.put("overrides", Map.of("labels", List.of("large-change-approved"), "per_policy",
                Map.of("lockfile-change", List.of("deps-ok"))));
        cfg.put("policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "over-budget", "action", "fail"),
                Map.of("id", "untested-source-change", "action", "fail", "overridable", false)));
        cfg.put("budgets", Map.of("default", Map.of("files", 5)));
        cfg.put("rule_actions", Map.of("policy.formatting-churn", "off"));
        cfg.put("emit_facts", true);
        Map<String, Object> meta = new HashMap<>(messyMeta());
        meta.put("pr_labels", List.of("large-change-approved", "deps-ok"));
        GateResult r = run(cfg, messyChange(), meta, GIT);
        assertFalse(r.isPassed());
        assertTrue(r.blocksCi());
        List<String> rules = rules(r);
        assertEquals("change-policy.override", rules.get(0));
        assertEquals("[POLICY-OVERRIDE] over-budget by label large-change-approved", r.getFindings().get(0).getMessage());
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().get(2).getMessage().startsWith("[FAIL] 240 source lines changed"), r.getFindings().get(2).getMessage());
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().stream().anyMatch(f -> f.getMessage().equals("[POLICY-OVERRIDE] lockfile-change by label deps-ok")));
        assertFalse(rules.contains("policy.formatting-churn"));
        Finding note = r.getFindings().get(r.getFindings().size() - 1);
        assertEquals(ChangePolicyGate.FACTS_RULE, note.getRuleId());
        assertTrue(note.getMessage().startsWith("{\"files.changed\":17,\"files.reviewable\":13,"), note.getMessage());
        assertTrue(note.getMessage().contains("\"meta.labels\":[\"large-change-approved\",\"deps-ok\"]"));
        assertEquals("change-policy.override", rules.get(1), "small-intent-large-diff is overridable in the pack");
        assertEquals("policy.infra-with-source", rules.get(3), "not overridable: the project-wide label does not apply");
        assertTrue(r.getMessage().startsWith("7 change policies matched (1 blocking)"), r.getMessage());
        assertFalse(r.getAdvisoryRules().contains("policy.untested-source-change"));
        assertTrue(r.getAdvisoryRules().containsAll(List.of("policy.infra-with-source", ChangePolicyGate.OVERRIDE_RULE,
                ChangePolicyGate.FACTS_RULE)), r.getAdvisoryRules().toString());
    }

    @Test
    void factsCountKindsAreasAndHistory() {
        Change c = new Change().mod("core/a.py", 2).mod("core/tests/test_a.py", 1).mod("ui/b.ts", 2)
                .file("new.py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.ADDED, List.of("def test_new():"), List.of())
                .file("old.py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.DELETED, List.of(), List.of("x"))
                .file("docs/r.md", ChangedFile.ChangeType.MODIFIED, List.of("doc"), List.of());
        c.files.add(new ChangedFile("moved.py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.RENAMED, "", "orig.py", null, null, null, null,
                null, null, null));
        c.files.add(new ChangedFile(null, ChangedFile.ChangeType.MODIFIED, ""));
        Map<String, Object> cfg = Map.of("areas", Map.of("core", List.of("core/**"), "ui", List.of("ui/**")),
                "budgets", Map.of("external", Map.of("files", 100), "external.core", Map.of("non_test_lines", 1),
                        "ui", Map.of("lines", 1, "files", 1)));
        Map<String, Object> f = facts(cfg, c, Map.of("pr_author_association", "NONE"), GIT);
        assertEquals(7, f.get("files.changed"));
        assertEquals(6, f.get("files.reviewable"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("files.added"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("files.deleted"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("files.renamed"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("files.by_area.ui"));
        assertEquals(8, f.get("lines.changed"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("lines.deleted"));
        assertEquals(6, f.get("lines.source_changed"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("lines.test_changed"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("lines.docs_changed"));
        assertEquals(7, f.get("lines.non_test_changed"));
        assertEquals("(root), core, docs, ui", f.get("scope.area_list"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("tests.files_changed"));
        assertEquals(0, f.get("tests.functions_added"), "def test_ in a non-test file is not counted");
        assertEquals(2, f.get("tests.source_areas_without_tests"), "(root) and ui have source but no tests");
        assertEquals(true, f.get("budget.exceeded"));
        assertEquals("core: 2 non-test lines > 1 (external.core); ui: 2 changed lines > 1 (ui)", f.get("budget.detail"));
        assertEquals("external", f.get("meta.author_tier"));
        assertEquals(2, f.get("git.commits"));
        assertEquals(300, f.get("git.behind_base"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("git.commits_without_body"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("git.conventional_prefix_commits"));
        assertNull(f.get("intent.class"));
        assertNull(f.get("meta.body_words"));

        Map<String, Object> maintainer = facts(cfg, c, Map.of("pr_author_association", "OWNER"), (ws, a) -> Optional.of("abc"));
        assertEquals("maintainer", maintainer.get("meta.author_tier"));
        assertEquals("ui: 2 changed lines > 1 (ui)", maintainer.get("budget.detail"), "no maintainer budget: default applies");
        assertNull(maintainer.get("git.commits"), "non-numeric output is unknown");
        assertEquals(1, maintainer.get("git.commits_without_body"));

        Map<String, Object> noGit = new LinkedHashMap<>(cfg);
        noGit.put("git_facts", false);
        assertNull(facts(noGit, c, Map.of(), GIT).get("git.commits"));
        Change noRefs = new Change().mod("a.py", 1);
        Diff blankRefs = new Diff(" ", "head", noRefs.files, noRefs.raw.toString(), 0, 0, null, false);
        assertNull(FactCollector.collect(blankRefs, noRefs.files, new FactCollector.Meta(null, null, null, null, null),
                PolicyConfig.load(Map.of()), GIT, Paths.get(".")).get("git.commits"));
        Diff nullRefs = new Diff(null, null, noRefs.files, noRefs.raw.toString(), 0, 0, null, false);
        assertNull(FactCollector.collect(nullRefs, noRefs.files, new FactCollector.Meta(null, null, null, null, null),
                PolicyConfig.load(Map.of()), GIT, Paths.get(".")).get("git.commits"));
        Diff headless = new Diff("base", "", noRefs.files, noRefs.raw.toString(), 0, 0, null, false);
        assertNull(FactCollector.collect(headless, noRefs.files, new FactCollector.Meta(null, null, null, null, null),
                PolicyConfig.load(Map.of()), GIT, Paths.get(".")).get("git.commits"));
        Diff headNull = new Diff("base", null, noRefs.files, noRefs.raw.toString(), 0, 0, null, false);
        assertNull(FactCollector.collect(headNull, noRefs.files, new FactCollector.Meta(null, null, null, null, null),
                PolicyConfig.load(Map.of()), GIT, Paths.get(".")).get("git.commits"));
    }

    @Test
    void wholeBudgetLimitsAndClaims() {
        Change c = new Change().mod("src/a.py", 30).mod("tests/test_a.py", 0)
                .file("tests/test_b.py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.MODIFIED, List.of("def test_one():", "def test_two():"), List.of());
        Map<String, Object> cfg = Map.of("budgets", Map.of("default", Map.of("lines", 10, "non_test_lines", 5)));
        Map<String, Object> f = facts(cfg, c, Map.of("pr_title", "Add thing", "pr_body", "Adds 3 tests. Fixes #4\n"
                + "<!-- Was generative AI used --> ## Notes"), GIT);
        assertEquals("32 changed lines > 10 (default); 30 non-test lines > 5 (default)", f.get("budget.detail"));
        assertEquals(2, f.get("tests.functions_added"));
        assertEquals(3, f.get("claims.tests_count"));
        assertEquals(1, f.get("claims.tests_overclaim"));
        assertEquals(true, f.get("claims.tests_added"));
        assertEquals(true, f.get("meta.linked_issue"));
        assertEquals("none missing", f.get("template.missing_list"));
        assertEquals(false, f.get("claims.ai_disclosure_mismatch"));
        assertEquals("feature", f.get("intent.class"));

        Map<String, Object> within = facts(Map.of(), new Change().mod("src/a.py", 1), Map.of("pr_body", "no claims"), GIT);
        assertEquals(false, within.get("budget.exceeded"));
        assertEquals("within budget", within.get("budget.detail"));
        assertEquals(0, within.get("claims.tests_overclaim"));
        assertEquals(false, within.get("meta.linked_issue"));
    }

    @Test
    void megaDiffBlocksAndCanBeOverridden() {
        Change c = new Change();
        for (int i = 0; i < 1000; i++) {
            c.files.add(new ChangedFile("pkg/f" + i + ".py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.MODIFIED, ""));
        }
        Map<String, Object> cfg = Map.of("overrides", Map.of("labels", List.of("mass-change-ok")));
        GateResult blocked = run(cfg, c, Map.of(), (ws, a) -> Optional.empty());
        assertTrue(blocked.blocksCi());
        assertEquals("policy.mega-diff", blocked.getFindings().get(0).getRuleId());
        assertTrue(blocked.getFindings().get(0).getMessage().startsWith("[FAIL] 1000 files changed."));
        GateResult overridden = run(cfg, c, Map.of("pr_labels", List.of("mass-change-ok")), (ws, a) -> Optional.empty());
        assertFalse(overridden.blocksCi());
        assertEquals("[POLICY-OVERRIDE] mega-diff by label mass-change-ok", overridden.getFindings().get(0).getMessage());
    }

    @Test
    void smallFixHugeDiffUsesTheRawDiffSize() {
        Change c = new Change().mod("src/a.py", 3);
        var config = new AIVConfig(List.of(new AIVConfig.GateConfig(ChangePolicyGate.ID, true, Map.of())), Map.of());
        Diff big = new Diff("base", "head", c.files, c.raw.toString(), 4500, 600, null, false);
        GateResult r = new ChangePolicyGate(k -> null, (ws, a) -> Optional.empty()).evaluate(
                new AIVContext(Paths.get("."), big, config, Map.of("pr_title", "Fix typo", "pr_body", "A long enough body "
                        + "that explains the reason for this change and how it was tested by hand locally today.")));
        assertEquals(List.of("policy.small-fix-huge-diff"), rules(r));
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().get(0).getMessage().contains("the diff changes 5100 lines"), r.getFindings().get(0).getMessage());
    }

    @Test
    void largeNewCodeUnits() {
        List<String> longFn = new ArrayList<>(List.of("class Big:", "    def run(self):"));
        for (int i = 0; i < 160; i++) {
            longFn.add("        x" + i + " = " + i);
        }
        longFn.addAll(List.of("def stub_a():", "    \"\"\"Doc.\"\"\"", "", "    pass", "func stubB() {", "    panic(\"not implemented\")",
                "fn real() {", "    let x = 1;", "def trailing():"));
        int[] shape = FactCollector.codeShape(longFn);
        assertEquals(160, shape[0]);
        assertEquals(2, shape[1]);
        assertEquals(0, FactCollector.codeShape(List.of("x = 1", "class A:", "    y = 2"))[0], "class bodies are not functions");

        Change c = new Change().file("src/new_module.py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.ADDED, longFn, List.of());
        Map<String, Object> f = facts(Map.of(), c, Map.of(), GIT);
        assertEquals(0, f.get("code.large_new_files"), "171 lines is under the 500-line threshold");
        assertEquals(160, f.get("code.max_function_lines"));
        assertEquals(2, f.get("code.stub_functions"));
        Change big = new Change().mod("src/a.py", 0);
        big.files.clear();
        big.file("src/huge.py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.ADDED, java.util.Collections.nCopies(500, "y = 1"), List.of())
                .file("tests/test_huge.py", ChangedFile.ChangeType.ADDED, java.util.Collections.nCopies(600, "z = 1"), List.of());
        Map<String, Object> g = facts(Map.of(), big, Map.of(), GIT);
        assertEquals(1, g.get("code.large_new_files"), "test files do not count");
        GateResult r = run(Map.of(), c, Map.of(), (ws, a) -> Optional.empty());
        assertTrue(rules(r).contains("policy.large-new-code-unit"), rules(r).toString());
    }

    @Test
    void metadataSources() {
        Map<String, String> env = Map.of("AIV_PR_TITLE", "Env title", "AIV_PR_LABELS", "[\"a\\\"b\",\"c\"]",
                "AIV_PR_DRAFT", " TRUE ", "AIV_PR_AUTHOR_ASSOCIATION", "MEMBER");
        ChangePolicyGate gate = new ChangePolicyGate(env::get, null);
        AIVContext empty = new AIVContext(Paths.get("."), new Diff("a", "b", List.of(), ""), new AIVConfig(List.of(), Map.of()));
        FactCollector.Meta m = gate.meta(empty);
        assertEquals("Env title", m.title());
        assertEquals("", m.body(), "a PR run with no description has an empty body, not an unknown one");
        assertEquals(List.of("a\"b", "c"), m.labels());
        assertEquals(true, m.draft());
        assertEquals("MEMBER", m.authorAssociation());

        FactCollector.Meta fromMeta = gate.meta(empty.withMetadata("pr_labels", "[]").withMetadata("pr_title", " "));
        assertEquals(List.of(), fromMeta.labels());
        assertEquals("Env title", fromMeta.title(), "blank metadata falls back to the environment");

        Function<String, String> none = k -> null;
        FactCollector.Meta local = new ChangePolicyGate(none, null).meta(empty);
        assertNull(local.title());
        assertNull(local.body());
        assertNull(local.labels());
        assertNull(local.draft());
        assertNull(local.authorAssociation());
        assertNull(new ChangePolicyGate(k -> " ", null).meta(empty).labels());
        assertEquals(List.of(), ChangePolicyGate.jsonStrings("null"));
    }

    @Test
    void excludedFilesCountTowardsReviewCost() {
        Change c = new Change().mod("src/a.py", 1).mod("gen/b.py", 1);
        ChangedFile excluded = c.files.remove(1);
        var config = new AIVConfig(List.of(new AIVConfig.GateConfig(ChangePolicyGate.ID, true,
                Map.of("emit_facts", true, "pack", "none"))), Map.of());
        AIVContext ctx = new AIVContext(Paths.get("."), c.diff(), config,
                Map.of(AIVContext.EXCLUDED_CHANGED_FILES, List.of("ignored", excluded)));
        GateResult r = new ChangePolicyGate(k -> null, (ws, a) -> Optional.empty()).evaluate(ctx);
        assertTrue(r.isPassed());
        assertEquals("0 change policies matched (0 blocking)", r.getMessage());
        assertTrue(r.getFindings().get(0).getMessage().contains("\"files.changed\":2"), r.getFindings().get(0).getMessage());
    }

    @Test
    void defaultConstructorUsesRealGitAndToleratesNoRepository(@TempDir Path dir) {
        Change c = new Change().mod("a.py", 1);
        var config = new AIVConfig(List.of(new AIVConfig.GateConfig(ChangePolicyGate.ID, true,
                Map.of("pack", "none", "git_timeout_ms", 5000))), Map.of());
        GateResult r = new ChangePolicyGate().evaluate(new AIVContext(dir, c.diff(), config));
        assertTrue(r.isPassed());
        GateResult noEntry = new ChangePolicyGate(k -> null, (ws, a) -> Optional.empty())
                .evaluate(new AIVContext(dir, c.diff(), new AIVConfig(List.of(), Map.of())));
        assertTrue(noEntry.isPassed(), noEntry.getMessage());
    }
}
```

Component J: real git tests. 50 lines, sha256 `44535c1eabde987c`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class GitHistoryTest {

    private static void git(Path dir, String... args) throws Exception {
        List<String> cmd = new java.util.ArrayList<>(List.of("git", "-c", "user.name=t", "-c", "user.email=t@example.com",
                "-c", "commit.gpgsign=false"));
        cmd.addAll(List.of(args));
        Process p = new ProcessBuilder(cmd).directory(dir.toFile()).redirectErrorStream(true).start();
        p.getInputStream().readAllBytes();
        assertEquals(0, p.waitFor(), String.join(" ", cmd));
    }

    @Test
    void readsHistoryFromARealRepository(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        git(dir, "init", "-q");
        Files.writeString(dir.resolve("a.txt"), "a");
        git(dir, "add", "a.txt");
        git(dir, "commit", "-q", "-m", "first");
        Files.writeString(dir.resolve("a.txt"), "b");
        git(dir, "commit", "-q", "-am", "feat: second", "-m", "with body");
        GitHistory h = GitHistory.process(10_000);
        assertEquals(Optional.of("2"), h.run(dir, List.of("rev-list", "--count", "HEAD")).map(String::strip));
        assertEquals(Optional.empty(), h.run(dir, List.of("rev-list", "--count", "nope..HEAD")), "git error is empty");
    }

    @Test
    void failuresAreEmpty(@TempDir Path dir) {
        assertTrue(GitHistory.git(dir.resolve("missing"), List.of("status"), 5_000).isEmpty(), "cannot start");
        // Not in the temp dir: on Windows the git wrapper's child can hold the directory briefly after the kill.
        assertTrue(GitHistory.git(Path.of("."), List.of("hash-object", "--stdin"), 300).isEmpty(),
                "timeout while waiting on stdin");
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/src/test/java/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/CorpusCalibrationRunner.java`

Component J: calibration entry point (test scope). 103 lines, sha256 `e367d6c364d0dd17`.

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy;

import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.AIVConfig;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.AIVContext;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.ChangedFile;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.Diff;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.Finding;
import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.model.GateResult;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Runs the real {@link ChangePolicyGate} over stored corpus PRs for calibration. Not a unit test: invoked
 * by {@code benchmarks/airflow/scripts/run-change-policy-calibration.py}, which prepares the input.
 *
 * <p>Input TSV columns: id, pack, base64 title, base64 body (empty when absent), base64 unified diff,
 * base64 file list ({@code status\tpath\told_path} per line), total additions, total deletions (the
 * numstat totals behind {@code lines.total_changed}). Output TSV: id, gate outcome, fired policy ids,
 * skipped policy ids, facts JSON.
 */
public final class CorpusCalibrationRunner {

    private CorpusCalibrationRunner() {
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        Base64.Decoder b64 = Base64.getDecoder();
        Map<String, AIVConfig> configs = new HashMap<>();
        // No checkout is available, so git history facts are off; PR metadata comes from the corpus.
        ChangePolicyGate gate = new ChangePolicyGate(k -> null, (ws, a) -> Optional.empty());
        try (BufferedReader in = Files.newBufferedReader(Path.of(args[0]), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
             BufferedWriter out = Files.newBufferedWriter(Path.of(args[1]), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            String line;
            while ((line = in.readLine()) != null) {
                String[] c = line.split("\t", -1);
                String title = new String(b64.decode(c[2]), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                String body = c[3].isEmpty() ? "" : new String(b64.decode(c[3]), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                String raw = new String(b64.decode(c[4]), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                int additions = c.length > 6 ? Integer.parseInt(c[6]) : 0;
                int deletions = c.length > 7 ? Integer.parseInt(c[7]) : 0;
                List<ChangedFile> files = new ArrayList<>();
                for (String f : new String(b64.decode(c[5]), StandardCharsets.UTF_8).split("\n")) {
                    String[] p = f.split("\t", -1);
                    if (p.length >= 2) {
                        files.add(new ChangedFile(p[1], type(p[0]), "", p.length > 2 && !p[2].isEmpty() ? p[2] : null,
                                null, null, null, null, null, null, null));
                    }
                }
                AIVConfig config = configs.computeIfAbsent(c[1], pack -> new AIVConfig(List.of(new AIVConfig.GateConfig(
                        ChangePolicyGate.ID, true, Map.of("pack", pack, "git_facts", false, "emit_facts", true,
                        // The corpus has no author association, so template-compliance (maintainers exempt) is
                        // skipped; this calibration-only variant measures the same text rule for every author.
                        "policies", List.of(Map.of("id", "calibration-template-any-author", "action", "warn",
                                "when", Map.of("any", List.of(Map.of("fact", "template.sections_missing", "gt", 0),
                                        Map.of("fact", "meta.body_words", "lt", 15)))))))), Map.of()));
                Map<String, Object> meta = new HashMap<>();
                meta.put("pr_title", title);
                meta.put("pr_body", body.isEmpty() ? " " : body);
                AIVContext ctx = new AIVContext(Path.of("."), new Diff("base", "head", files, raw, additions, deletions, null, false),
                        config, meta);
                GateResult r = gate.evaluate(ctx);
                List<String> fired = new ArrayList<>();
                String facts = "{}";
                for (Finding f : r.getFindings()) {
                    if (f.getRuleId().startsWith("policy.")) {
                        fired.add(f.getRuleId().substring(7));
                    } else if (f.getRuleId().equals(ChangePolicyGate.FACTS_RULE)) {
                        facts = f.getMessage();
                    }
                }
                String skipped = r.getMessage().contains("skipped, facts unavailable: ")
                        ? r.getMessage().substring(r.getMessage().indexOf("unavailable: ") + 13) : "";
                out.write(c[0] + "\t" + (r.isPassed() ? "pass" : r.blocksCi() ? "fail" : "advisory") + "\t"
                        + String.join(",", fired) + "\t" + skipped.replace('\t', ' ') + "\t" + facts + "\n");
            }
        }
    }

    private static ChangedFile.ChangeType type(String status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case "added" -> ChangedFile.ChangeType.ADDED;
            case "removed" -> ChangedFile.ChangeType.DELETED;
            case "renamed" -> ChangedFile.ChangeType.RENAMED;
            case "copied" -> ChangedFile.ChangeType.COPIED;
            default -> ChangedFile.ChangeType.MODIFIED;
        };
    }
}
```

### `aiv-plugin-change-policy/README.md`

Component J: module README. 126 lines, sha256 `099f6bb8ea9c635b`.

````markdown
# Module: `aiv-plugin-change-policy` (gate `change-policy`)

The change policy gate lets a project say, in its pinned AIV config, what a reviewable pull request
looks like: a size budget by author tier and area, tests for source changes, a title that matches the
diff, a description that does not overclaim tests, and template and history rules. It computes
deterministic facts about the change and evaluates declarative policies over them. Design and evidence:
[CHANGE-POLICY-ENGINE-DESIGN.md](../docs/AI-integration/CHANGE-POLICY-ENGINE-DESIGN.md) and
[MAINTAINER-PAIN-AND-PR-PATTERNS-ANALYSIS.md](../docs/AI-integration/MAINTAINER-PAIN-AND-PR-PATTERNS-ANALYSIS.md).

## Architecture role

Default-off gate (listed in `DEFAULT_OFF_GATES`). It reads the diff in `AIVContext` (including files
that `exclude_paths` removed, because they still cost review time), PR metadata from the CI
environment, and read-only `git` history. It never calls a model and never writes anything.

| Class | Role |
|---|---|
| `Classifier`, `Glob` | one category and one area per path; strict globs with no substring fallback |
| `DiffStats` | added and removed lines per file from the unified diff |
| `MetaText` | intent, linked issue, template sections, test claims, disclosure consistency |
| `GitHistory` | `git rev-list` and `git log`; any failure makes the fact unknown |
| `FactCollector` | builds the fact map, including `budget.exceeded` |
| `Condition` | parses and evaluates `all` / `any` / `not` / leaf conditions, three-valued |
| `PolicyConfig` | loads the built-in pack and overlays the project config, with validation |
| `ChangePolicyGate` | evaluates policies, overrides, rule actions, findings |

## Runtime flow

1. Load the pack (`oss-maintainer` by default) and overlay the project config. Invalid config is exit 2.
2. Read PR metadata: `AIV_PR_TITLE`, `AIV_PR_BODY`, `AIV_PR_LABELS` (JSON array),
   `AIV_PR_AUTHOR_ASSOCIATION`, `AIV_PR_DRAFT`. The composite action exports all five.
3. Collect facts. Missing inputs leave facts unknown (for example, no PR metadata in a local run).
4. Evaluate each policy. TRUE fires, FALSE passes, UNKNOWN skips and is listed in the gate message.
5. A fired policy with a matching override label becomes a `change-policy.override` notice.
6. Any fired `fail` policy fails the gate; otherwise fired `warn` policies make it advisory.

## Configuration

```yaml
gates:
  - id: change-policy
    enabled: true
    config:
      pack: airflow                  # oss-maintainer (default), airflow, strict-agent, none
      budgets:
        default: { files: 50, lines: 1500 }
        external: { files: 30, lines: 800 }
        external.core: { non_test_lines: 200 }
      template: { required_sections: ["Why", "How was this tested"] }
      overrides:
        labels: [large-change-approved]                     # applies to overridable policies
        per_policy: { untested-source-change: [tests-not-applicable] }
      policies:
        - { id: untested-source-change, action: fail }      # partial override of a pack policy
        - id: no-docs-only-bumps
          when: { all: [ { fact: intent.class, eq: bump }, { fact: lines.docs_changed, gt: 0 } ] }
          action: warn
          message: "Bump PR changes {lines.docs_changed} docs lines."
      emit_facts: false              # true adds a change-policy.facts note with the JSON snapshot
```

| Key | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `pack` | built-in pack to start from |
| `classification`, `areas`, `area_depth` | extra globs per category; named areas; fallback depth (default 1) |
| `non_reviewable_categories` | default `lockfile`, `generated`, `vendored`, `binary` |
| `budgets` | keys `default`, `<tier>`, `<area>`, `<tier>.<area>`; limits `files`, `lines`, `non_test_lines` |
| `author_tiers` | tier name to GitHub associations; unlisted associations are `external` |
| `template` | `required_sections`, `ignore_patterns` (regexes for boilerplate lines) |
| `linked_issue_patterns` | extra regexes, for example `AIRFLOW-\d+`; `#123` and issue URLs always count |
| `overrides` | project-wide `labels`; `per_policy` labels |
| `policies` | merged by `id` with the pack, field by field |
| `params` | values referenced as `${name}` in conditions |
| `git_facts`, `git_timeout_ms` | history facts on (default) and the per-command timeout |

Facts and operators are listed in the design doc section 4. Unknown facts or operators are errors.

## Built-in policies

`oss-maintainer` ships 14 policies. All are `warn` except `mega-diff` (1,000+ changed files, `fail`,
overridable), which never flagged a merged PR in the Airflow calibration. Size and shape policies:
`mega-diff`, `over-budget`, `small-fix-huge-diff` (small-change title, 5,000+ total lines),
`small-intent-large-diff`, `untested-source-change`, `large-new-code-unit` (new 500+ line file, 150+
line function or stub bodies). The `airflow` pack adds `commit-hygiene`; `strict-agent` makes the size
and test policies blocking.

## Findings

- `policy.<id>`: file `(diff)`, message prefixed `[WARN]` or `[FAIL]`, rendered from the policy message.
- `change-policy.override`: `[POLICY-OVERRIDE] <id> by label <label>`.
- `change-policy.facts`: only with `emit_facts: true`.

`rule_actions` (for example `policy.formatting-churn: off`) and `unsuppressible_rules` apply as for any gate.

## Interactions

- The composite action (`action.yml`) exports the PR metadata variables.
- Labels and fix briefs use the normal AIV outputs; the gate adds no new comment of its own.
- Magpie and the review ledger can read `change-policy.facts` from the JSON report.

## Failure behavior

Configuration errors raise `IllegalArgumentException` (exit 2). Git failures, shallow clones and
missing metadata never fail the gate; the affected policies are skipped and named in the message.

## Extension points

New facts go in `FactCollector` and `Facts.NAMES`; new packs are YAML files under
`src/main/resources/io/github/vaquarkhan/aiv/plugin/changepolicy/packs/` and may `extends` another pack.

## Tests and build

Unit tests cover globs, diff parsing, classification, text facts, the condition language, pack merging,
config validation, every pack policy, overrides, rule actions, history facts against a real temporary
repository, and git failure paths. `CorpusCalibrationRunner` (test scope) runs the gate on stored corpus
PRs for `benchmarks/airflow/scripts/run-change-policy-calibration.py`.

```bash
mvn -pl aiv-plugin-change-policy verify
python benchmarks/airflow/scripts/run-change-policy-calibration.py
```

## Boundary

Policies describe changes, not authors. A fired policy says a PR is expensive or inconsistent to
review, never that it was AI-written.
````

### `benchmarks/airflow/scripts/run-change-policy-calibration.py`

Component J: calibration on the corpus. 255 lines, sha256 `7353dd4a503592e2`.

```python
# Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Calibrate the change-policy gate on every stored PR patch in aiv-airflow-bench.

Runs the real Java gate (aiv-plugin-change-policy, through CorpusCalibrationRunner) on each PR, then
reports per policy: flag rate on merged and on closed-unmerged PRs (Wilson 95% interval on merged),
lift (closed rate / merged rate), merge rate when flagged, and example PRs for manual review.

    mvn -o -pl aiv-plugin-change-policy -am install      # builds classes and test classes
    python benchmarks/airflow/scripts/run-change-policy-calibration.py [--bench ../aiv-airflow-bench]

Writes benchmarks/airflow/results/change-policy-calibration-latest.json and
benchmarks/airflow/reports/change-policy-calibration-latest.md. Git history facts are unavailable
without checkouts, and the corpus has no author_association, so behind-base and
ci-change-by-non-collaborator are reported as skipped.
"""
import argparse
import base64
import glob
import json
import math
import os
import re
import subprocess
import tempfile
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--bench", default=str(ROOT.parent / "aiv-airflow-bench"))
ap.add_argument("--ceiling", type=float, default=0.02, help="merged-PR flag-rate ceiling for promotion")
ap.add_argument("--out-json", default=str(ROOT / "benchmarks/airflow/results/change-policy-calibration-latest.json"))
ap.add_argument("--out-md", default=str(ROOT / "benchmarks/airflow/reports/change-policy-calibration-latest.md"))
ap.add_argument("--rows", default=None, help="also write per-PR rows (fired policies and facts) to this JSON file")
ARGS = ap.parse_args()

C = Path(ARGS.bench) / "corpus"
SOURCES = {"airflow-ai-year": C / "ai-year" / "prs", "airflow-e2e": C / "e2e-proof" / "prs"}
for p in ["cosmic", "curl", "flink", "ghostty", "godot", "kafka"]:
    SOURCES[p] = C / "benchmarks" / p / "prs"


def outcome(pr):
    if pr.get("merged"):
        return "merged"
    return "closed" if (pr.get("state") or "").lower() == "closed" else "open"


def disclosed(pr):
    d = pr.get("disclosure") or {}
    fl = pr.get("flag") or {}
    return bool(d.get("disclosed") or d.get("generated_by") or fl.get("ai_related"))


BOT = re.compile(r"(?i)(\[bot\]$|^github-actions|^dependabot|^renovate)")
MECHANICAL_TITLE = re.compile(r"(?i)^\s*(\[v\d+-\d+-test\]|\[v\d|sync v\d|prepare (providers )?release|bump the |update .*constraints|upgrade important ci)")


def mechanical(pr):
    """Bot, backport and release PRs, reported separately because maintainers handle them differently."""
    user = pr.get("user")
    user = (user or {}).get("login") if isinstance(user, dict) else user
    return bool(BOT.search(user or "") or MECHANICAL_TITLE.search(pr.get("title") or ""))


def b64(s):
    return base64.b64encode((s or "").encode("utf-8")).decode("ascii")


def unified(files):
    out = []
    for f in files:
        path = f.get("filename") or ""
        old = f.get("previous_filename") or path
        out.append(f"diff --git a/{old} b/{path}\n--- a/{old}\n+++ b/{path}\n")
        if f.get("patch"):
            out.append(f["patch"].rstrip("\n") + "\n")
    return "".join(out)


def wilson(k, n, z=1.96):
    if n == 0:
        return (0.0, 0.0)
    p = k / n
    d = 1 + z * z / n
    c = p + z * z / (2 * n)
    h = z * math.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / n + z * z / (4 * n * n))
    return ((c - h) / d, (c + h) / d)


def classpath():
    mod = ROOT / "aiv-plugin-change-policy" / "target"
    m2 = Path.home() / ".m2" / "repository"
    jars = [str(mod / "classes"), str(mod / "test-classes"), str(ROOT / "aiv-api" / "target" / "aiv-api-1.0.4.jar")]
    for pattern in ["org/yaml/snakeyaml/2.2/snakeyaml-2.2.jar", "org/slf4j/slf4j-api/*/slf4j-api-*.jar"]:
        found = sorted(glob.glob(str(m2 / pattern)))
        if found:
            jars.append(found[-1])
    return os.pathsep.join(jars)


rows, seen = [], set()
for src, d in SOURCES.items():
    for f in sorted(d.glob("*.json")):
        try:
            pr = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            continue
        repo = "airflow" if src.startswith("airflow") else src
        key = (repo, pr.get("number"))
        if key in seen or not pr.get("files"):
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        files = pr["files"]
        listing = "\n".join(f"{x.get('status') or 'modified'}\t{x.get('filename')}\t{x.get('previous_filename') or ''}" for x in files)
        rows.append({"id": f"{repo}#{pr.get('number')}", "repo": repo, "outcome": outcome(pr), "ai": disclosed(pr),
                     "title": pr.get("title") or "", "pack": "airflow" if repo == "airflow" else "oss-maintainer",
                     "tier": "patch", "mechanical": mechanical(pr),
                     "tsv": "\t".join([f"{repo}#{pr.get('number')}", "airflow" if repo == "airflow" else "oss-maintainer",
                                       b64(pr.get("title")), b64(pr.get("body")), b64(unified(files)), b64(listing),
                                       str(pr.get("additions") or 0), str(pr.get("deletions") or 0)])})

# Index tier: every Airflow PR in the full index, without patches. Only file count, total lines, title and
# body are real, so only policies over those facts (mega-diff, small-fix-huge-diff) are reported for it.
INDEX_POLICIES = ["mega-diff", "small-fix-huge-diff"]
for line in (C / "airflow-all" / "prs.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n"):
    if not line.strip():
        continue
    r = json.loads(line)
    n = r["number"]
    listing = "\n".join(f"modified\tindex-only/file-{i}" for i in range(r.get("changed_files") or 0))
    rows.append({"id": f"airflow-index#{n}", "repo": "airflow-index", "pr": n,
                 "outcome": "merged" if r.get("merged") else ("closed" if (r.get("state") or "").upper() == "CLOSED" else "open"),
                 "ai": r.get("label") in ("ai_disclosed", "ai_tool_mention", "ai_slop_discussion") or bool(r.get("generated_by")),
                 "title": r.get("title") or "", "pack": "airflow", "tier": "index", "mechanical": mechanical(r),
                 "tsv": "\t".join([f"airflow-index#{n}", "airflow", b64(r.get("title")), b64(r.get("body")), b64(""),
                                   b64(listing), str(r.get("additions") or 0), str(r.get("deletions") or 0)])})

with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
    inp, outp = Path(tmp) / "in.tsv", Path(tmp) / "out.tsv"
    inp.write_text("\n".join(r["tsv"] for r in rows) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    subprocess.run(["java", "-cp", classpath(), "io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.plugin.changepolicy.CorpusCalibrationRunner",
                    str(inp), str(outp)], check=True)
    results = {}
    for line in outp.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        rid, gate, fired, skipped, facts = line.split("\t", 4)
        results[rid] = {"gate": gate, "fired": [x for x in fired.split(",") if x], "skipped": skipped,
                        "facts": json.loads(facts)}
for r in rows:
    r.update(results[r["id"]])
    del r["tsv"]

ALL_POLICIES = ["mega-diff", "over-budget", "small-fix-huge-diff", "small-intent-large-diff", "untested-source-change",
                "large-new-code-unit", "infra-with-source", "ci-change-by-non-collaborator", "lockfile-change",
                "formatting-churn", "description-claims-mismatch", "template-compliance", "disclosure-consistency",
                "behind-base", "commit-hygiene"]


def evaluate(pool, policies):
    decided = [r for r in pool if r["outcome"] in ("merged", "closed")]
    merged = [r for r in decided if r["outcome"] == "merged"]
    closed = [r for r in decided if r["outcome"] == "closed"]
    skipped = Counter()
    for r in pool:
        for part in r["skipped"].split("; "):
            if part:
                skipped[part.split(" (")[0]] += 1
    table = []
    for p in policies:
        km = sum(p in r["fired"] for r in merged)
        kc = sum(p in r["fired"] for r in closed)
        ka = sum(p in r["fired"] for r in pool if r["ai"])
        kn = sum(p in r["fired"] for r in pool if not r["ai"])
        pm, pc = km / max(1, len(merged)), kc / max(1, len(closed))
        lo, hi = (max(0.0, x) for x in wilson(km, len(merged)))
        flagged = km + kc
        table.append({
            "policy": p, "merged": [km, len(merged)], "closed": [kc, len(closed)],
            "merged_rate": pm, "merged_rate_ci95": [lo, hi], "closed_rate": pc,
            "lift": (pc / pm) if pm else None, "merge_rate_when_flagged": (km / flagged) if flagged else None,
            "ai": [ka, sum(r["ai"] for r in pool)], "not_ai": [kn, sum(not r["ai"] for r in pool)],
            "skipped_prs": skipped.get(p, 0),
            "promotable": bool(flagged and pm <= ARGS.ceiling and pc > pm),
            "examples": [f"{r['id']} {r['outcome']}: {r['title'][:80]}" for r in decided if p in r["fired"]][:8],
        })
    fired = lambda r: any(p in r["fired"] for p in policies)
    return {"prs": len(pool), "merged": len(merged), "closed": len(closed),
            "baseline_merge_rate": len(merged) / max(1, len(decided)),
            "any_policy": {"merged": [sum(fired(r) for r in merged), len(merged)],
                           "closed": [sum(fired(r) for r in closed), len(closed)]},
            "blocking": {"merged": [sum(r["gate"] == "fail" for r in merged), len(merged)],
                         "closed": [sum(r["gate"] == "fail" for r in closed), len(closed)]},
            "policies": table}


patch = [r for r in rows if r["tier"] == "patch"]
index = [r for r in rows if r["tier"] == "index"]
views = {
    "patch-all": (patch, ALL_POLICIES), "patch-human": ([r for r in patch if not r["mechanical"]], ALL_POLICIES),
    "index-all": (index, INDEX_POLICIES), "index-human": ([r for r in index if not r["mechanical"]], INDEX_POLICIES),
}
summary = {"ceiling": ARGS.ceiling, "by_repo": Counter(r["repo"] for r in rows),
           "views": {k: evaluate(pool, pols) for k, (pool, pols) in views.items()}}
Path(ARGS.out_json).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
Path(ARGS.out_json).write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=1, default=dict), encoding="utf-8")
if ARGS.rows:
    Path(ARGS.rows).write_text(json.dumps(rows), encoding="utf-8")


def pct(x):
    return "n/a" if x is None else f"{100 * x:.1f}%"


def frac(pair):
    return f"{pair[0]} / {pair[1]} ({pct(pair[0] / max(1, pair[1]))})"


VIEW_TITLES = {
    "patch-all": "Stored patches, all PRs (Airflow with the `airflow` pack, six other projects with `oss-maintainer`)",
    "patch-human": "Stored patches, excluding bot, backport and release PRs",
    "index-all": "Full Airflow index (15,575 PRs, size and title facts only)",
    "index-human": "Full Airflow index, excluding bot, backport and release PRs",
}
md = ["# Change-policy calibration", "",
      "Generated by `benchmarks/airflow/scripts/run-change-policy-calibration.py` with the real gate "
      "(`aiv-plugin-change-policy`). Git history and author association are not in the corpus, so policies that "
      "need them are skipped. The index tier has no patches, so only `mega-diff` and `small-fix-huge-diff` are "
      "reported for it.", "",
      f"Promotion test: merged flag rate at most {pct(ARGS.ceiling)} and more common in closed PRs than merged ones.", ""]
for key, title in VIEW_TITLES.items():
    v = summary["views"][key]
    md += [f"## {title}", "",
           f"PRs: {v['prs']}; decided {v['merged']} merged, {v['closed']} closed; baseline merge rate "
           f"{pct(v['baseline_merge_rate'])}. Any policy fired: merged {frac(v['any_policy']['merged'])}, closed "
           f"{frac(v['any_policy']['closed'])}. Gate would block: merged {frac(v['blocking']['merged'])}, closed "
           f"{frac(v['blocking']['closed'])}.", "",
           "| Policy | Merged flagged (95% CI) | Closed flagged | Lift | Merge rate when flagged | AI / not AI | Skipped PRs | Promotable |",
           "|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---|"]
    for t in v["policies"]:
        lo, hi = t["merged_rate_ci95"]
        lift = "n/a" if t["lift"] is None else f"{t['lift']:.2f}"
        md.append(f"| `{t['policy']}` | {t['merged'][0]} ({pct(t['merged_rate'])}, {pct(lo)} to {pct(hi)}) | "
                  f"{t['closed'][0]} ({pct(t['closed_rate'])}) | {lift} | {pct(t['merge_rate_when_flagged'])} | "
                  f"{pct(t['ai'][0] / max(1, t['ai'][1]))} / {pct(t['not_ai'][0] / max(1, t['not_ai'][1]))} | "
                  f"{t['skipped_prs']} | {'yes' if t['promotable'] else 'no'} |")
    md.append("")
md += ["## Examples (first decided PRs per policy, full views, for manual review)", ""]
for key in ("patch-all", "index-all"):
    for t in summary["views"][key]["policies"]:
        if t["examples"]:
            md.append(f"- {key} `{t['policy']}`: " + "; ".join(t["examples"][:4]))
Path(ARGS.out_md).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
Path(ARGS.out_md).write_text("\n".join(md) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
print("\n".join(md))
```

### `benchmarks/airflow/scripts/mine-change-patterns.py`

Component J: signal mining behind the design. 258 lines, sha256 `72d8eb2d28499a7a`.

```python
# Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Mine change-shape patterns from every stored patch in aiv-airflow-bench and compare by outcome.

Facts follow docs/AI-integration/CHANGE-POLICY-ENGINE-DESIGN.md section 4, approximated from GitHub
patch text (no repository checkout, so reuse against existing code is not measured here).

    python benchmarks/airflow/scripts/mine-change-patterns.py [--bench ../aiv-airflow-bench] [--out <dir>]

Prints per-signal flag rates for merged vs closed and AI-flagged vs not, and merge rate per signal.
With --out, also writes change-pattern-examples.json and change-pattern-rows.json there.
Results are recorded in docs/AI-integration/MAINTAINER-PAIN-AND-PR-PATTERNS-ANALYSIS.md.
"""
import argparse
import fnmatch
import hashlib
import json
import re
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

_ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
_ap.add_argument("--bench", default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4] / "aiv-airflow-bench"))
_ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)
ARGS = _ap.parse_args()
C = Path(ARGS.bench) / "corpus"
SOURCES = {"airflow-ai-year": C / "ai-year" / "prs", "airflow-e2e": C / "e2e-proof" / "prs"}
for p in ["cosmic", "curl", "flink", "ghostty", "godot", "kafka"]:
    SOURCES[p] = C / "benchmarks" / p / "prs"

CATS = [
    ("lockfile", ["*package-lock.json", "*yarn.lock", "*pnpm-lock.yaml", "*uv.lock", "*poetry.lock", "*Cargo.lock",
                  "*go.sum", "*Gemfile.lock", "*composer.lock", "*.lock"]),
    ("generated", ["*generated*", "*_pb2.py", "*.min.js", "*.snap", "*/dist/*", "*.svg"]),
    ("vendored", ["vendor/*", "*/vendor/*", "third_party/*", "*/third_party/*", "thirdparty/*"]),
    ("ci", [".github/*", ".gitlab-ci*", "*Jenkinsfile", ".circleci/*", "azure-pipelines*", "*.github/workflows/*"]),
    ("ai-instruction", ["*AGENTS.md", "*CLAUDE.md", "*GEMINI.md", ".github/copilot-instructions.md", ".github/instructions/*",
                        ".cursor/*", ".cursorrules", ".kiro/*", ".github/skills/*"]),
    ("dependency-manifest", ["*requirements*.txt", "*pyproject.toml", "*setup.py", "*setup.cfg", "*package.json",
                             "*pom.xml", "*build.gradle*", "*Cargo.toml", "*go.mod", "*Gemfile", "*.csproj"]),
    ("migration", ["*migrations/*", "*alembic/*"]),
    ("test", ["*test/*", "*tests/*", "*__tests__/*", "*test_*.py", "*_test.py", "*_test.go", "*Test.java", "*Tests.java",
              "*.test.*", "*.spec.*", "*IT.java", "*/testing/*", "*testdata/*"]),
    ("docs", ["*.md", "*.rst", "*.txt", "docs/*", "*/docs/*", "*.adoc", "*newsfragments/*", "*CHANGELOG*", "*RELEASE_NOTES*"]),
    ("config", ["*.yaml", "*.yml", "*.json", "*.toml", "*.ini", "*.cfg", "*.properties", "*.conf", "*.xml"]),
    ("binary", ["*.png", "*.jpg", "*.gif", "*.ico", "*.pdf", "*.jar", "*.zip", "*.woff*", "*.ttf"]),
]
NON_REVIEWABLE = {"lockfile", "generated", "vendored", "binary"}
SMALL_INTENT = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(typo|fix(es|ed)?|bump|minor|small|tweak|nit|doc(s|umentation)?|spelling|grammar)\b")
COMMENT_LINE = re.compile(r"^\s*(#|//|/\*|\*|--|<!--)")
CODEISH_COMMENT = re.compile(r"^\s*(#|//)\s*[\w.]+\s*(\(|=|\.|\[)[^\n]*[)\];:]?\s*$")
NARRATING = re.compile(r"(?i)^\s*(#|//)\s*(increment|initialize|initialise|loop (over|through)|check if|return the|"
                       r"create (a|an|the) new|set the|get the|call the|import the|define (a|the))\b")
BROAD_EXCEPT = re.compile(r"^\s*(except\s*(Exception)?\s*:|catch\s*\(\s*(Exception|Throwable|\.\.\.)\b)")
DEF = re.compile(r"^\s*(def|async def)\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\(|^\s*(public|private|protected|static|\s)*[\w<>\[\], ]+\s+([a-z]\w*)\s*\([^;]*\)\s*(\{|throws)|^\s*func\s+(\([^)]*\)\s*)?([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\(|^\s*(export\s+)?(async\s+)?function\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\(")


def category(path):
    p = path.replace("\\", "/")
    for name, globs in CATS:
        if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(p, g) or fnmatch.fnmatch("/" + p, g) for g in globs):
            return name
    return "source"


def added_removed(patch):
    add, rem = [], []
    for line in (patch or "").split("\n"):
        if line.startswith("+") and not line.startswith("+++"):
            add.append(line[1:])
        elif line.startswith("-") and not line.startswith("---"):
            rem.append(line[1:])
    return add, rem


def norm(line):
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", line).strip()


def norm_ident(line):
    s = norm(line)
    s = re.sub(r"(['\"]).*?\1", "S", s)
    s = re.sub(r"\b\d+(\.\d+)?\b", "N", s)
    return re.sub(r"\b[A-Za-z_]\w*\b", "I", s)


def windows(lines, k=6):
    sig = [l for l in (norm(x) for x in lines) if len(l) > 3 and l not in "{}()[];" and not COMMENT_LINE.match(l)
           and not l.startswith(("import ", "from ", "package "))]
    return {hashlib.sha1("\n".join(sig[i:i + k]).encode()).hexdigest() for i in range(len(sig) - k + 1)}


def facts(pr):
    files = pr.get("files") or []
    f = Counter()
    cats = Counter()
    rev_files, rev_lines, src_lines, test_lines = 0, 0, 0, 0
    ws_only, rename_only, comment_only = 0, 0, 0
    commented_code, narrating, broad_except, defs = 0, 0, 0, []
    blocks = defaultdict(set)
    rblocks = defaultdict(set)
    for fl in files:
        path = fl.get("filename") or ""
        cat = category(path)
        cats[cat] += 1
        add, rem = added_removed(fl.get("patch"))
        status = fl.get("status")
        is_ws = bool(add or rem) and sorted(norm(x) for x in add if norm(x)) == sorted(norm(x) for x in rem if norm(x))
        is_rename_only = status == "renamed" and not add and not rem
        ws_only += is_ws
        rename_only += is_rename_only
        if add and all(COMMENT_LINE.match(x) or not x.strip() for x in add) and cat == "source":
            comment_only += 1
        reviewable = cat not in NON_REVIEWABLE and not is_ws and not is_rename_only
        changes = (fl.get("additions") or 0) + (fl.get("deletions") or 0)
        if reviewable:
            rev_files += 1
            rev_lines += changes
            if cat == "source":
                src_lines += changes
            if cat == "test":
                test_lines += changes
        if cat in ("source", "test"):
            commented_code += sum(1 for x in add if CODEISH_COMMENT.match(x))
            narrating += sum(1 for x in add if NARRATING.match(x))
            broad_except += sum(1 for x in add if BROAD_EXCEPT.match(x))
            for x in add:
                m = DEF.match(x)
                if m:
                    name = next((g for g in (m.group(2), m.group(4), m.group(7), m.group(10)) if g), None)
                    if name and name not in ("if", "for", "while", "switch", "return", "catch", "new"):
                        defs.append(name)
            for w in windows(add):
                blocks[w].add(path)
            ident_lines = [norm_ident(x) for x in add]
            for w in windows(ident_lines):
                rblocks[w].add(path)
    intra = sum(1 for v in blocks.values() if len(v) > 1)
    intra_renamed = sum(1 for v in rblocks.values() if len(v) > 1)
    dup_defs = sum(c - 1 for c in Counter(defs).values() if c > 1)
    reviewable_cats = {c for c in cats if c not in NON_REVIEWABLE}
    title = pr.get("title") or ""
    return {
        "files": pr.get("changed_files") or len(files),
        "files_listed": len(files),
        "lines": (pr.get("additions") or 0) + (pr.get("deletions") or 0),
        "rev_files": rev_files, "rev_lines": rev_lines, "src_lines": src_lines, "test_lines": test_lines,
        "non_reviewable_files": sum(cats[c] for c in NON_REVIEWABLE),
        "ws_only": ws_only, "rename_only": rename_only, "comment_only_src": comment_only,
        "cats_mixed": len(reviewable_cats), "has_ci": cats["ci"] > 0, "has_deps": cats["dependency-manifest"] > 0,
        "has_ai_instr": cats["ai-instruction"] > 0, "has_lock": cats["lockfile"] > 0,
        "commented_code": commented_code, "narrating": narrating, "broad_except": broad_except,
        "intra_dup": intra, "intra_dup_renamed": intra_renamed, "dup_defs": dup_defs,
        "small_intent": bool(SMALL_INTENT.search(title)),
        "truncated": bool(pr.get("files_truncated") or pr.get("too_big") or any(x.get("patch_truncated") or x.get("patch_omitted") for x in files)),
    }


def outcome(pr):
    if pr.get("merged"):
        return "merged"
    return "closed" if (pr.get("state") or "").lower() == "closed" else "open"


def disclosed(pr):
    d = pr.get("disclosure") or {}
    fl = pr.get("flag") or {}
    return bool(d.get("disclosed") or d.get("generated_by") or fl.get("ai_related"))


rows = []
seen = set()
for src, d in SOURCES.items():
    for f in sorted(d.glob("*.json")):
        try:
            pr = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            continue
        repo = "airflow" if src.startswith("airflow") else src
        key = (repo, pr.get("number"))
        if key in seen or not pr.get("files"):
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        r = facts(pr)
        r.update({"repo": repo, "pr": pr.get("number"), "outcome": outcome(pr), "ai": disclosed(pr), "title": pr.get("title")})
        rows.append(r)

print("PRs with patch data:", len(rows), Counter(r["repo"] for r in rows))
print("outcomes:", Counter(r["outcome"] for r in rows), "AI-flagged:", sum(r["ai"] for r in rows))
print("truncated patches:", sum(r["truncated"] for r in rows))

SIGNALS = {
    "rev_files>=20": lambda r: r["rev_files"] >= 20,
    "rev_files>=50": lambda r: r["rev_files"] >= 50,
    "rev_lines>=1000": lambda r: r["rev_lines"] >= 1000,
    "small_intent & rev_files>10": lambda r: r["small_intent"] and r["rev_files"] > 10,
    "cats_mixed>=3": lambda r: r["cats_mixed"] >= 3,
    "ci+source mixed": lambda r: r["has_ci"] and r["src_lines"] > 0,
    "deps changed": lambda r: r["has_deps"],
    "lockfile changed": lambda r: r["has_lock"],
    "ws_only>=1": lambda r: r["ws_only"] >= 1,
    "ws_only>=5": lambda r: r["ws_only"] >= 5,
    "non_reviewable>=1": lambda r: r["non_reviewable_files"] >= 1,
    "intra_dup>=1": lambda r: r["intra_dup"] >= 1,
    "intra_dup_renamed>=2": lambda r: r["intra_dup_renamed"] >= 2,
    "dup_defs>=1": lambda r: r["dup_defs"] >= 1,
    "commented_code>=3": lambda r: r["commented_code"] >= 3,
    "narrating>=3": lambda r: r["narrating"] >= 3,
    "broad_except>=2": lambda r: r["broad_except"] >= 2,
    "comment_only_src>=1": lambda r: r["comment_only_src"] >= 1,
    "src>=200 & no tests": lambda r: r["src_lines"] >= 200 and r["test_lines"] == 0,
    "ai-instruction touched": lambda r: r["has_ai_instr"],
}


def rate(sub, fn):
    return (sum(1 for r in sub if fn(r)), len(sub))


groups = {
    "merged": [r for r in rows if r["outcome"] == "merged"],
    "closed": [r for r in rows if r["outcome"] == "closed"],
    "ai": [r for r in rows if r["ai"]],
    "not-ai": [r for r in rows if not r["ai"]],
}
print(f"\n{'signal':30} {'merged':>14} {'closed':>14} {'lift':>5} {'AI':>14} {'not AI':>14}")
summary = {}
for name, fn in SIGNALS.items():
    vals = {g: rate(sub, fn) for g, sub in groups.items()}
    pm = vals["merged"][0] / max(1, vals["merged"][1])
    pc = vals["closed"][0] / max(1, vals["closed"][1])
    lift = pc / pm if pm else float("inf")
    cell = lambda v: f"{v[0]:4}/{v[1]:<4} {100 * v[0] / max(1, v[1]):4.1f}%"
    print(f"{name:30} {cell(vals['merged']):>14} {cell(vals['closed']):>14} {lift:5.2f} {cell(vals['ai']):>14} {cell(vals['not-ai']):>14}")
    summary[name] = {g: v for g, v in vals.items()}

# merge rate per signal (closed + merged only)
print(f"\n{'signal':30} {'flagged decided':>15} {'merge rate':>10}   baseline")
decided = groups["merged"] + groups["closed"]
base = len(groups["merged"]) / max(1, len(decided))
for name, fn in SIGNALS.items():
    fl = [r for r in decided if fn(r)]
    if fl:
        mr = sum(1 for r in fl if r["outcome"] == "merged") / len(fl)
        print(f"{name:30} {len(fl):15} {100 * mr:9.1f}%   {100 * base:.1f}%")

# examples for manual review
ex = defaultdict(list)
for r in rows:
    for name, fn in SIGNALS.items():
        if fn(r) and len(ex[name]) < 6:
            ex[name].append(f"{r['repo']}#{r['pr']} {r['outcome']} files={r['files']} rev={r['rev_files']} :: {(r['title'] or '')[:70]}")
if ARGS.out:
    out = Path(ARGS.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out / "change-pattern-examples.json").write_text(json.dumps(ex, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    (out / "change-pattern-rows.json").write_text(json.dumps(rows), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"\nexamples and rows written to {out}")
```

### `benchmarks/airflow/scripts/label-pr-slop-patterns.py`

Component J: PR pattern labels over the corpus. 425 lines, sha256 `999707979805d1f2`.

```python
# Copyright 2026 Vaquar Khan
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Label Apache Airflow PRs in the stored corpus with the slop-like patterns maintainers name.

Uses only data already in aiv-airflow-bench and this repository (no network):

* Tier A, index (corpus/airflow-all/prs.jsonl, 15,575 PRs): title, body, size, outcome, AI disclosure.
* Tier B, patches (corpus/ai-year/prs + corpus/e2e-proof/prs, Airflow only): added and removed lines.
* Tier C, AIV census (benchmarks/airflow/reports/census-latest.json): real AIV gates on 627 real checkouts.

    python benchmarks/airflow/scripts/label-pr-slop-patterns.py [--bench ../aiv-airflow-bench]

Writes benchmarks/airflow/results/pr-pattern-labels-latest.jsonl (one row per PR with its labels and
evidence) and benchmarks/airflow/reports/pr-pattern-labels-latest.md (counts, merge rates, examples).
Labels describe the change, never who wrote it. Heuristic labels are candidates for review, not verdicts.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import re
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--bench", default=str(ROOT.parent / "aiv-airflow-bench"))
ap.add_argument("--out-jsonl", default=str(ROOT / "benchmarks/airflow/results/pr-pattern-labels-latest.jsonl"))
ap.add_argument("--out-md", default=str(ROOT / "benchmarks/airflow/reports/pr-pattern-labels-latest.md"))
ARGS = ap.parse_args()
C = Path(ARGS.bench) / "corpus"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- label catalogue
LABELS = [
    ("L1", "small-fix-many-files", "A", "Title promises a small change (typo, docs, fix, bump, minor) but 20+ files change"),
    ("L2", "big-pr", "A", "50+ files or 1,000+ changed lines"),
    ("L3", "small-fix-1000-lines", "A", "Small-change title with 1,000+ changed lines"),
    ("L4", "commit-churn", "-", "Every commit rewrites the whole change (needs per-commit data)"),
    ("L5", "description-mismatch", "B", "Description claims tests the diff does not add, or the title promises a fix but the diff is mostly new code"),
    ("L6", "security-smell", "B", "Added non-test code with a hardcoded secret, disabled TLS, shell=True, eval/exec, unsafe deserialization, SQL built from strings"),
    ("L7", "hallucination-or-ai-artifact", "B", "Placeholder or AI-chatter text in added code, unfilled template values, or AIV hard markers"),
    ("L8", "scaffolding-non-modular", "B", "Stub bodies, one very long new function, a 500+ line new source file, or deep nesting"),
    ("L9", "duplicate-not-reuse", "B", "Same code block or helper added in several files, duplicated test bodies, or a new utils/helpers module"),
]
SMALL = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(typos?|spelling|grammar|docs?|documentation|readme|fix(es|ed)?|bug|bump|minor|small|tweak|nit)\b")
BOT = re.compile(r"(?i)(\[bot\]$|^github-actions|^dependabot|^renovate)")
MECHANICAL_TITLE = re.compile(r"(?i)^\s*(\[v\d+-\d+-test\]|\[v\d|sync v\d|prepare (providers )?release|bump the |update .*constraints|upgrade important ci)")

TEST_PATH = re.compile(r"(^|/)(tests?|testing|__tests__|testdata)/|(^|/)test_[^/]*\.py$|_test\.(py|go)$|\.(test|spec)\.[jt]sx?$|conftest\.py$")
DOC_PATH = re.compile(r"\.(md|rst|txt|adoc|svg|png|jpg)$|(^|/)docs?/|newsfragments/")
SRC_EXT = re.compile(r"\.(py|java|go|ts|tsx|js|jsx|scala|kt|rs|c|cc|cpp|h|sh)$")

SECURITY = [
    ("hardcoded-secret", re.compile(r"""(?i)\b(password|passwd|secret|api_?key|access_?key|auth_?token|private_?key)\b\s*[:=]\s*["'][^"'\s{}$<>]{8,}["']""")),
    ("aws-key", re.compile(r"\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b")),
    ("private-key", re.compile(r"-----BEGIN (RSA |EC |OPENSSH )?PRIVATE KEY")),
    ("tls-verify-off", re.compile(r"\bverify\s*=\s*False\b|_create_unverified_context|CERT_NONE")),
    ("shell-true", re.compile(r"\bshell\s*=\s*True\b")),
    ("eval-exec", re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])(eval|exec)\s*\(")),
    ("unsafe-deserialize", re.compile(r"\bpickle\.loads?\(|\byaml\.load\((?![^)]*SafeLoader)|\bmarshal\.loads\(")),
    ("sql-from-string", re.compile(r"\.execute\(\s*f[\"']|\.execute\([^)]*\.format\(|\.execute\([^)]*[\"']\s*%\s*[\w(]")),
    ("os-system", re.compile(r"\bos\.system\(|\bos\.popen\(")),
    ("world-writable", re.compile(r"\b0o?777\b|chmod\s+777")),
    ("insecure-temp", re.compile(r"\btempfile\.mktemp\(")),
]
PLACEHOLDER_SECRET = re.compile(r"(?i)(test|dummy|fake|example|sample|changeme|xxx|placeholder|secret123|password123|mock)")
AI_ARTIFACT = [
    ("todo-implement", re.compile(r"(?i)\b(todo|fixme)\b\s*:?\s*(implement|add (the )?(actual|real)|replace with|fill in)")),
    ("placeholder-word", re.compile(r"(?i)#.*\bplaceholder\b|\bplaceholder (implementation|logic|value)\b")),
    ("your-x-here", re.compile(r"(?i)\byour[_\- ]?(api[_\- ]?key|token|password|secret|value|code|logic)[_\- ]?here\b")),
    ("elided-code", re.compile(r"(?i)(\.\.\.|#|//)\s*(rest of|existing|remaining) (the )?(code|implementation|logic)")),
    ("ai-chatter", re.compile(r"(?i)\b(as an ai( language model)?|i cannot (help|assist)|i'?m sorry, but|here is the (updated|modified|complete) code)\b")),
    ("implementation-here", re.compile(r"(?i)\b(implementation|logic) (goes|would go) here\b|\bin a real (implementation|scenario|application)\b")),
    ("demo-only", re.compile(r"(?i)\bfor (demonstration|illustration) purposes\b|\bsimplified (version|implementation)\b|\bmock (implementation|response) for now\b")),
    ("edit-artifact", re.compile(r"^(<<<<<<< SEARCH|>>>>>>> REPLACE)\s*$")),
]
DEF = re.compile(r"^(\s*)(async\s+)?def\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\(")
CLASS = re.compile(r"^(\s*)class\s+\w+")
STUB = re.compile(r"^\s*(pass|\.\.\.|raise NotImplementedError(\(.*\))?|return None\s*#.*todo.*)\s*$", re.I)
TEST_DEF = re.compile(r"^\s*(async\s+)?def\s+test\w*\s*\(|^\s*@(Test|ParameterizedTest)\b|^\s*(it|test)\s*\(\s*['\"`]")
CLAIM_COUNT = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:added|adds|add|wrote|introduces?|includes?)\s+(\d{1,4})\s+(?:new\s+|unit\s+|additional\s+)*(?:test cases|tests)\b"
                         r"|(?<![#\w.])(\d{1,4})\s+(?:new|additional)\s+(?:unit\s+)?(?:test cases|tests)\b")
CLAIM_ADDED = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(added|adds|add|wrote|new|includes?|updated?)\b[^.\n]{0,30}?\b(unit |integration )?tests?\b")
NEGATION = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(no|not|without|none|nothing|never|will|would|could|can|should|if|happy to|todo|follow-?up)\b|n't")
COMMENT = re.compile(r"^\s*(#|//|/\*|\*|--)")
HELPER_MODULE = re.compile(r"(?i)(^|/)[^/]*(utils?|helpers?|common|misc|tools)[^/]*\.py$")


def small_intent(title):
    return bool(SMALL.search(title or ""))


def mechanical(user, title):
    return bool(BOT.search(user or "") or MECHANICAL_TITLE.search(title or ""))


def norm(line):
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", line).strip()


def ident_norm(line):
    s = re.sub(r"(['\"]).*?\1", "S", norm(line))
    s = re.sub(r"\b\d+(\.\d+)?\b", "N", s)
    return re.sub(r"\b[A-Za-z_]\w*\b", "I", s)


def windows(lines, k=6, f=norm):
    sig = [x for x in (f(l) for l in lines) if len(x) > 3 and not COMMENT.match(x)
           and not x.startswith(("import ", "from ", "package ", "@")) and x not in ("{", "}", "):", "])", ")")]
    return {hashlib.sha1("\n".join(sig[i:i + k]).encode()).hexdigest() for i in range(len(sig) - k + 1)}


def added(patch):
    out = []
    for l in (patch or "").split("\n"):
        if l.startswith("+") and not l.startswith("+++"):
            out.append(l[1:])
    return out


def body_claims(body, test_defs, test_files, src_lines):
    clean = re.sub(r"(?s)<!--.*?-->", "", body or "")
    claimed = 0
    for m in CLAIM_COUNT.finditer(clean):
        claimed = max(claimed, int(m.group(1) or m.group(2)))
    says_added = False
    for line in clean.split("\n"):
        if re.match(r"^\s*[-*]\s*\[\s\]", line):
            continue
        for m in CLAIM_ADDED.finditer(line):
            if not NEGATION.search(line[max(0, m.start() - 25):m.end()]):
                says_added = True
    reasons = []
    if claimed and claimed > test_defs + 2:
        reasons.append(f"claims {claimed} tests added, diff adds {test_defs} test functions")
    if says_added and test_files == 0 and src_lines > 0:
        reasons.append("says tests were added, no test file changed")
    return reasons


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- tier A: index
index = {}
for line in (C / "airflow-all" / "prs.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n"):
    if line.strip():
        r = json.loads(line)
        index[r["number"]] = r


def outcome(r):
    return "merged" if r.get("merged") else ("closed" if (r.get("state") or "").upper() == "CLOSED" else "open")


def ai_flag(r):
    return r.get("label") in ("ai_disclosed", "ai_tool_mention", "ai_slop_discussion") or bool(r.get("generated_by"))


rows = {}
for n, r in index.items():
    lines = (r.get("additions") or 0) + (r.get("deletions") or 0)
    files = r.get("changed_files") or 0
    t = r.get("title") or ""
    row = {"pr": n, "url": r.get("url"), "title": t, "user": r.get("user"), "outcome": outcome(r), "ai": ai_flag(r),
           "files": files, "lines": lines, "mechanical": mechanical(r.get("user"), t), "tiers": ["A"],
           "labels": {}}
    if small_intent(t) and files >= 20:
        row["labels"]["small-fix-many-files"] = f"{files} files"
    if files >= 50 or lines >= 1000:
        row["labels"]["big-pr"] = f"{files} files, {lines} lines"
    if small_intent(t) and lines >= 1000:
        row["labels"]["small-fix-1000-lines"] = f"{lines} lines"
    rows[n] = row

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- tier B: patches
patch_prs = {}
for d in [C / "ai-year" / "prs", C / "e2e-proof" / "prs"]:
    for f in sorted(d.glob("*.json")):
        try:
            pr = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if pr.get("files") and pr.get("number") not in patch_prs:
            patch_prs[pr["number"]] = pr

for n, pr in patch_prs.items():
    row = rows.get(n)
    if row is None:
        t = pr.get("title") or ""
        row = rows[n] = {"pr": n, "url": pr.get("url"), "title": t, "user": (pr.get("user") or {}).get("login") if isinstance(pr.get("user"), dict) else pr.get("user"),
                         "outcome": "merged" if pr.get("merged") else ("closed" if (pr.get("state") or "").lower() == "closed" else "open"),
                         "ai": bool((pr.get("flag") or {}).get("ai_related")), "files": pr.get("changed_files") or 0,
                         "lines": (pr.get("additions") or 0) + (pr.get("deletions") or 0), "tiers": [], "labels": {}}
        row["mechanical"] = mechanical(row["user"], t)
    row["tiers"].append("B")
    sec, art, scaf, dup = Counter(), Counter(), [], []
    sig = defaultdict(set)  # label -> sub-signal keys, for per-signal merge rates
    blocks, rblocks, tblocks = defaultdict(set), defaultdict(set), defaultdict(set)
    fn_files = defaultdict(set)
    test_defs = test_files = src_lines = new_src_lines = 0
    for fl in pr["files"]:
        path = fl.get("filename") or ""
        add = added(fl.get("patch"))
        is_test, is_doc = bool(TEST_PATH.search(path)), bool(DOC_PATH.search(path))
        is_src = bool(SRC_EXT.search(path)) and not is_test and not is_doc
        if is_test:
            test_files += 1
            test_defs += sum(1 for l in add if TEST_DEF.match(l))
            for w in windows(add):
                tblocks[w].add(path)
        if is_src:
            src_lines += (fl.get("additions") or 0) + (fl.get("deletions") or 0)
            if fl.get("status") == "added":
                new_src_lines += fl.get("additions") or 0
        if not is_doc:
            for l in add:
                for name, rx in AI_ARTIFACT:
                    if rx.search(l):
                        art[name] += 1
        if not is_src:
            continue
        for l in add:
            for name, rx in SECURITY:
                if rx.search(l) and not (name == "hardcoded-secret" and PLACEHOLDER_SECRET.search(l)):
                    sec[name] += 1
        # scaffolding: stubs, long functions, god files, nesting
        stubs, longest, current, depth = 0, 0, 0, 0
        for i, l in enumerate(add):
            if DEF.match(l) or CLASS.match(l):
                longest = max(longest, current)
                current = 0
                nxt = next((x for x in add[i + 1:] if x.strip() and not x.strip().startswith(("#", '"""', "'''"))), "")
                if DEF.match(l) and STUB.match(nxt):
                    stubs += 1
                if DEF.match(l):
                    fn_files[DEF.match(l).group(3)].add(path)
            else:
                current += 1
            if path.endswith(".py") and len(l) - len(l.lstrip(" ")) >= 24 and l.strip():
                depth += 1
        longest = max(longest, current)
        if stubs >= 2:
            scaf.append(f"{stubs} stub functions in {path}")
            sig["scaffolding-non-modular"].add("stub-functions")
        if longest >= 150 and any(DEF.match(x) for x in add):
            scaf.append(f"~{longest}-line function body in {path}")
            sig["scaffolding-non-modular"].add("long-function")
        if fl.get("status") == "added" and (fl.get("additions") or 0) >= 500:
            scaf.append(f"new {fl.get('additions')}-line file {path}")
            sig["scaffolding-non-modular"].add("new-500-line-file")
        if depth >= 10:
            scaf.append(f"{depth} lines nested 6+ levels in {path}")
            sig["scaffolding-non-modular"].add("deep-nesting")
        if fl.get("status") == "added" and HELPER_MODULE.search(path):
            dup.append(f"new helper module {path}")
            sig["duplicate-not-reuse"].add("new-helper-module")
        for w in windows(add):
            blocks[w].add(path)
        for w in windows(add, f=ident_norm):
            rblocks[w].add(path)
    exact = sum(1 for v in blocks.values() if len(v) > 1)
    renamed = sum(1 for v in rblocks.values() if len(v) > 1)
    test_dup = sum(1 for v in tblocks.values() if len(v) > 1)
    same_fn = sorted(n for n, v in fn_files.items() if len(v) > 1 and not n.startswith("_") and n not in ("__init__", "main", "execute", "run", "setup", "get", "set"))
    for cond, key, text in ((exact, "identical-blocks", f"{exact} identical 6-line blocks across files"),
                            (renamed >= 2, "renamed-copies", f"{renamed} renamed-copy blocks across files"),
                            (test_dup, "duplicated-tests", f"{test_dup} duplicated test blocks across files"),
                            (same_fn, "same-helper-in-several-files",
                             f"same helper defined in several files: {', '.join(same_fn[:3])}")):
        if cond:
            dup.append(text)
            sig["duplicate-not-reuse"].add(key)
    mism = body_claims(pr.get("body"), test_defs, test_files, src_lines)
    if mism:
        sig["description-mismatch"].add("test-claims")
    if small_intent(row["title"]) and new_src_lines >= 300:
        mism.append(f"small-change title, {new_src_lines} lines of new source files")
        sig["description-mismatch"].add("small-title-new-code")
    sig["security-smell"].update(sec)
    sig["hallucination-or-ai-artifact"].update(art)
    if pr.get("markers_in_patch"):
        sig["hallucination-or-ai-artifact"].add("discovery-marker")
    row["signals"] = {k: sorted(v) for k, v in sig.items() if v}
    if mism:
        row["labels"]["description-mismatch"] = "; ".join(mism)
    if sec:
        row["labels"]["security-smell"] = ", ".join(f"{k} x{v}" for k, v in sec.items())
    if art or pr.get("markers_in_patch"):
        row["labels"]["hallucination-or-ai-artifact"] = ", ".join([f"{k} x{v}" for k, v in art.items()]
                                                                    + [f"marker {m}" for m in (pr.get("markers_in_patch") or [])][:3])
    if scaf:
        row["labels"]["scaffolding-non-modular"] = "; ".join(scaf[:3])
    if dup:
        row["labels"]["duplicate-not-reuse"] = "; ".join(dup[:3])
    row["patch_truncated"] = bool(pr.get("files_truncated") or any(x.get("patch_truncated") or x.get("patch_omitted") for x in pr["files"]))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- tier C: AIV census
census = json.loads((ROOT / "benchmarks/airflow/reports/census-latest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
census_rules = Counter()
census_n = 0
for case in census["cases"]:
    if case.get("source") != "github" or not case.get("pr"):
        continue
    census_n += 1
    hits = [f.get("rule_id") for g in case.get("gates") or [] for f in g.get("findings") or []]
    census_rules.update(hits)
    row = rows.get(case["pr"])
    if row is None:
        continue
    row["tiers"].append("C")
    hard = [h for h in hits if h and h.split(".")[0] in ("invariant", "package-existence", "syntax") or (h or "").startswith("design.forbidden")]
    if hard:
        prev = row["labels"].get("hallucination-or-ai-artifact")
        row["labels"]["hallucination-or-ai-artifact"] = "; ".join(x for x in [prev, "AIV: " + ", ".join(hard)] if x)
    sec_hits = [h for h in hits if (h or "").startswith("security.")]
    if sec_hits:
        row["labels"]["security-smell"] = "; ".join(x for x in [row["labels"].get("security-smell"), "AIV: " + ", ".join(sec_hits)] if x)
```
