        facts.put("author.open_prs", pr.author().openPrs());
        facts.put("author.failing_open_prs", pr.author().failingOpenPrs());
        if (pr.author().lastActivityAt() != null) {
            facts.put("author.days_since_activity", Duration.between(pr.author().lastActivityAt(), asOf).toDays());
        }
        facts.put("checks.required_failed", pr.checks().requiredFailed());
        facts.put("checks.required_missing", pr.checks().requiredMissing());
        facts.put("checks.conflicts", pr.checks().conflicts());
        facts.put("review.unresolved_threads", pr.review().unresolvedThreads());
        facts.put("review.maintainer_engaged", pr.review().maintainerEngaged());
        facts.put("branch.commits_behind_base", pr.branch().commitsBehindBase());
        facts.put("issue.linked", pr.issue().linked());
        facts.put("issue.accepted", pr.issue().accepted());
        facts.put("ci.definition_touched", pr.ci().definitionTouched());
        facts.put("files.changed", pr.files().changed());
        facts.put("files.lines_changed", pr.files().linesChanged());
        return facts;
    }

    private static void write(Path output, String text) throws IOException {
        Path parent = output.getParent();
        if (parent != null) {
            Files.createDirectories(parent);
        }
        Files.writeString(output, text, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private static Object readJson(Path input) throws IOException {
        return JsonLite.parse(Files.readString(input, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private static Object readYaml(Path input) throws IOException {
        Object value = new Yaml(new SafeConstructor(new LoaderOptions())).load(Files.newInputStream(input));
        if (!(value instanceof Map<?, ?>)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("triage policy root must be a YAML map");
        }
        return value;
    }

    private static Instant parseInstant(String value, String field) {
        try {
            return Instant.parse(value);
        } catch (DateTimeParseException e) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(field + " must be RFC-3339 UTC instant, got '" + value + "'");
        }
    }

    private static String digest(Object value) {
        return digestForAlgorithm("SHA-256", value);
    }

    /** Package-visible for a deterministic failure-path test; production always requests SHA-256. */
    static String digestForAlgorithm(String algorithm, Object value) {
        try {
            byte[] hash = MessageDigest.getInstance(algorithm).digest(JsonLite.write(value).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            StringBuilder hex = new StringBuilder("sha256:");
            for (byte b : hash) {
                hex.append(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%02x", b));
            }
            return hex.toString();
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("SHA-256 unavailable", e);
        }
    }

    /** Strict arguments for a side-effect-free replay. */
    record Args(Path input, Path policy, Path jsonOutput, Path markdownOutput, String asOf) {
        static Args parse(String[] args) {
            if (args.length == 0 || !"replay".equals(args[0])) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Usage: aiv triage replay --input <snapshot.json> --policy <policy.yaml> "
                        + "--output-json <result.json> --output-markdown <result.md> [--as-of <RFC-3339>]");
            }
            Path input = null, policy = null, json = null, markdown = null;
            String asOf = null;
            for (int i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
                if (!Set.of("--input", "--policy", "--output-json", "--output-markdown", "--as-of").contains(args[i])
                        || i + 1 >= args.length || args[i + 1].startsWith("--")) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown triage option or missing value: " + args[i]);
                }
                String value = args[++i];
                switch (args[i - 1]) {
                    case "--input" -> input = once(input, Path.of(value), "--input");
                    case "--policy" -> policy = once(policy, Path.of(value), "--policy");
                    case "--output-json" -> json = once(json, Path.of(value), "--output-json");
                    case "--output-markdown" -> markdown = once(markdown, Path.of(value), "--output-markdown");
                    case "--as-of" -> {
                        if (asOf != null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Duplicate --as-of");
                        if (value.isBlank()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("--as-of must not be blank");
                        asOf = value;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (input == null || policy == null || json == null || markdown == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("triage replay needs --input, --policy, --output-json and --output-markdown");
            }
            return new Args(input.toAbsolutePath(), policy.toAbsolutePath(), json.toAbsolutePath(), markdown.toAbsolutePath(), asOf);
        }

        private static Path once(Path previous, Path value, String option) {
            if (previous != null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Duplicate " + option);
            return value;
        }
    }

    /** Parsed snapshot. Every absent optional nested field stays {@code null} and is reported as unavailable. */
    record Snapshot(String repository, Instant capturedAt, List<PullRequest> pullRequests, Object original) {
        static Snapshot parse(Object raw) {
            Map<String, Object> root = requiredMap(raw, "snapshot");
            rejectUnknown(root, ROOT_KEYS, "snapshot");
            requireEquals(string(root.get("schema_version"), "snapshot.schema_version"), SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA, "snapshot.schema_version");
            String repository = nonBlank(root.get("repository"), "snapshot.repository");
            Instant capturedAt = parseInstant(nonBlank(root.get("captured_at"), "snapshot.captured_at"), "snapshot.captured_at");
            List<Object> values = requiredList(root.get("pull_requests"), "snapshot.pull_requests");
            if (values.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("snapshot.pull_requests must not be empty");
            List<PullRequest> prs = new ArrayList<>();
            Set<Long> ids = new LinkedHashSet<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < values.size(); i++) {
                PullRequest pr = PullRequest.parse(values.get(i), "snapshot.pull_requests[" + i + "]");
                if (!ids.add(pr.number())) throw new IllegalArgumentException("snapshot has duplicate PR number " + pr.number());
                prs.add(pr);
            }
            return new Snapshot(repository, capturedAt, List.copyOf(prs), raw);
        }
    }

    record PullRequest(long number, String title, String body, Instant createdAt, Instant updatedAt, Boolean draft,
                       List<String> labels, Author author, Checks checks, Review review, Branch branch, Issue issue,
                       Ci ci, FilesFact files) {
        static PullRequest parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = requiredMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, PR_KEYS, path);
            long number = positiveLong(m.get("number"), path + ".number", false);
            String title = stringOrEmpty(m.get("title"), path + ".title");
            String body = stringOrEmpty(m.get("body"), path + ".body");
            Instant created = optionalInstant(m.get("created_at"), path + ".created_at");
            Instant updated = optionalInstant(m.get("updated_at"), path + ".updated_at");
            return new PullRequest(number, title, body, created, updated, optionalBoolean(m.get("draft"), path + ".draft"),
                    optionalStrings(m.get("labels"), path + ".labels"), Author.parse(m.get("author"), path + ".author"),
                    Checks.parse(m.get("checks"), path + ".checks"), Review.parse(m.get("review"), path + ".review"),
                    Branch.parse(m.get("branch"), path + ".branch"), Issue.parse(m.get("issue"), path + ".issue"),
                    Ci.parse(m.get("ci"), path + ".ci"), FilesFact.parse(m.get("files"), path + ".files"));
        }
    }

    record Author(String login, String association, Long priorMergedPrs, Long openPrs, Long failingOpenPrs, Instant lastActivityAt) {
        static Author parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = optionalMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, AUTHOR_KEYS, path);
            String association = optionalString(m.get("association"), path + ".association");
            return new Author(optionalString(m.get("login"), path + ".login"),
                    association == null ? null : association.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("prior_merged_prs"), path + ".prior_merged_prs"),
                    optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("open_prs"), path + ".open_prs"), optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("failing_open_prs"), path + ".failing_open_prs"),
                    optionalInstant(m.get("last_activity_at"), path + ".last_activity_at"));
        }
    }

    record Checks(Long requiredFailed, Long requiredMissing, Boolean conflicts) {
        static Checks parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = optionalMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, CHECK_KEYS, path);
            return new Checks(optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("required_failed"), path + ".required_failed"),
                    optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("required_missing"), path + ".required_missing"),
                    optionalBoolean(m.get("conflicts"), path + ".conflicts"));
        }
    }

    record Review(Long unresolvedThreads, Boolean maintainerEngaged) {
        static Review parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = optionalMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, REVIEW_KEYS, path);
            return new Review(optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("unresolved_threads"), path + ".unresolved_threads"),
                    optionalBoolean(m.get("maintainer_engaged"), path + ".maintainer_engaged"));
        }
    }

    record Branch(Long commitsBehindBase) {
        static Branch parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = optionalMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, BRANCH_KEYS, path);
            return new Branch(optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("commits_behind_base"), path + ".commits_behind_base"));
        }
    }

    record Issue(Boolean linked, Boolean accepted) {
        static Issue parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = optionalMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, ISSUE_KEYS, path);
            return new Issue(optionalBoolean(m.get("linked"), path + ".linked"), optionalBoolean(m.get("accepted"), path + ".accepted"));
        }
    }

    record Ci(Boolean definitionTouched) {
        static Ci parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = optionalMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, CI_KEYS, path);
            return new Ci(optionalBoolean(m.get("definition_touched"), path + ".definition_touched"));
        }
    }

    record FilesFact(Long changed, Long linesChanged) {
        static FilesFact parse(Object raw, String path) {
            Map<String, Object> m = optionalMap(raw, path);
            rejectUnknown(m, FILE_KEYS, path);
            return new FilesFact(optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("changed"), path + ".changed"),
                    optionalNonNegativeLong(m.get("lines_changed"), path + ".lines_changed"));
        }
    }

    record Policy(String name, String readyLabel, String needsPermissionLabel, String ciSafetyLabel,
                  Set<String> externalAssociations, long newcomerPriorMergedPrs, long newcomerLargeChangeLines,
                  long maxOpenPrs, long maxFailingOpenPrs, long staleDraftDays, long behindBaseCommits,
                  Set<String> enabledRules, Object original) {
        static Policy parse(Object raw) {
            Map<String, Object> root = requiredMap(raw, "policy");
            rejectUnknown(root, POLICY_KEYS, "policy");
            requireEquals(string(root.get("schema_version"), "policy.schema_version"), POLICY_SCHEMA, "policy.schema_version");
            String name = nonBlank(root.get("name"), "policy.name");
            Map<String, Object> labels = optionalMap(root.get("labels"), "policy.labels");
            rejectUnknown(labels, LABEL_KEYS, "policy.labels");
            Map<String, Object> thresholds = requiredMap(root.get("thresholds"), "policy.thresholds");
            rejectUnknown(thresholds, THRESHOLD_KEYS, "policy.thresholds");
            Set<String> associations = new LinkedHashSet<>();
            for (String association : requiredStrings(thresholds.get("external_associations"), "policy.thresholds.external_associations")) {
                associations.add(association.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
            }
            List<Object> ruleValues = requiredList(root.get("rules"), "policy.rules");
            Set<String> enabled = new LinkedHashSet<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < ruleValues.size(); i++) {
                Map<String, Object> rule = requiredMap(ruleValues.get(i), "policy.rules[" + i + "]");
                rejectUnknown(rule, RULE_KEYS, "policy.rules[" + i + "]");
                String id = nonBlank(rule.get("id"), "policy.rules[" + i + "].id");
                if (!RULE_IDS.contains(id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("policy.rules[" + i + "]: unknown rule '" + id + "'");
                Boolean active = optionalBoolean(rule.get("enabled"), "policy.rules[" + i + "].enabled");
                if (active == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("policy.rules[" + i + "].enabled is required");
                if (active && !enabled.add(id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("policy enables rule more than once: " + id);
            }
            return new Policy(name, label(labels, "ready", "ready-for-maintainer-review"),
                    label(labels, "needs_permission", "maintainer-permission-needed"),
                    label(labels, "ci_safety", "maintainer-ci-approval-needed"), Set.copyOf(associations),
                    positiveLong(thresholds.get("newcomer_prior_merged_prs"), "policy.thresholds.newcomer_prior_merged_prs", true),
                    positiveLong(thresholds.get("newcomer_large_change_lines"), "policy.thresholds.newcomer_large_change_lines", false),
                    positiveLong(thresholds.get("max_open_prs"), "policy.thresholds.max_open_prs", false),
                    positiveLong(thresholds.get("max_failing_open_prs"), "policy.thresholds.max_failing_open_prs", false),
                    positiveLong(thresholds.get("stale_draft_days"), "policy.thresholds.stale_draft_days", false),
                    positiveLong(thresholds.get("behind_base_commits"), "policy.thresholds.behind_base_commits", false), Set.copyOf(enabled), raw);
        }

        private static String label(Map<String, Object> labels, String key, String defaultValue) {
            String value = optionalString(labels.get(key), "policy.labels." + key);
            return value == null ? defaultValue : value;
        }
    }

    record Proposal(String ruleId, String action, int priority, Map<String, Object> evidence, String message) {
        Map<String, Object> toMap() {
            return Map.of("rule_id", ruleId, "action", action, "priority", priority, "evidence", evidence, "message", message);
        }
    }

    record Decision(long prNumber, Map<String, Object> facts, List<Proposal> proposals, List<String> skippedRules) {
        Map<String, Object> toMap() {
            return Map.of("number", prNumber, "facts", facts, "proposals", proposals.stream().map(Proposal::toMap).toList(),
                    "skipped_rules", skippedRules);
        }
    }

    record Result(Snapshot snapshot, Policy policy, Instant asOf, List<Decision> decisions) {
        int proposalCount() {
            return decisions.stream().mapToInt(d -> d.proposals().size()).sum();
        }

        Map<String, Object> toMap() {
            Map<String, Long> actions = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            Map<String, Long> rules = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            for (Decision d : decisions) {
                for (Proposal p : d.proposals()) {
                    actions.merge(p.action(), 1L, Long::sum);
                    rules.merge(p.ruleId(), 1L, Long::sum);
                }
            }
            Map<String, Object> source = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            source.put("repository", snapshot.repository());
            source.put("captured_at", snapshot.capturedAt().toString());
            source.put("input_digest", digest(snapshot.original()));
            source.put("policy_name", policy.name());
            source.put("policy_digest", digest(policy.original()));
            source.put("as_of", asOf.toString());
            return Map.of("schema_version", RESULT_SCHEMA, "mode", "dry-run", "side_effects", List.of(), "source", source,
                    "summary", Map.of("pull_requests", decisions.size(), "proposals", proposalCount(),
                            "actions", actions, "rules", rules), "decisions", decisions.stream().map(Decision::toMap).toList());
        }

        String markdown() {
            StringBuilder md = new StringBuilder("# AIV Triage Replay\n\n");
            md.append("**Mode:** dry-run. No GitHub API call, comment, label, draft, close, assignment or CI rerun was attempted.\n\n");
            md.append("| Field | Value |\n|---|---|\n");
            md.append("| Repository | `").append(snapshot.repository()).append("` |\n");
            md.append("| Captured at | `").append(snapshot.capturedAt()).append("` |\n");
            md.append("| Evaluated as of | `").append(asOf).append("` |\n");
            md.append("| Policy | `").append(policy.name()).append("` |\n");
            md.append("| Pull requests | ").append(decisions.size()).append(" |\n");
            md.append("| Proposed actions | ").append(proposalCount()).append(" |\n\n");
            md.append("## Proposed actions\n\n");
            boolean any = false;
            for (Decision d : decisions) {
                if (d.proposals().isEmpty()) continue;
                any = true;
                md.append("### PR #").append(d.prNumber()).append("\n\n");
                for (Proposal p : d.proposals()) {
                    md.append("- **").append(p.action()).append("** from `").append(p.ruleId()).append("`: ")
                            .append(p.message()).append(" Evidence: `").append(JsonLite.write(p.evidence())).append("`.\n");
                }
                if (!d.skippedRules().isEmpty()) {
                    md.append("- Unavailable: `").append(String.join("; ", d.skippedRules())).append("`.\n");
                }
                md.append('\n');
            }
            if (!any) md.append("No action was proposed.\n\n");
            md.append("## Unavailable facts\n\n");
            boolean skipped = false;
            for (Decision d : decisions) {
                if (!d.skippedRules().isEmpty()) {
                    skipped = true;
                    md.append("- PR #").append(d.prNumber()).append(": `")
                            .append(String.join("; ", d.skippedRules())).append("`\n");
                }
            }
            if (!skipped) md.append("None.\n");
            return md.toString();
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private static Map<String, Object> requiredMap(Object raw, String path) {
        if (!(raw instanceof Map<?, ?> map)) throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must be an object");
        Map<String, Object> out = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        map.forEach((key, value) -> out.put(String.valueOf(key), value));
        return out;
    }

    private static Map<String, Object> optionalMap(Object raw, String path) {
        return raw == null ? Map.of() : requiredMap(raw, path);
    }

    private static List<Object> requiredList(Object raw, String path) {
        if (!(raw instanceof List<?> list)) throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must be an array");
        return new ArrayList<>(list);
    }

    private static List<String> requiredStrings(Object raw, String path) {
        List<Object> values = requiredList(raw, path);
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Object value : values) out.add(nonBlank(value, path + "[]"));
        if (out.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must not be empty");
        return out;
    }

    private static List<String> optionalStrings(Object raw, String path) {
        if (raw == null) return List.of();
        List<Object> values = requiredList(raw, path);
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Object value : values) out.add(nonBlank(value, path + "[]"));
        return List.copyOf(out);
    }

    private static void rejectUnknown(Map<String, Object> map, Set<String> allowed, String path) {
        for (String key : map.keySet()) if (!allowed.contains(key)) throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + ": unknown key '" + key + "'");
    }

    private static void requireEquals(String actual, String expected, String path) {
        if (!expected.equals(actual)) throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must equal '" + expected + "'");
    }

    private static String string(Object raw, String path) {
        if (!(raw instanceof String value)) throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must be a string");
        return value;
    }

    private static String stringOrEmpty(Object raw, String path) {
        return raw == null ? "" : string(raw, path);
    }

    private static String nonBlank(Object raw, String path) {
        String value = string(raw, path).trim();
        if (value.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must not be blank");
        return value;
    }

    private static String optionalString(Object raw, String path) {
        return raw == null ? null : nonBlank(raw, path);
    }

    private static Boolean optionalBoolean(Object raw, String path) {
        if (raw == null) return null;
        if (raw instanceof Boolean value) return value;
        throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must be a boolean");
    }

    private static Long optionalNonNegativeLong(Object raw, String path) {
        return raw == null ? null : positiveLong(raw, path, true);
    }

    private static long positiveLong(Object raw, String path, boolean zeroAllowed) {
        if (!(raw instanceof Number value) || value.longValue() != value.doubleValue()
                || value.longValue() < (zeroAllowed ? 0 : 1)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(path + " must be a " + (zeroAllowed ? "non-negative" : "positive") + " integer");
        }
        return value.longValue();
    }

    private static Instant optionalInstant(Object raw, String path) {
        return raw == null ? null : parseInstant(string(raw, path), path);
    }
}
```

package io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.cli;

import io.github.vaquarkhan.aiv.util.JsonLite;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/** @author Viquar Khan */
class TriageCommandTest {

    @Test
    void replayProducesEveryProposalAndNoSideEffects(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        List<Map<String, Object>> prs = new ArrayList<>();
        prs.add(pr(1, Map.of("failing_open_prs", 4)));
        prs.add(pr(2, Map.of("open_prs", 6)));
        prs.add(pr(3, Map.of("draft", true, "last_activity_at", "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z")));
        prs.add(pr(4, Map.of("required_failed", 1)));
        prs.add(pr(5, Map.of("conflicts", true)));
        prs.add(pr(6, Map.of("unresolved_threads", 2)));
        prs.add(pr(7, Map.of("definition_touched", true)));
        prs.add(pr(8, Map.of("prior_merged_prs", 0, "lines_changed", 600, "accepted", false)));
        prs.add(pr(9, Map.of()));
        prs.add(pr(10, Map.of("association", "MEMBER")));
        Path input = write(dir.resolve("snapshot.json"), snapshot(prs));
        Path policy = write(dir.resolve("policy.yaml"), policy(allRules()));
        Path json = dir.resolve("out/result.json");
        Path markdown = dir.resolve("out/result.md");

        assertEquals(0, Main.run(new String[]{"triage", "replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(),
                "--output-json", json.toString(), "--output-markdown", markdown.toString()}));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> result = (Map<String, Object>) JsonLite.parse(Files.readString(json));
        assertEquals(TriageCommand.RESULT_SCHEMA, result.get("schema_version"));
        assertEquals("dry-run", result.get("mode"));
        assertEquals(List.of(), result.get("side_effects"));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> summary = (Map<String, Object>) result.get("summary");
        assertEquals(15L, summary.get("proposals"));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        List<Object> decisions = (List<Object>) result.get("decisions");
        assertEquals(10, decisions.size());
        assertAction(decisions, 1, "close-pr", "close-author-quality-violations");
        assertAction(decisions, 2, "hold-open-pr-limit", "hold-open-pr-limit");
        assertAction(decisions, 3, "close-pr", "close-stale-draft");
        assertAction(decisions, 4, "draft-pr", "draft-required-ci-failure");
        assertAction(decisions, 5, "request-rebase", "rebase-conflicted-or-stale-branch");
        assertAction(decisions, 6, "hold-review", "hold-unresolved-review");
        assertAction(decisions, 7, "require-maintainer-ci-approval", "hold-external-ci-definition-change");
        assertAction(decisions, 8, "require-maintainer-permission", "hold-large-newcomer-without-accepted-issue");
        assertAction(decisions, 9, "mark-ready", "ready-for-maintainer-review");
        assertNoActions(decisions, 10);
        String report = Files.readString(markdown);
        assertTrue(report.contains("No GitHub API call, comment, label, draft, close, assignment or CI rerun was attempted."));
        assertTrue(report.contains("PR #9"));
        assertFalse(report.contains("PR #10\n\n- **"));
    }

    @Test
    void unknownFieldsSkipRulesRatherThanGuessing(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        Map<String, Object> incomplete = pr(12, Map.of("remove", List.of("checks", "review", "branch", "ci", "draft")));
        Path input = write(dir.resolve("input.json"), snapshot(List.of(incomplete)));
        Path policy = write(dir.resolve("policy.yaml"), policy(allRules()));
        Path json = dir.resolve("result.json");

        assertEquals(0, Main.run(new String[]{"triage", "replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(),
                "--output-json", json.toString(), "--output-markdown", md.toString()}));

        assertTrue(text.contains("Unavailable facts"));

        assertTrue(text.contains("ready-for-maintainer-review"));

    @Test
    void asOfControlsStaleDraftDeterministically(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        Map<String, Object> stale = pr(13, Map.of("draft", true, "last_activity_at", "2026-10-20T00:00:00Z"));
        Path input = write(dir.resolve("input.json"), snapshot(List.of(stale)));
        Path policy = write(dir.resolve("policy.yaml"), policy(List.of("close-stale-draft")));
        Path jsonA = dir.resolve("a.json");
        Path mdA = dir.resolve("a.md");
        assertEquals(0, Main.run(new String[]{"triage", "replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(),
                "--output-json", jsonA.toString(), "--output-markdown", mdA.toString(), "--as-of", "2026-10-26T00:00:00Z"}));
        assertFalse(Files.readString(mdA).contains("close-pr"));
        Path jsonB = dir.resolve("b.json");
        Path mdB = dir.resolve("b.md");
        assertEquals(0, Main.run(new String[]{"triage", "replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(),
                "--output-json", jsonB.toString(), "--output-markdown", mdB.toString(), "--as-of", "2026-10-28T00:00:00Z"}));
        assertTrue(Files.readString(mdB).contains("close-pr"));

    }

    @Test

                "--output-markdown", oneMd.toString(), "--as-of", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z"};
        String[] second = {"triage", "replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(), "--output-json", two.toString(),
                "--output-markdown", twoMd.toString(), "--as-of", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z"};

    @Test
    void argsAndSchemaAreStrict(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        Path input = write(dir.resolve("input.json"), snapshot(List.of(pr(15, Map.of()))));
        Path policy = write(dir.resolve("policy.yaml"), policy(List.of("ready-for-maintainer-review")));
        Path json = dir.resolve("x.json");
        Path md = dir.resolve("x.md");
        List<String[]> badArgs = List.of(new String[]{}, new String[]{"nope"}, new String[]{"replay"},
                new String[]{"replay", "--input", input.toString()},
                new String[]{"replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(),
                        "--output-json", json.toString(), "--output-markdown", md.toString()},
                new String[]{"replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(), "--output-json", json.toString(),
                        "--output-markdown", md.toString(), "--publish-github-checks"},
                new String[]{"replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(), "--output-json", json.toString(),
                        "--output-markdown", md.toString(), "--as-of", "yesterday"});
        for (String[] args : badArgs) assertEquals(2, TriageCommand.run(args), String.join(" ", args));

        assertEquals(2, runMalformed(dir, "{\"schema_version\":\"bad\",\"repository\":\"x\",\"captured_at\":\"2026-10-24T00:00:00Z\",\"pull_requests\":[]}", policy));
        assertEquals(2, runMalformed(dir, "{\"schema_version\":\"aiv-triage-snapshot/1\",\"repository\":\"x\",\"captured_at\":\"bad\",\"pull_requests\":[]}", policy));
        assertEquals(2, runMalformed(dir, "{\"schema_version\":\"aiv-triage-snapshot/1\",\"repository\":\"x\",\"captured_at\":\"2026-10-24T00:00:00Z\",\"unknown\":true,\"pull_requests\":[{\"number\":1}]}", policy));
        Path invalidPolicy = write(dir.resolve("invalid.yaml"), "schema_version: aiv-triage-policy/1\nname: x\nthresholds: {}\nrules: []\n");
        assertEquals(2, Main.run(command(input, invalidPolicy, json, md)));
    }

    @Test
    void directParsersRejectBadNestedTypesAndRuleDefinitions() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Snapshot.parse(Map.of()));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Snapshot.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA,
                "repository", "x", "captured_at", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z", "pull_requests", List.of(Map.of("number", 0)))));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Policy.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.POLICY_SCHEMA,
                "name", "x", "thresholds", Map.of("external_associations", List.of("NONE"), "newcomer_prior_merged_prs", 0,
                        "newcomer_large_change_lines", 1, "max_open_prs", 1, "max_failing_open_prs", 1, "stale_draft_days", 1,
                        "behind_base_commits", 1), "rules", List.of(Map.of("id", "not-a-rule", "enabled", true)))));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Policy.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.POLICY_SCHEMA,
                "name", "x", "thresholds", Map.of("external_associations", "NONE"), "rules", List.of())));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Args.parse(new String[]{"replay", "--input", "x", "--policy", "y",
                "--output-json", "z", "--output-markdown", "m", "--as-of", ""}));
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private static void assertAction(List<Object> decisions, long number, String action, String rule) {
        Map<String, Object> d = decision(decisions, number);
        List<Object> proposals = (List<Object>) d.get("proposals");
        assertTrue(proposals.stream().map(v -> (Map<String, Object>) v)
                .anyMatch(p -> action.equals(p.get("action")) && rule.equals(p.get("rule_id"))), number + " " + proposals);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private static void assertNoActions(List<Object> decisions, long number) {
        assertEquals(List.of(), decision(decisions, number).get("proposals"));
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private static Map<String, Object> decision(List<Object> decisions, long number) {
        return decisions.stream().map(v -> (Map<String, Object>) v).filter(d -> ((Number) d.get("number")).longValue() == number)
                .findFirst().orElseThrow();
    }

    private static int runMalformed(Path dir, String snapshot, Path policy) throws Exception {
        Path input = write(dir.resolve("malformed-" + Math.abs(snapshot.hashCode()) + ".json"), snapshot);
        return Main.run(command(input, policy, dir.resolve("out.json"), dir.resolve("out.md")));
    }

    private static String[] command(Path input, Path policy, Path json, Path md) {
        return new String[]{"triage", "replay", "--input", input.toString(), "--policy", policy.toString(),
                "--output-json", json.toString(), "--output-markdown", md.toString()};
    }

    private static Path write(Path path, String value) throws Exception {
        Files.writeString(path, value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        return path;
    }

    private static String snapshot(List<Map<String, Object>> prs) {
        return JsonLite.write(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA, "repository", "example/project",
                "captured_at", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z", "pull_requests", prs));
    }

    private static String policy(List<String> rules) {
        StringBuilder y = new StringBuilder("schema_version: aiv-triage-policy/1\nname: test-policy\nlabels:\n"
                + "  ready: ready\n  needs_permission: permission\n  ci_safety: ci-safety\nthresholds:\n"
                + "  external_associations: [CONTRIBUTOR, FIRST_TIME_CONTRIBUTOR, FIRST_TIMER, NONE]\n"
                + "  newcomer_prior_merged_prs: 0\n  newcomer_large_change_lines: 500\n  max_open_prs: 5\n"
                + "  max_failing_open_prs: 3\n  stale_draft_days: 7\n  behind_base_commits: 100\nrules:\n");
        for (String rule : rules) y.append("  - id: ").append(rule).append("\n    enabled: true\n");
        return y.toString();
    }

    private static List<String> allRules() {
        return List.of("close-author-quality-violations", "hold-open-pr-limit", "close-stale-draft", "draft-required-ci-failure",
                "rebase-conflicted-or-stale-branch", "hold-unresolved-review", "hold-external-ci-definition-change",
                "hold-large-newcomer-without-accepted-issue", "ready-for-maintainer-review");
    }

    private static Map<String, Object> pr(long number, Map<String, Object> changes) {
        Map<String, Object> author = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("login", "new-user", "association", "CONTRIBUTOR",
                "prior_merged_prs", 0, "open_prs", 1, "failing_open_prs", 0, "last_activity_at", "2026-10-23T00:00:00Z"));
        Map<String, Object> checks = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("required_failed", 0, "required_missing", 0, "conflicts", false));
        Map<String, Object> review = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("unresolved_threads", 0, "maintainer_engaged", false));
        Map<String, Object> branch = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("commits_behind_base", 0));
        Map<String, Object> issue = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("linked", true, "accepted", true));
        Map<String, Object> ci = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("definition_touched", false));
        Map<String, Object> files = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("changed", 2, "lines_changed", 80));
        Map<String, Object> pr = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.ofEntries(
                Map.entry("number", number), Map.entry("title", "Fix test"), Map.entry("body", "Fixes #1."),
                Map.entry("created_at", "2026-10-23T00:00:00Z"), Map.entry("updated_at", "2026-10-23T00:00:00Z"),
                Map.entry("draft", false), Map.entry("labels", List.of()), Map.entry("author", author),
                Map.entry("checks", checks), Map.entry("review", review), Map.entry("branch", branch),
                Map.entry("issue", issue), Map.entry("ci", ci), Map.entry("files", files)));
        for (Map.Entry<String, Object> e : changes.entrySet()) {
            switch (e.getKey()) {
                case "failing_open_prs", "prior_merged_prs", "open_prs", "association", "last_activity_at" -> author.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "required_failed", "required_missing", "conflicts" -> checks.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "unresolved_threads", "maintainer_engaged" -> review.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "commits_behind_base" -> branch.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "linked", "accepted" -> issue.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "definition_touched" -> ci.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "changed", "lines_changed" -> files.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "draft", "labels" -> pr.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                case "remove" -> ((List<?>) e.getValue()).forEach(k -> pr.remove(String.valueOf(k)));
                default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("test change key " + e.getKey());
            }
        }
        return pr;
    }

    @Test
    void missingFieldsAndBranchVariantsAreEvidenceNotDefaults(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        Map<String, Object> missingAuthor = pr(20, Map.of("failing_open_prs", 0, "open_prs", 6));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> author = (Map<String, Object>) missingAuthor.get("author");
        author.remove("failing_open_prs");
        Map<String, Object> staleMissingActivity = pr(21, Map.of("draft", true));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> staleAuthor = (Map<String, Object>) staleMissingActivity.get("author");
        staleAuthor.remove("last_activity_at");
        Map<String, Object> missingRequired = pr(22, Map.of("required_failed", 0));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> checks = (Map<String, Object>) missingRequired.get("checks");
        checks.remove("required_missing");
        Map<String, Object> behind = pr(23, Map.of("commits_behind_base", 101));
        Map<String, Object> missingNewcomer = pr(24, Map.of("prior_merged_prs", 0, "lines_changed", 600, "accepted", false));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> newAuthor = (Map<String, Object>) missingNewcomer.get("author");
        newAuthor.remove("prior_merged_prs");
        Map<String, Object> noAssociation = pr(25, Map.of("association", "NONE"));
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> noAssocAuthor = (Map<String, Object>) noAssociation.get("author");
        noAssocAuthor.remove("association");
        noAssociation.remove("labels");
        Path input = write(dir.resolve("missing.json"), snapshot(List.of(missingAuthor, staleMissingActivity, missingRequired, behind,
                missingNewcomer, noAssociation)));
        Path policy = write(dir.resolve("policy.yaml"), policy(allRules()));
        Path json = dir.resolve("out.json");
        Path md = dir.resolve("out.md");
        assertEquals(0, Main.run(command(input, policy, json, md)));
        String report = Files.readString(md);
        assertTrue(report.contains("author.failing_open_prs"));
        assertTrue(report.contains("author.last_activity_at"));
        assertTrue(report.contains("checks.required_missing"));
        assertTrue(report.contains("author.prior_merged_prs"));
        assertTrue(report.contains("request-rebase"));
        assertTrue(report.contains("author.association"));
    }

    @Test
    void parserAndIoFailuresAreRejected(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        Path policy = write(dir.resolve("policy.yaml"), policy(List.of("ready-for-maintainer-review")));
        Path json = dir.resolve("x.json");
        Path md = dir.resolve("x.md");

        assertEquals(2, Main.run(command(input, listPolicy, json, md)));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Snapshot.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA,
                "repository", "x", "captured_at", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z", "pull_requests", List.of(pr(1, Map.of()), pr(1, Map.of())))));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Snapshot.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA,
                "repository", "x", "captured_at", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z", "pull_requests", List.of())));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Snapshot.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA,
                "repository", "x", "captured_at", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z", "pull_requests", List.of("not-map"))));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Snapshot.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA,
                "repository", "x", "captured_at", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z", "pull_requests", List.of(pr(27, Map.of("draft", "no"))))));
        Map<String, Object> labelsBlank = pr(28, Map.of("labels", List.of(" ")));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Snapshot.parse(Map.of("schema_version", TriageCommand.SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA,
                "repository", "x", "captured_at", "2026-10-24T00:00:00Z", "pull_requests", List.of(labelsBlank))));
    }

    @Test
    void policyParserCoversLabelsDisabledRulesAndStrictValues() {
        Map<String, Object> thresholds = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        thresholds.put("external_associations", List.of("none"));
        thresholds.put("newcomer_prior_merged_prs", 0);
        thresholds.put("newcomer_large_change_lines", 1);
        thresholds.put("max_open_prs", 1);
        thresholds.put("max_failing_open_prs", 1);
        thresholds.put("stale_draft_days", 1);
        thresholds.put("behind_base_commits", 1);
        Map<String, Object> base = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        base.put("schema_version", TriageCommand.POLICY_SCHEMA);
        base.put("name", "x");
        base.put("thresholds", thresholds);
        base.put("rules", List.of(Map.of("id", "ready-for-maintainer-review", "enabled", false)));
        TriageCommand.Policy p = TriageCommand.Policy.parse(base);
        assertEquals("ready-for-maintainer-review", p.readyLabel());
        assertTrue(p.enabledRules().isEmpty());
        base.put("labels", Map.of("ready", "custom-ready", "needs_permission", "permission", "ci_safety", "ci"));
        p = TriageCommand.Policy.parse(base);
        assertEquals("custom-ready", p.readyLabel());
        Map<String, Object> duplicate = new LinkedHashMap<>(base);
        duplicate.put("rules", List.of(Map.of("id", "ready-for-maintainer-review", "enabled", true),
                Map.of("id", "ready-for-maintainer-review", "enabled", true)));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Policy.parse(duplicate));
        Map<String, Object> missingEnabled = new LinkedHashMap<>(base);
        missingEnabled.put("rules", List.of(Map.of("id", "ready-for-maintainer-review")));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Policy.parse(missingEnabled));
        thresholds.put("max_open_prs", -1);
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Policy.parse(base));
        thresholds.put("max_open_prs", "one");
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Policy.parse(base));
        thresholds.put("max_open_prs", 1);
        thresholds.put("external_associations", List.of());
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Policy.parse(base));
    }

    @Test
    void parserCoversOptionalAndMissingFacts(@TempDir Path dir) throws Exception {
        Map<String, Object> missingOpen = pr(29, Map.of());
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> missingOpenAuthor = (Map<String, Object>) missingOpen.get("author");
        missingOpenAuthor.remove("open_prs");
        Map<String, Object> noTitles = pr(30, Map.of());
        noTitles.remove("title");
        noTitles.remove("body");
        noTitles.remove("labels");
        Path input = write(dir.resolve("mixed.json"), snapshot(List.of(missingOpen, noTitles)));
        Path policy = write(dir.resolve("policy.yaml"), policy(List.of("hold-open-pr-limit", "ready-for-maintainer-review")));
        Path json = dir.resolve("mixed.json.out");
        Path md = dir.resolve("mixed.md");
        assertEquals(0, Main.run(command(input, policy, json, md)));
        assertTrue(Files.readString(md).contains("author.open_prs"));
        String digest = TriageCommand.digestForAlgorithm("SHA-256", "x");
        assertTrue(digest.startsWith("sha256:"));
        assertEquals(71, digest.length());
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> TriageCommand.digestForAlgorithm("no-such-digest", "x"));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TriageCommand.Args.parse(new String[]{"replay", "--input", "x", "--policy", "y",
                "--output-json", "z", "--output-markdown", "m", "--as-of", "a", "--as-of", "b"}));
    }
}
```

# AIV Triage starter policy. Replay it against saved PR history before a project ever enables live actions.
schema_version: aiv-triage-policy/1
name: oss-maintainer
labels:
  ready: ready-for-maintainer-review
  needs_permission: maintainer-permission-needed
  ci_safety: maintainer-ci-approval-needed
thresholds:
  external_associations: [CONTRIBUTOR, FIRST_TIME_CONTRIBUTOR, FIRST_TIMER, NONE]
  newcomer_prior_merged_prs: 0
  newcomer_large_change_lines: 500
  max_open_prs: 5
  max_failing_open_prs: 3
  stale_draft_days: 7
  behind_base_commits: 100
rules:
  - { id: close-author-quality-violations, enabled: true }
  - { id: hold-open-pr-limit, enabled: true }
  - { id: close-stale-draft, enabled: true }
  - { id: draft-required-ci-failure, enabled: true }
  - { id: rebase-conflicted-or-stale-branch, enabled: true }
  - { id: hold-unresolved-review, enabled: true }
  - { id: hold-external-ci-definition-change, enabled: true }
  - { id: hold-large-newcomer-without-accepted-issue, enabled: true }
  - { id: ready-for-maintainer-review, enabled: true }
